* The `all_...` union tables over the system log tables (`system.all_query_log`, `system.all_text_log`, and so on) are now created by default: the default server configuration enables the `create_union_system_log_tables` section with `merge_rotated_tables`, so queries over the rotated versions of a log no longer have to remember constructs like `merge(system, '^query_log')`. The `cluster` setting is shown as a commented-out example, because the table definitions would reference the cluster and require it to exist. [#117943](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117943) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* In a `DataLakeCatalog` database with `allow_experimental_iceberg_compaction = 1`, only `Iceberg` tables are now cached. `DeltaLake` and `Paimon` tables no longer keep the first schema they saw, so `SELECT *` and `DESCRIBE` pick up added columns without recreating the database or restarting the server. Closes [#122548](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122548). [#122568](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122568) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The background memory worker now corrects the global memory tracker from real memory usage on every tick (server setting `memory_worker_correct_memory_tracker`, enabled by default). Previously the tracker was a pure counter that was re-baselined only at startup and when it went negative, so upward accounting drift was permanent: servers were observed refusing every query with `MEMORY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`, reporting more than 100 GiB in use while actually using 7 GiB, until restart. The drift itself remains visible as the difference between the `MemoryTrackingUncorrected` and `MemoryTracking` metrics. [#118094](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118094) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The AI function per-query quotas are now disabled by default: `ai_function_max_input_tokens_per_query`, `ai_function_max_output_tokens_per_query`, and `ai_function_max_api_calls_per_query` default to `0`, meaning no limit. To keep the previous caps, set the three settings explicitly or use `compatibility = '26.9'`. [#121424](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121424) ([George Larionov](https://github.com/george-larionov)).
* Named `Tuple` columns in `Pretty` formats are now displayed as subcolumns, with the element names shown in additional header and footer lines. Controlled by the new setting `output_format_pretty_named_tuples_as_subcolumns` (enabled by default). A named tuple rendered inside one cell, for example nested in an `Array` or when the setting is disabled, is still displayed according to `output_format_pretty_named_tuples_as_json`. [#78927](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/78927) ([Engel Danila](https://github.com/aaaengel)).
* Show the query result in `Pretty` formats as soon as it arrives. Previously a query that returns a few rows early and then keeps reading, such as `SELECT DISTINCT` over a large table, displayed nothing until it finished or was cancelled. A later fix prevents a background write error after the query has finished from surfacing as a late `LOGICAL_ERROR`. [#117005](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117005) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)). [#125028](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125028) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Display non-printable control characters as Unicode "Control Pictures" in the `Vertical` and `Pretty*` output formats by default, so they are no longer silently swallowed and no longer deform the table. `TAB`, the line feed, and `ESC` are excluded, because a terminal interprets them rather than swallowing them. The new setting `output_format_pretty_display_control_characters` can be disabled to restore raw-byte output. [#101774](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/101774) ([Yash ](https://github.com/Onyx2406)).
* Expired authentication methods are now removed from a user automatically. Every `ALTER USER` drops the authentication methods whose `VALID UNTIL` deadline has already passed and keeps all the others, so short-lived credentials can be rotated indefinitely with `ALTER USER ... ADD IDENTIFIED WITH ... VALID UNTIL ...` without the expired ones accumulating against `max_authentication_methods_per_user`. A method added by the same statement is not affected, and a user whose every method is expired keeps them rather than being left with no way to authenticate. [#118880](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118880) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* On `ReplicatedMergeTree`, the sync value `3` (`mutations_sync`, `lightweight_deletes_sync`, `alter_sync`) now waits for the active replicas, as documented, instead of all of them, so an inactive replica no longer causes `UNFINISHED`. This also affects `OPTIMIZE`, `TRUNCATE`, `DROP`/`DETACH PARTITION`, `REPLACE PARTITION`, and `MOVE PARTITION TO TABLE`. [#120592](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120592) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* A number written directly as the first argument of `CAST` is now read with the target type when that type reads it more precisely than the number itself carries it, such as a `Decimal` or an integer wider than 64 bits. `Nullable`, `Array`, and `LowCardinality` of those types take the same exact path. [#116043](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116043) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `TTL ... GROUP BY (a, b, c)` is now accepted and means the same as `TTL ... GROUP BY a, b, c`, matching how `ORDER BY (a, b, c)` works. Previously the parenthesized form was rejected with `TTL Expression GROUP BY key should be a prefix of primary key`. [#118426](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118426) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `sequenceMatch`, `sequenceCount`, and `sequenceMatchEvents` now accept a `DateTime64` timestamp instead of failing. Events are ordered at the column's full precision, so two events within the same second are no longer ambiguous. [#118427](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118427) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `argMin` and `argMax` now consistently return the value from the first row among rows with the same minimum or maximum within a block, matching `argMinIf` and `argMaxIf`. [#122892](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122892) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Added the setting `analyzer_compatibility_allow_cte_redefinition`. When enabled, a CTE name may be defined more than once in a single `WITH` clause; a later definition shadows the earlier one as the query analysis before 24.3 did. `MATERIALIZED` CTEs and CTEs in `WITH RECURSIVE` cannot be redefined. [#121623](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121623) ([Dmitry Novik](https://github.com/novikd)).
* Added settings `semi_join_include_columns_from_both_sides` and `anti_join_include_columns_from_both_sides` (enabled by default) for `SEMI` and `ANTI` joins in the analyzer. When enabled, behavior is unchanged: columns from both sides are accessible after the join. Disable either setting to enforce SQL-standard semantics, where only the preserved side's columns are visible after the join. [#96662](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/96662) ([Zhang Yifan](https://github.com/zhangyifan27)).
* The setting `aggregate_function_input_format` with the values `value` and `array` now works in every input format, including `RowBinary`, `Native`, and `Parquet`, and for `AggregateFunction` nested in `Array`, `Tuple`, and `Map`. In the JSON formats the value is now a JSON value, for example `{"x": [1, 2, 3]}`, not a string containing it. [#121120](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121120) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Compression codec names and their keyword arguments are now matched case-insensitively, so `CODEC(zstd)` works like `CODEC(ZSTD)` and `ALP(std)` like `ALP(STD)`. Previously a codec whose name is mixed-case, such as `Delta`, `DoubleDelta`, `Gorilla` or `Quantized`, could not be used in the `default_compression_codec`, `marks_compression_codec` and `primary_key_compression_codec` settings, nor in the `<compression>` section of the server configuration, because those paths upper-cased the codec name. [#119313](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119313) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Added aliases `allow_suspicious_indexes`, `ignore_data_skipping_indexes`, `force_data_skipping_indexes`, and `secondary_indexes_enable_bulk_filtering` for the settings spelled with `indices`, so both plural forms are accepted. [#122565](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122565) ([Mohammed Sohail](https://github.com/msohail22)).
* `APPLY (f, 'prefix')` now keeps the table qualifier in generated column names. [#122702](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122702) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Async insert now supports eligible single-block local `INSERT ... SELECT` queries, with synchronous fallback for larger, multi-block, empty, transactional, or non-parallel-quorum inserts. [#111663](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111663) ([Mikhail f. Shiryaev](https://github.com/Felixoid)).
* Added the opt-in `optimize_row_order_if_no_order_by` setting for `MergeTree` tables with an empty sorting key (`ORDER BY ()` / `ORDER BY tuple()`). When enabled, it applies row-order optimization automatically to inserts into such tables. [#104591](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/104591) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `WHATIF` can now estimate hypothetical projections without reading the whole table: past `max_rows_to_scan` rows it estimates from a sample of granules and reports the resulting range in `marks_span`, `max_rows_to_read` lowers that budget, and it follows `(prefer|force)_optimize_projection`. It now also uses the real projection-optimizer weighing logic, so its verdicts, reasons, and pruned ranges track the optimizer more closely. [#118233](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118233) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)). [#121766](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121766) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)). [#122060](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122060) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)). [#123540](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123540) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)). [#123587](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123587) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Added the setting `parallel_replicas_for_queries_with_multiple_tables` (default `true`) to control whether parallel replicas are used for queries joining multiple tables. When disabled, parallel replicas are not used for queries with `JOIN`, where the non-leftmost side is read in full on every replica. The setting does not affect a `UNION` query without a `JOIN`, where each branch is an independent single-table read, nor `ARRAY JOIN`. [#76595](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/76595) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* `max_joined_block_size_rows` and `max_joined_block_size_bytes` now also bound the result blocks of `parallel_hash` and `grace_hash` joins: squashing of the join output no longer merges blocks past these limits. Closes [#124198](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124198). [#124304](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124304) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Added the setting `query_plan_filter_push_down_below_limit_by` (default `1`), controlling whether filters on `LIMIT BY` key columns are pushed below the `LIMIT BY` step. Setting it to `0` restores the plan shape used before 26.7, for queries where the pushed predicate makes index analysis costlier than the scan it saves. [#121675](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121675) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Added the `use_aggregation_memory_tracker` setting to choose how `GROUP BY` measures memory when deciding on two-level aggregation. `compatibility` below `26.9` restores the measurement used before 26.7. [#124439](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124439) ([Hechem Selmi](https://github.com/m-selmi)).
* `system.query_log` and `system.processes` now report the metrics of each task of a distributed query plan (`make_distributed_plan = 1`) in a row of its own: a task is a secondary query with its own `query_id`, correlated to the statement that started it by `initial_query_id`. The initiator's row reports its own work instead of the volume its tasks read. [#120949](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120949) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `system.query_log` now records the databases, tables, and columns read by a replica that executes a query plan shipped to it, which happens with `parallel_replicas_plan_based`. [#122657](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122657) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Queries with `send_logs_level` now also forward text logs from stateless `make_distributed_plan` workers through the coordinator, so worker-side exception context reaches the client. [#118603](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118603) ([Antonio Filipovic](https://github.com/antoniofilipovic)).
* The setting `parallel_replicas_insert_select_local_pipeline` is obsolete. It was a workaround for a performance problem fixed in 25.5, and it duplicated `parallel_replicas_local_plan`, which already decides whether the initiator reads its own share of a distributed `INSERT SELECT`. Use `parallel_replicas_local_plan = 0` to leave all the reading to the remote replicas. [#121653](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121653) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Distributed `SELECT` queries now emit one OpenTelemetry span per remote shard read, with attributes such as `clickhouse.cluster`, `clickhouse.shard_num`, `clickhouse.processed_stage`, `clickhouse.query_id`, `clickhouse.initial_query_id`, and `clickhouse.target_host`, so slow or failed shards can be identified directly from `system.opentelemetry_span_log`. [#115784](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115784) ([Diego Gomes Tomé](https://github.com/diegomestre2)).
* Reads with parallel replicas are now covered by OpenTelemetry spans: a summary span with the totals of the coordination, one span per announcement and per read request handled by the coordinator, and `clickhouse.replica_num` on the per-replica spans, so the work distribution between replicas can be inspected in `system.opentelemetry_span_log`. [#118570](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118570) ([Diego Gomes Tomé](https://github.com/diegomestre2)).
* Added profile events for network activity: `DNSRequests`, `DNSRequestMicroseconds`, and `DNSRequestError`; `DNSReverseRequests`, `DNSReverseRequestMicroseconds`, and `DNSReverseError`; `TLSHandshakes`, `TLSHandshakeMicroseconds`, and `TLSHandshakeErrors`; `TLSServerHandshakes`, `TLSServerHandshakeMicroseconds`, and `TLSServerHandshakeErrors`; and `HTTPServerConnectionsErrors`. DNS now exposes timing as well as error counts, and TLS handshakes are now observable. [#116136](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116136) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Added the `AsyncInsertFlush` profile event. Compare it with `AsyncInsertQuery` to see how many queries are batched into one asynchronous `INSERT` flush. [#120187](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120187) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Add the `RuntimeFilterLookupsBeforeBuildFinished` profile event, counting blocks that a JOIN runtime filter passed through unfiltered because its build side had not finished yet (for example with `join_overflow_mode = 'break'` and a finite `max_rows_in_join` / `max_bytes_in_join`). [#107108](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107108) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Added `last_error_symbols` and `last_error_lines` to `system.errors` and `system.error_log`. Closes [#74561](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/74561). [#100374](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/100374) ([Narasimha Pakeer](https://github.com/npakeer)).
* `system.processors_profile_log` now has entries for queries that fail with an exception, and new columns `exception_code` and `exception`. [#121503](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121503) ([Aleksandr Efimov](https://github.com/alexandrefimov)).
* `DROP TABLE` and `DROP DATABASE` now write a `RemovePart` event to `system.part_log` for every part of the dropped table, as merges, `DROP PARTITION`, and `TRUNCATE` already do. [#122093](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122093) ([Garrett Thomas](https://github.com/garrettthomaskth)).
* Added the server setting `min_allocation_size_to_log_stack_trace`. When a single charge to the global memory tracker reaches it, the stack trace is written to the server log and to `system.trace_log` with trace type `MemoryLargeAllocation`. This helps attribute a global tracked total that has grown far beyond real memory usage, a state in which the server refuses every allocation while far below its limit. Default `0` keeps it off. [#119527](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119527) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Show throttler and workload IO scheduler wait time in the client progress bar (`ThrottlerSleepMicroseconds`, `SchedulerIOReadWaitMicroseconds` and `SchedulerIOWriteWaitMicroseconds` are summed into the `waited` figure, and the stalled sections of the bar are colored yellow). [#118536](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118536) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* `EXPLAIN ANALYZE time=1` shows more information about time and parallelism of every step. [#114786](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114786) ([Kirill Kopnev](https://github.com/Fgrtue)).
* Peak-memory reporting under `EXPLAIN ANALYZE` now accounts for aggregation correctly. [#123324](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123324) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Removed nine unneeded `ProfileEvents`: the eight never-incremented `DistributedIndexAnalysisParts`, `ParallelReplicasDeniedRequests`, `WasmMemoryAllocated`, `S3QueueSetFileProcessingMicroseconds`, `S3QueueSetFileProcessedMicroseconds`, `S3QueueSetFileFailedMicroseconds`, `FileSegmentRemoveMicroseconds`, and `ExternalDataSourceLocalCacheReadBytes`, plus the duplicate `CachedReadBufferPredownloadedBytes`, which always matched `CachedReadBufferPredownloadedFromSourceBytes`. This reduces per-query memory and bookkeeping overhead; queries and dashboards should use `CachedReadBufferPredownloadedFromSourceBytes`. [#123040](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123040) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)). [#123042](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123042) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Killing a query that is loading skip-index marks from object storage now stops the read promptly: the remote read path checks query cancellation before each buffer fill, instead of reading on for minutes after the query was already cancelled. [#113436](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113436) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reduced cancellation latency for `GROUP BY ... WITH TOTALS` queries: `KILL QUERY` now interrupts totals accumulation within a single chunk instead of waiting for all aggregate states to be merged. [#119470](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119470) ([Roman Vasin](https://github.com/rvasin)).
* `KILL QUERY` and Ctrl+C in `clickhouse-client` now cancel long-running functions inside `WHERE` and `HAVING` filters instead of waiting for the function to finish. [#112705](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112705) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `KILL MUTATION`, `SYSTEM STOP MERGES`, `DETACH`/`DROP` of a table, `REPLACE`/`ATTACH PARTITION ... FROM` and server shutdown now stop a running mutation within a fraction of a second when the mutation's predicate has an `IN (subquery)` whose subquery works without reading new data (for example a cross join). Previously they waited until the whole set was built, and `REPLACE`/`ATTACH PARTITION ... FROM` gave up with `TIMEOUT_EXCEEDED` after 120 seconds. `KILL MUTATION` now also stops a mutation that is already writing its new part. [#124122](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124122) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `KILL QUERY` and `max_execution_time` (with the default `timeout_overflow_mode = 'throw'`) now interrupt writing the `Parquet` format in the middle of a block that is split into many row groups (for example with a small `output_format_parquet_row_group_size`), instead of only after the whole block is written. [#125489](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125489) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `DROP TABLE`, `DETACH TABLE`, server shutdown, and `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY SETTING refresh_statistics_interval` no longer wait for a background refresh of the statistics cache of a `MergeTree` table to read every part. On object storage with thousands of parts this wait could take minutes per table and delay a restart. Closes [#125136](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125136). [#125165](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125165) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `DROP TABLE` and other operations that cancel merges and mutations no longer wait for an in-progress `vector_similarity` index build to finish. [#123062](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123062) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)). [#123739](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123739) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* `KILL QUERY WHERE query_id = '<id>'` now cancels the caller's own query without requiring `SELECT` on `system.processes`. For a caller holding neither that grant nor `KILL QUERY`, an id that is not running as them cancels nothing and returns no rows. Any other `WHERE` condition keeps requiring the privileges it requires today. [#117999](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117999) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Access to `system.jemalloc_profile_text`, `system.jemalloc_sampled_allocations`, `system.jemalloc_bins`, `system.jemalloc_arena_bins`, and `system.jemalloc_stats` now requires `SYSTEM_JEMALLOC`; the heap profiles flushed by `system.jemalloc_profile_text` and `system.jemalloc_sampled_allocations` are also removed after being returned. [#121622](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121622) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)). [#121634](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121634) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Added the `access_control_improvements.readonly_restricts_set_role` server setting, `false` by default. When enabled, `SET ROLE` is refused with `READONLY` in a session or query whose `readonly` setting is not zero. Previously `SET ROLE` was the only identity-changing statement that `readonly` did not refuse. [#118021](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118021) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Secret arguments of table functions, table engines, database engines, backup engines, and dictionary sources are now declared next to each feature and are masked consistently in `SHOW CREATE`, system tables, query logs, and `EXPLAIN AST`, instead of relying on a central hardcoded parser list. [#123364](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123364) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* The `PostgreSQL`, `MaterializedPostgreSQL`, `DataLakeCatalog`, `Cluster`, `Remote`, and `HDFS` database engines key the exemption for the server's replay of its own stored metadata on the metadata loader instead of on the query being internal. A `HDFS` database restored with `RESTORE DATABASE` is now checked against `remote_url_allow_hosts`. [#120280](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120280) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Allow `remote_url_allow_hosts` to match the bucket name of an `S3` URL. [#124453](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124453) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Support proxy credentials in the `http_proxy` and `https_proxy` environment variables. Previously the userinfo part of the proxy URI was discarded, so ClickHouse could not use an authenticating proxy. Proxy credentials are sent only to the proxy, rather than to the target server inside a `CONNECT` tunnel. [#123069](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123069) ([Akanshaak](https://github.com/Akanshaak)). [#124396](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124396) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Interserver authentication failures on a listener that uses the PROXY protocol now record the client address from the PROXY header in `system.session_log` when `auth_use_forwarded_address` is enabled, instead of the proxy address. [#125326](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125326) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Added the `tcp_with_proxy_allow_interserver_mode` server setting, which allows disabling the native protocol's interserver mode on `tcp_with_proxy_port`. [#123188](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123188) ([Pradeep Chhetri](https://github.com/chhetripradeep)).
* Reject unexpected packets in the native protocol before deserializing their payload, avoiding unnecessary processing of data types and aggregate function states before returning `UNEXPECTED_PACKET_FROM_CLIENT`. [#119368](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119368) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Added the setting `kill_throw_if_noop`, enabled by default, which makes local `KILL QUERY` and `KILL MUTATION` statements throw `NO_QUERY_TO_KILL` when their selection produces no eligible target. `ON CLUSTER` execution remains tolerant of empty matches. Set `kill_throw_if_noop = false` to preserve the previous behavior. Related [#83348](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/83348). [#85023](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/85023) ([zwy991114](https://github.com/zwy991114)).
* Merges and queries on `S3` disks no longer hold one HTTP connection per open stream for their whole duration once connections are scarce. Previously this let a few merges of tables with many substreams, for example a `JSON` column, saturate `disk_connections_hard_limit`, stall each other, and fail every other request to the disks with `HTTP_CONNECTION_LIMIT_REACHED`. Once the `Disk` connection group is at or above `disk_connections_soft_limit`, reads from `S3` disks fetch each buffer fill with a separate request and return the connection to the pool between fills; the new profile event `ReadBufferFromS3RequestsCut` counts such requests. [#119730](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119730) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)). [#124701](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124701) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `filesystem_cache_boundary_alignment` setting can now be changed per query. For disk reads, `0` means to use the cache configuration's `boundary_alignment`; for non-disk reads, `0` still disables alignment. [#118791](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118791) ([Kseniia Sumarokova](https://github.com/kssenii)).
* Added the filesystem cache setting `use_real_disk_size` (disabled by default), which accounts for cache reservations in filesystem allocation units instead of raw bytes. It makes `FilesystemCacheSize`, eviction-size metrics, and `current_size` in `system.filesystem_cache_settings` approximate the disk footprint. The accounting is block-aligned rather than exact and can include reserve-ahead bytes of in-flight reads. [#79490](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/79490) ([codeworse](https://github.com/codeworse)).
* Custom HTTP headers configured for S3 are now handled case-insensitively by name, so a header spelled `X-Amz-Meta-Foo` reaches Google Cloud Storage translated to `x-goog-meta-foo`, as `x-amz-meta-foo` already did. [#120014](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120014) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Better error messages for S3 request retries: the HTTP response code is no longer printed as `18446744073709551615` when the request received no response at all, and the misleading message about a possible time skew is no longer printed on every retry. [#118439](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118439) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Allow `DESCRIBE FILESYSTEM CACHE` on the introspection port. [#115894](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115894) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Files on encrypted disks now support positional reads, so a table with a `UNIQUE KEY` on an encrypted disk can be loaded again after a server restart. [#119991](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119991) ([johnjing](https://github.com/JingYanchao)).
* The `url` table function and the `URL` table engine now expand glob patterns lazily instead of materializing every address before the query starts. `glob_expansion_max_elements` now limits how many addresses a query actually generates rather than how large the pattern is, so a query that stops early - for example under a `LIMIT` - can use a pattern that describes far more addresses than the limit. [#115680](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115680) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `Iceberg` manifest writing now uses sequence-number inheritance: `ADDED` entries have `NULL` `sequence_number` and `file_sequence_number` unless compaction assigns an explicit sequence number. [#120031](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120031) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* `Iceberg` manifest statistics are now written for `boolean` and `fixed` columns. [#121548](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121548) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* When a table is created through a catalog, `CREATE TABLE catalog.table ENGINE = IcebergS3` can now omit explicit credentials and reuse the catalog credentials. [#114477](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114477) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... EXECUTE expire_snapshots` now works for `Iceberg` tables in transactional catalogs such as Iceberg REST and Unity. [#123248](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123248) ([alesapin](https://github.com/alesapin)).
* A `DataLakeCatalog` database whose catalog cannot be built no longer reports its tolerated table-listing failure as an error of the query that enumerated it: the skipped database is now logged at `debug` instead of `error`, so a query such as `SELECT ... FROM system.tables` or `system.iceberg_history` no longer sends a server-side exception to a client whose `send_logs_level` includes `error`. Set `show_data_lake_catalogs_in_system_tables = 1` to have the real exception raised instead. [#121475](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121475) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* When a `DeltaLake` write commit fails, ClickHouse now always reports the commit error rather than an error raised while removing the data files the failed `INSERT` had already uploaded, and every removal that fails during that cleanup is logged with the path it was left on. Previously the first failing removal replaced the commit error in the response, so the reason nothing was written was lost and no path was logged. Closes [#122023](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122023). [#122123](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122123) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading an `Iceberg` table whose position delete file has no `pos` or `file_path` column now fails with `ICEBERG_SPECIFICATION_VIOLATION` naming the file and the missing column, instead of a `LOGICAL_ERROR` (missing `pos`) or a misleading `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` (missing `file_path`). [#123583](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123583) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `Paimon` `BOOLEAN` columns are now read as `Bool` instead of `Int8`, matching `Iceberg` and `DeltaLake`. `DESCRIBE` and `toTypeName` now report `Bool`, and text formats render the values as `false` and `true` instead of `0` and `1`. [#119404](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119404) ([JIaQi Tang](https://github.com/JiaQiTang98)).
* Avro files now include per-column size statistics in their metadata. [#121392](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121392) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* The `Parquet` reader now rejects a column chunk whose metadata has a negative `num_values`, `total_uncompressed_size`, or `unencoded_byte_array_data_bytes` with `INCORRECT_DATA` instead of failing later with a misleading memory-allocation error. It also caps memory estimates derived from these fields at the reader's watermarks and ignores pre-allocations too large to ever succeed, so absurdly large values no longer cause an internal error. Closes [#124224](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124224). [#124330](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124330) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Bumped the bundled `ORC` library so that an `ORC` file declaring a compression codec the reader does not implement is rejected with an error naming the codec, instead of an internal error that debug and sanitizer builds treat as a failed assertion. [#115889](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115889) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `INSERT ... VALUES` over a stream that fails mid-row now reports that read failure directly, instead of retrying the failed read while recovering from a parse error. This affects an HTTP request body that is shorter than its declared `Content-Length` and is streamed to the input format. [#121916](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121916) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ClickHouse Keeper no longer drops the client session when the outgoing request queue stays full for longer than `operation_timeout_ms`. The enqueue wait is now three times `session_timeout_ms`, so a short burst of backpressure no longer invalidates the session's ephemeral nodes and watches. [#121559](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121559) ([Kseniia Sumarokova](https://github.com/kssenii)).
* ClickHouse Keeper now reports orphaned nodes in a snapshot as `CORRUPTED_DATA` instead of an opaque internal error. With `keeper_server.remove_orphaned_nodes_on_startup = true` and `keeper_server.digest_enabled = false`, it can also remove those nodes during startup so the snapshot still loads. [#95761](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/95761) ([Seva Potapov](https://github.com/seva-potapov)).
* The Keeper `rcfg` command now honors its `retry` count per action, stops once `max_total_wait_time_ms` is exhausted, and rejects a negative `retry` before running any action. [#122758](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122758) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Updated `NuRaft` to fix handling of abandoned peers when a removed server rejoins. [#120736](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120736) ([Antonio Andelic](https://github.com/antonio2368)).
* Added the setting `get_zookeeper_lock_acquire_timeout_ms` so Keeper-backed queries such as reads from `system.zookeeper`, `zookeeperSessionUptime`, and `SYSTEM RECONNECT ZOOKEEPER` can fail fast while another thread is reconnecting Keeper. [#94836](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/94836) ([c-end](https://github.com/c-end)).
* Activating a replica whose `active` or `is_active` node in `Keeper` is still held by another session is now reported as `REPLICA_ALREADY_EXISTS` instead of `LOGICAL_ERROR`, so it no longer aborts the server in debug and sanitizer builds. The same applies when that node is rewritten while it is being removed. [#114408](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114408) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The ZooKeeper client, the Graphite metrics writer, the Redis source, `system.zookeeper_info`, the distributed query exchange, and host names in `listen_host` and `bind_host` now resolve through the ClickHouse DNS cache, so these lookups are cached, visible in `system.dns_cache` and subject to `dns_allow_resolve_names_to_ipv4` and `dns_allow_resolve_names_to_ipv6`. Previously they resolved names on their own, bypassing the cache. [#120831](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120831) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Added the `http_x_clickhouse_format_overrides_output_format` setting (default `true`). When disabled, the `X-ClickHouse-Format` HTTP header only sets `default_format`, as it did before 26.8, instead of overriding a query's `FORMAT` clause via `output_format`. [#121132](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121132) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The Prometheus HTTP API accepts `RFC3339` times with `Z` or a `+hh:mm` offset and fractional seconds in `time`, `start`, and `end`, as Prometheus does, so clients like vmalert work. String time arguments of `prometheusQuery`, `timeSeriesSelector`, and the `timeSeries*ToGrid` functions accept `RFC3339` too. [#122808](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122808) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* PromQL regex matchers that list plain literals, such as `{job=~"api|web"}`, now use the primary key and return exactly the listed values, as in Prometheus. Before, only the first alternative was anchored at the start and only the last at the end, so such a matcher also returned values like `api-server` or `myweb`. [#122796](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122796) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* PromQL instant queries now return series in a deterministic order: `topk` and `bottomk` at the root are sorted by value, as Prometheus returns them, and other instant queries are sorted by their labels, like range queries, so repeated requests list the series in the same order. [#122347](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122347) ([James Cunningham](https://github.com/JTCunning)). [#122976](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122976) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* `/api/v1/format_query` and PromQL serialization now print durations in Prometheus units, so `sum(rate(x[5m]))` formats as itself rather than as `sum(rate(x[300]))`. [#120939](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120939) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* PromQL now validates label names in `label_join` and `label_replace`. [#118190](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118190) ([Minh Vu](https://github.com/fallintoplace)).
* A Prometheus remote-write or remote-read request whose body cannot be decompressed is now answered with `400` instead of `500`, and the error names the size of the block and the uncompressed length it declares. [#120938](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120938) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* Added the `merge_selector_min_age_to_disable_right_tail_heuristic` `MergeTree` setting. It lets the `Simple` and `StochasticSimple` merge selectors include small parts at the right end of a range in merge candidates once every part in the range is old enough. [#122638](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122638) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Added the `MergeTree` settings `merge_selector_small_parts_min_count`, `merge_selector_small_parts_threshold`, and `merge_selector_small_parts_max_age` to reduce the number of merge operations under rapid small-part insertion by requiring a minimum batch size before merging small fresh parts. [#100436](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/100436) ([Amos Bird](https://github.com/amosbird)).
* Calculate projections using the query analyzer and reject missing projection columns rather than writing incomplete projection parts. Row policies and `additional_table_filters` for `system.one` no longer affect projection calculation. [#118375](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118375) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN ..., MODIFY ORDER BY ...` now rejects a new sorting key that uses a subcolumn of the added `DEFAULT` or `MATERIALIZED` column, and its error message names whether the expression kind is `DEFAULT` or `MATERIALIZED` instead of always saying `default expression`. [#121873](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121873) ([GON](https://github.com/gonappuccino)).
* An unresolved name in a column `DEFAULT`, `MATERIALIZED`, or `ALIAS` expression is now reported by the name the user wrote, instead of an internal `<column>_tmp_alter<random>` alias that appears nowhere in the query and also broke the "Maybe you meant" hint. A sorting, primary, or partition key that contains a nullable column or a constant now names that column or constant. [#116871](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116871) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Web UI: save and load the list of tabs, their queries, and query parameters as a Markdown file. The tab bar is now always shown, and the connection settings always live behind the key button in its corner. [#121234](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121234) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Web UI: added a `Format` button to the query editor, which formats the query with `formatQuery`. [#121652](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121652) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)). [#122477](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122477) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Web UI: clicking a cell of a `JSON` column pretty-prints the value with two-space indentation and syntax highlighting. [#118640](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118640) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The graph view of the Web UI for `EXPLAIN ... graph = 1` now follows the light/dark theme instead of always drawing a white canvas with black lines, and its labels use a sans-serif font instead of the Graphviz default. Rendering a graph also works when `play.html` is opened as a local file. [#116208](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116208) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* In the Web UI, the digits of a long number in the result table are now grouped by a thin gap, as in `1 234 567`, instead of an underline. The gap is an empty element, so selecting or copying the number still yields the digits alone. [#119839](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119839) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Show a checkmark on the Play UI result-table copy button for 1.2 s after a cell value is copied to the clipboard. [#120617](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120617) ([yanglongwei](https://github.com/ylw510)).
* In the Web UI, the browser window title of a renamed query tab is now exactly the tab's name. [#122067](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122067) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Web UI: when a login last succeeded with an empty password, a stale password autofilled by the browser's password manager for that server and user is no longer used. [#118638](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118638) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The schema visualizer now supports hiding and showing table categories by clicking their legend labels, with a Hide all / Show all control. [#124963](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124963) ([Mark Needham](https://github.com/mneedham)).
* The command-line client no longer shows as-you-type hints on a query recalled from the history or otherwise displayed at once, so pressing Up/Down keeps navigating the history until the line is edited. [#115065](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115065) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The progress bar in `clickhouse-client` and `clickhouse-local` no longer flickers when the query result is redirected to a pipe or a file. [#123549](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123549) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A bare table name can now use the archive path syntax in `clickhouse-local`, so `SELECT * FROM 'archive.tar.zst::data.native'` reads a file inside an archive, like `SELECT * FROM file('archive.tar.zst::data.native')`. The same applies to tables of a `URL` database with a `file://` target and of a `Filesystem` database. [#118170](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118170) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Clean up client output when a web terminal or SSH session is closed during a running query, avoiding spurious write-buffer errors. [#125558](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125558) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A musl-built ClickHouse binary is now self-contained enough to run in a `FROM scratch` container image with no extra files: CA certificates are embedded, `localhost` resolves to loopback even without `/etc/hosts`, and DNS no longer falls back to the unreachable localhost when `/etc/resolv.conf` is missing. [#115389](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115389) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Hedged requests are now supported on macOS. Previously `use_hedged_requests` was silently ignored on macOS and distributed queries always used non-hedged connections. [#119915](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119915) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Determine the number of CPUs from the kernel's sysfs lists instead of the libc, so per-CPU structures and `auto` thread counts do not depend on the libc's interpretation of the affinity mask. [#118912](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118912) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Workloads created without a parent workload are now allowed; they become children of an implicit root workload, so several workload trees can coexist. [#118578](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118578) ([Sergei Trifonov](https://github.com/serxa)).
* `RANDOMIZE FOR` is now applied on each replica of a refreshable materialized view independently, so a random replica performs each refresh instead of the same one every time. Replicas no longer agree on `next_refresh_time`, so the value reported by `system.view_refreshes` on one replica is the time that replica would refresh at, not necessarily when the refresh happens. Closes [#78410](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/78410). [#112979](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112979) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `FileLog` now reads a file again from the beginning if, at the next read, it is shorter than the offset already read from it (for example after `logrotate` with `copytruncate`), instead of failing every read of the table with `CANNOT_READ_ALL_DATA` until the file grows back to its old size. [#123390](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123390) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A relative path in the `FileLog` table engine is now resolved against `user_files_path`, like in the `file` table function, so `FileLog('nginx/', 'JSONEachRow')` reads `<user_files_path>/nginx/`. A relative path that already points inside `user_files_path` from the server's working directory keeps its meaning. [#123397](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123397) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Connections from the `MongoDB` table engine, table function, and dictionary source now identify themselves to the MongoDB server with the ClickHouse name and version in the driver handshake metadata. MongoDB operators can tell ClickHouse connections apart from other clients in server logs, `currentOp` output, and monitoring tools. [#122205](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122205) ([bisht2050](https://github.com/bisht2050)).
* Lookups of `YTsaurus` dictionaries can now be throttled. [#100044](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/100044) ([MikhailBurdukov](https://github.com/MikhailBurdukov)).
* AI functions using the `openai`, `huggingface`, or `tei` provider send the query ID and the initial query ID in the `X-ClickHouse-Query-Id` and `X-ClickHouse-Initial-Query-Id` HTTP headers. [#123622](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123622) ([George Larionov](https://github.com/george-larionov)).
* `aiFilter` now requests structured JSON Schema output. [#121526](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121526) ([Andrew Wilkins](https://github.com/axw)).
* WebAssembly UDF calls with `ABI BUFFERED_V1` now measure the whole input batch instead of one serialization per row, so usage is priced by the stream the guest actually receives for formats whose cost is not additive across rows. [#118989](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118989) ([Vasily Chekalkin](https://github.com/bacek)).
* Allow the `ON CLUSTER` clause for `RENAME TABLE` and `EXCHANGE TABLES` on a `Replicated` or Cloud `Shared` database when the cluster name matches the database's automatic cluster name. The clause is treated as a no-op and the database engine propagates the DDL as usual, which lets scripts written for non-`Replicated` databases be ported without rewriting `ON CLUSTER` clauses. [#106192](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/106192) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Stored HTTP handler queries no longer break when a request supplies `max_parser_depth`, `max_parser_backtracks`, or `max_query_size`, and the `CREATE HANDLER`, `ALTER HANDLER`, and `DROP HANDLER` documentation is now published. [#114883](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114883) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Improve memory tracking for query sources, including recursive CTEs and external data sources, so their container allocations count toward memory limits. [#124952](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124952) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* A subquery with its own `SETTINGS` clause now plans `GROUP BY` using the settings after constraints and `max_threads` clamping are applied, instead of reapplying the clause as written. This prevents nested `SETTINGS max_threads = ...` from bypassing settings constraints or expanding the aggregation pipeline to an excessive number of streams. [#123156](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123156) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Improved the error message of `parseDateTime`. [#120489](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120489) ([Robert Schulze](https://github.com/rschu1ze)).
* Restoring an invalid data part from backup now reports `BACKUP_DAMAGED` instead of `CORRUPTED_DATA`. [#113085](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113085) ([Alexander Tokmakov](https://github.com/tavplubix)).
* Deserializing an aggregate function state that declares an oversized element count now reports `TOO_LARGE_ARRAY_SIZE` and names both the offending count and the limit, instead of a bare `POCO_EXCEPTION` carrying no numbers. [#121381](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121381) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A setting given a value of the wrong type or out of range now names the setting and the value in the error message. [#116882](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116882) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* When a dictionary with a `PostgreSQL` source and a custom `QUERY` returns its columns in an order that does not match the dictionary structure, the error now names the destination dictionary column and the column order the query must return, instead of only reporting that a value could not be parsed. [#114212](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114212) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A `PostgreSQL` dictionary whose `QUERY` returns fewer columns than the dictionary structure now fails to load with a `NUMBER_OF_COLUMNS_DOESNT_MATCH` error that names the column order the `QUERY` must use, instead of a `LOGICAL_ERROR`. A `QUERY` that returns too many columns gets the same column-order hint. [#125397](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125397) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A column matcher (`*`, `t.*`, `COLUMNS('...')`) inside `additional_table_filters`, `additional_result_filter` or a row policy is now rejected with a clear `BAD_ARGUMENTS` diagnostic instead of the obscure `Unqualified matcher * cannot be resolved. There are no table sources`. `CREATE` / `ALTER ROW POLICY` with a matcher now fails immediately. A matcher inside a subquery of such a filter, for example `x IN (SELECT * FROM allowed)`, keeps working. [#119250](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119250) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A matcher (`*`, `t.*`, `COLUMNS(...)`) used as a whole `LIMIT AFTER` or `LIMIT UNTIL` boundary is now rejected with `UNEXPECTED_EXPRESSION`, as in `WHERE`, instead of failing with `std::out_of_range` or silently using only its first column. [#123309](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123309) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `CREATE FUNCTION` now rejects a SQL user-defined function whose lambda arguments cannot be evaluated, instead of storing a function that later fails with `BAD_ARGUMENTS` on every call. Closes [#121197](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121197). [#121370](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121370) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A correlated subquery in `HAVING` of a `WITH TOTALS` query that also uses `max_rows_to_group_by` with `group_by_overflow_mode = 'any'` (and `totals_mode` other than `after_having_exclusive`) now fails with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` instead of a logical error. [#122601](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122601) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The `mergeTreeAnalyzeIndexes` and `mergeTreeAnalyzeIndexesUUID` table functions now reject a `vector_search_index_analysis` limit above `max_limit_for_vector_search_queries`, the bound under which the plan optimization rewrites a vector search query. A larger limit previously made the vector similarity index reserve memory proportional to it and exhaust the server's memory. [#122154](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122154) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An unconditioned `CROSS JOIN` is no longer reported as `INNER` in `EXPLAIN` and in the join columns of `system.query_log` when the `dpsub` join order algorithm runs with a conflict detector enabled. Query results were not affected. [#122236](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122236) ([Fisnik Kastrati](https://github.com/fkastrati)).
* Formatting a query where an `IN` is the left operand of a tuple element (`(x IN (1)).1`) inside a multi-argument function call is now stable across a format-parse-format round trip. [#124325](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124325) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Exchanging `-Merge` aggregate states with older servers now stays compatible: the state version of a `-Merge` function follows the state version of the function it merges only for peers that announce the new protocol revision `54494`. [#123070](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123070) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* An aggregate function parameter whose value has no unambiguous bare literal form, such as a `Decimal`, a wide integer, a `UUID`, an `IPv4` or `IPv6`, or a non-negative `Int64`, is now always printed with an explicit `::Type` suffix, and inside an array or tuple parameter the suffix goes on the elements. Such a parameter therefore reads back as the same value for every family, instead of only for the ones that had opted in. The typed form also travels over the `Native` protocol, so an insert into a column whose parameter was spelled without the type is now rejected there too, as it already was locally. [#121725](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121725) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Diagnostics about slowly acquired locks are now logged at `Information` level instead of `Warning`, so they no longer reach clients that use `send_logs_level = 'warning'` while still being written to the server log with a stack trace. [#123797](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123797) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Adaptive aggregation now logs less noisily by reducing the severity of its messages. [#123312](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123312) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* `kqlRangeCount` now rejects the `Int256` minimum span with step `-1` instead of incorrectly reporting an empty range. [#117212](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117212) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A distributed query whose shards plan aggregation with the experimental `enable_cascades_optimizer` no longer hits the logical error `GroupingAggregatedTransform pushed bucket N twice` on the initiator. [#119814](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119814) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Local execution of distributed query plans (`distributed_plan_execute_locally = 1`) now materializes constant columns in its in-memory exchange like serialized exchanges do, fixing a query that could fail with a `LOGICAL_ERROR` when it sorted by a constant expression. [#124097](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124097) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The internal function `__bitSwapLastTwo` now raises the same `BAD_ARGUMENTS` error for unsupported argument types when the expression is JIT-compiled as when it is interpreted. [#117613](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117613) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a UBSan failure during parallel parsing of `TabSeparated` input with an empty buffer. [#122636](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122636) ([Ray Winkelman](https://github.com/rwinkelman)).
* The `ErrorCode` registry is now sparse, so ClickHouse can reserve gaps between error codes without preallocating counters for them. [#111796](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111796) ([Maxim Orlovsky](https://github.com/UberDever)).
* Fixed a signed integer overflow when a negative or huge `lock_acquire_timeout` value is used as the timeout for scheduling a background task. [#117898](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117898) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Cancelled marks-loading background tasks are no longer counted as errors in `system.errors` and `system.error_log`. Previously `ASYNC_LOAD_CANCELED` was recorded during normal operation whenever a reader was dropped before its asynchronously loaded marks were needed. [#118928](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118928) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... EXECUTE expire_snapshots` for `Iceberg` tables now counts a file as deleted only when its removal succeeds, reports the removals that return an error in a new `failed_deletions_count` metric like `remove_orphan_files`, and counts a file that several expired manifests list only once. Closes [#125332](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125332). [#125379](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125379) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reading an archive that libarchive fails to open without giving a reason (for example a `.tar.gz` with a truncated gzip trailer) now throws `CANNOT_UNPACK_ARCHIVE` instead of `STD_EXCEPTION` (`fmt::format_error: string pointer is null`). Closes [#125620](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125620). [#125650](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125650) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `RESTORE` from a tar backup with a non-empty `password` setting now fails with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED`, like `BACKUP` to a tar archive, instead of `LOGICAL_ERROR`. Closes [#125648](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125648). [#125666](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125666) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).

#### Bug Fix (user-visible misbehavior in an official stable release)

* Fixed silent data loss when writing to an S3 key that already holds an object, for example `INSERT INTO FUNCTION s3(...)` with `s3_truncate_on_insert = 1`. Closes [#114348](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/114348). [#118829](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118829) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fix possible data loss when a part's `checksums.txt` is repaired (recalculated and written on load, or by `CHECK TABLE`) and the server loses power afterwards. The metadata rewrite is now durable and atomic (write to a temporary file, fsync, atomically rename), and an empty `checksums.txt` is now recalculated on load instead of detaching the whole part as broken. [#111414](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111414) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a part being detached as broken, losing its rows, when `columns.txt` or `metadata_version.txt` survives a power loss at length zero. Such a file is now treated like an absent one, which the load path already handles safely. [#119582](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119582) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix data loss when loading a part with a broken projection: a marks file damaged by an interrupted write could be accepted as an empty part instead of being detached as broken. [#119586](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119586) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed replica recovery of a `Replicated` database deleting the data of a `KeeperMap` table, for example after the server was killed during an `ALTER` of that table. A `KeeperMap` table that still exists in Keeper is now kept or renamed with its data, instead of being dropped and re-created empty. Closes [#111957](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/111957). [#122917](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122917) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `DROP DATABASE` of a `Replicated` database now drops a materialized view with `ENGINE = TimeSeries` instead of failing with `It's not initial query` and leaving the database half-dropped. [#123663](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123663) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed data loss when a single `ALTER TABLE` renamed a `Nested` member, or another column with a dot in its name, and modified the renamed column. Such an `ALTER` is now rejected with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED`, as it already was for other columns. [#122794](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122794) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed merges deleting all rows of a part whose rows TTL was never calculated, although none of its rows had expired: a part attached with `ATTACH PARTITION ... FROM` from a table without that rows TTL, or written before `MODIFY TTL` with `materialize_ttl_after_modify = 0` added it, for example after `MODIFY COLUMN ... REMOVE TTL`. The rows were lost in background TTL merges and in merges with fully expired parts. Closes [#121124](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121124). [#123068](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123068) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a race in `MergeTree` where an `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` or `DELETE` mutation submitted concurrently with another mutation could be silently skipped on some parts while being reported as done. [#124167](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124167) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* A mutation on a `Replicated*` table whose predicate contains a lambda can no longer prune to too few partitions when a lambda parameter name matches an `ALIAS` or `EPHEMERAL` column definition. Previously some matching rows were left unmutated while the mutation still finished successfully. [#121193](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121193) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix background merges with TTL silently stopping for the whole server. A merge with TTL that was selected and then dropped before it ran — for example because its table was dropped right after the expired rows were inserted — leaked one of the `max_number_of_merges_with_ttl_in_pool` slots, which are shared by all tables. After two such merges no table would delete expired rows any more until the server was restarted. [#121236](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121236) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Make the asynchronous startup of a `ReplicatedMergeTree` table robust against a concurrent `DETACH`, which could previously hang the startup forever or leave background tasks running on a table that was already shut down. [#121672](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121672) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix corrupted shared data of nested `JSON` objects after a failed attempt to parse a value during insert. It could lead to errors like `Unexpected version of JSON type binary encoding` or `Unknown type code` when reading the data after merges or when serializing it in binary formats. [#123898](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123898) ([Rory Shanks](https://github.com/rorylshanks)).
* The server no longer terminates when a transient error such as `MEMORY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`, a network error, or concurrent `SYSTEM RESTART REPLICA` happens while outdated data parts are loading in the background. Such errors are now retried later instead of aborting the server. [#117981](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117981) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed silent row loss when a `MergeTree` table with a projection is read with a `WHERE` in which an `AND` is used with negative polarity, under a `NOT`, as an operand of a comparison, or as the branch condition of `if`/`multiIf`/`CASE`, and one of that `AND`'s conjuncts references a column the projection does not store. [#120286](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120286) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed some `SELECT` queries silently returning fewer rows when the table has a projection that exists only in some of its parts, for example one added with `ALTER TABLE ... ADD PROJECTION` after data was inserted, and `query_plan_remove_unused_columns` is `0`, which `compatibility` up to `25.11` sets. [#124022](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124022) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong query results with skip indexes on `SummingMergeTree`, `AggregatingMergeTree`, `CoalescingMergeTree`, and `GraphiteMergeTree` tables: with `optimize_on_insert`, the default, an `INSERT` built every skip index whose expression is not a plain column from the rows before they were merged, so queries using the index could skip granules with matching rows. `SummingMergeTree` also no longer keeps rows whose summed columns total zero because of such an index. [#123291](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123291) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results when a `WHERE` clause mixing `AND` and `OR` was evaluated with skip indexes (`use_skip_indexes_for_disjunctions`, enabled by default). If a `minmax` or `set` index could not evaluate one atom of the predicate for a granule, the per-atom verdicts recorded for that granule were shifted, so one atom's verdict was applied to another and granules holding matching rows were skipped. [#124303](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124303) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a query silently returning fewer rows after an unrelated `JOIN` had run: a join runtime filter moved into a read's `PREWHERE` made the read record a false "no matching rows" verdict in the query condition cache. [#119823](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119823) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the query condition cache serving one session's granule verdicts to another when the two differ only in a setting a conversion function captured, such as `precise_float_parsing`, which silently dropped matching rows from the second session's result. [#119168](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119168) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `LEFT`, `RIGHT`, `FULL`, and `ANY` outer `JOIN` silently dropping non-matched rows when the `WHERE` filter reads a `Date32` or `Enum` column of the non-preserved side. Closes [#121115](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121115). [#121181](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121181) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a join runtime filter silently dropping rows whose join key is a float. When the build side had a single distinct value the filter replaced set membership with `equals`, but a hash join compares float keys bitwise, so `NaN` did not match itself and `0.0` matched `-0.0`. [#119720](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119720) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix wrong values after lazy materialization expands a sparse column during a `JOIN`: rows could receive values from other rows. [#124442](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124442) ([Nikolai Kochetov](https://github.com/KochetovNicolai)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... EXECUTE expire_snapshots` on an `Iceberg` table now always keeps the head snapshot of every branch, even when the table property `history.expire.min-snapshots-to-keep` is `0`. Previously such a table could lose all its data files. Closes [#124333](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124333). [#125365](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125365) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an `ssl_certificate` authentication bypass where an embedded NUL byte in a certificate's Common Name (for example `john\0.evil.com`) was truncated server-side to `john`, letting a holder of such a certificate authenticate as a different user. [#117403](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117403) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Fixed one failed write to a non-replicated `MergeTree` table's deduplication log (with `non_replicated_deduplication_window` greater than zero) making every following `INSERT` into that table fail with `Cannot write to canceled buffer` until the server was restarted. The log now replaces a writer that a failed flush left unusable, no longer finalizes such a writer while rotating or shutting down, and no longer stops removing its outdated files, which could also make `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY SETTING non_replicated_deduplication_window` fail. [#118350](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118350) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results after `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN <column> <type> AFTER <other>` on a column used in `PARTITION BY`. Closes [#92834](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/92834). [#120862](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120862) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a merge or a mutation making a table unreadable (`Stream ... is not found`) when a column that a part does not hold is dropped and its name is also the name of a subcolumn of another column, for example `a.size0` next to an `Array` column `a`. [#118246](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118246) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix wrong rows returned by `ORDER BY ... LIMIT` over a `MergeTree` table read in the order of a function of its sorting key, for example `ORDER BY toStartOfMonth(date) DESC, i`, when the sorting-key column uses sparse serialization. [#109424](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109424) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... DROP PARTITION` and other partition commands with an invalid or out-of-range `Date` or `DateTime` literal, such as `'2024-02-30'` or `'9999-01-01'`, no longer silently act on a different partition. They now raise an error. [#122419](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122419) ([DeviousCardi](https://github.com/DeviousCardi)).
* Fixed wrong query results and unsorted parts in `MergeTree` tables whose sorting, primary or partition key reads a subcolumn of a column (for example ``PRIMARY KEY cityHash64(`c1.1`)`` or `ORDER BY t.a`) when a column `TTL` or an `UPDATE` changes that column. A column `TTL` on such a column is now refused as on any other key column, `UPDATE` of a column that a `MATERIALIZED` key column of this kind is computed from is refused, and TTL merges of a table already stored with such a `TTL` fail instead of writing unsorted parts. [#123628](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123628) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* After a canary OOM, only the merges and mutations that are already running are cancelled. Newly scheduled ones now run as usual instead of all later merges and mutations staying cancelled until server restart. Closes [#121748](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121748). [#123870](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123870) ([Javier G. Sogo](https://github.com/jgsogo)).
* Fixed a spurious `Cannot schedule a task: no free thread ... (CANNOT_SCHEDULE_TASK)` error that rejected a valid `SELECT` over a `MergeTree` table when it had more parts to analyse than the query had threads and their analysis took longer than `lock_acquire_timeout`. Index analysis now runs one pool job per thread instead of one per part, which also makes it faster for queries over many parts. [#116822](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116822) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results and `CANNOT_READ_ALL_DATA` or `INCORRECT_DATA` errors when reading `MergeTree` parts in packed storage (`min_bytes_for_full_part_storage`), for example with parallel replicas, with a patch part of a lightweight update or delete, or when reading a `JSON` subcolumn. [#116920](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116920) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ILLEGAL_COLUMN` (`Illegal column ... of first argument of function to...`) raised by every query touching a column that was dropped and re-added with a different type, for as long as a part still carried the old column. The re-added column now reads as its default, as it does once the drop's mutation rewrites the part. Queries served from a projection no longer return the old values of such a column. [#118585](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118585) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed reading stale data, or a `Sizes of nested column and null map of Nullable column are not equal` / `Got empty stream for SerializationLowCardinality keys` error, from a column that was turned into `ALIAS` or `EPHEMERAL` (or dropped while its partition was detached) and later made physical again, when a mutation that rewrites only some columns (for example `MATERIALIZE INDEX`) ran on a Wide part in between. [#124516](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124516) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a use-after-free (on some versions a crash with `Pure virtual function called!`) on `INSERT`, merge or `SELECT ... FINAL` of a `SummingMergeTree` table with a `Nested` column whose name ends in `Map` and whose element type has `LowCardinality` inside an `Array` or `Map`, for example `Array(LowCardinality(String))`. [#125533](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125533) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a `MergeTree` table with a rows `TTL` and a `text` index never dropping its expired parts: the `TTLDrop` merge failed with `Text index transform for index ... not found` and retried forever. [#113385](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113385) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed dropping whole parts with TTL (`ttl_only_drop_parts = 1`) still reading all data from large, fully expired parts. [#108626](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/108626) ([Michael Jarrett](https://github.com/EmeraldShift)).
* Fixed a server crash on `CREATE TABLE` or `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY TTL` when a `TTL` expression uses the result of `arrayMap` with a constant lambda, such as `toString(arrayMap(x -> CAST(1, 'Dynamic'), arr))`. [#122868](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122868) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` (`Not found column _row_exists in block`) that permanently blocked all further mutations of a `MergeTree` table after a lightweight `DELETE` run with `lightweight_delete_mode = 'lightweight_update'` was followed by a heavyweight `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` and then by another mutation rewriting all columns. Closes [#115570](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/115570). [#115665](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115665) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ALTER DELETE` and `ALTER UPDATE` silently skipping rows when the predicate references an `ALIAS` column whose definition contains a non-deterministic function such as `now`. [#117273](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117273) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a patch part whose `source_parts.dat` lost its content to a zeroed block loading as an empty index, which silently unapplied an acknowledged lightweight `UPDATE` and then let background cleanup delete the only copy of it. Such a part is now detached as broken. [#119585](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119585) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... DELETE` and `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` skipping a whole partition when the parts the initiating replica holds in it are all empty: rows another replica had acknowledged in that partition survived the mutation on every replica. [#119149](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119149) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a pending lightweight `DELETE` read with `apply_mutations_on_fly = 1` evaluating its predicate over patch parts created after it, which made rows disappear from the result and reappear after the mutation materialized. Closes [#118049](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/118049). [#118919](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118919) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)). [#119306](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119306) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
