* Fixed lightweight `UPDATE` for parts adopted from another table via `REPLACE PARTITION` or `ATTACH PARTITION FROM`: after such a part was cloned again (by a mutation that does not modify it, by a clone instead of a fetch of a replicated mutation, or by a move between disks), applying patch parts could silently drop updated rows in release builds and raise the `current_version != existing_version` exception in debug and sanitizer builds. [#115979](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115979) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed lightweight `UPDATE ... ON CLUSTER` on `ReplicatedMergeTree` tables running on every replica of a shard instead of once per shard, which applied assignments such as `SET v = v + 1` once per replica. A plain `UPDATE` in a `Replicated` database with more than one shard had the same problem. [#122393](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122393) ([Pratham Nayak](https://github.com/protomn)).
* Fixed a concurrent heavyweight `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE`/`DELETE` silently dropping an acknowledged lightweight `UPDATE` on a plain `MergeTree` table. [#119155](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119155) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed mutations such as `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` failing with `CANNOT_READ_ALL_DATA` on every attempt, and never completing, when they read a column together with its `LowCardinality` subcolumn in a Wide part: a `Dynamic` column and its `LowCardinality(String)` subcolumn, or a `Map` with `map_serialization_version = 'with_buckets'` and its `keys` or `values` subcolumn. The problem appeared in 26.6. [#123160](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123160) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an acknowledged lightweight `UPDATE` being invisible on `ReplicatedMergeTree` when it targets a column whose `RENAME COLUMN` mutation has not materialized yet. [#119144](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119144) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a lightweight `UPDATE` made before `ALTER TABLE ... RENAME COLUMN` on a `ReplicatedMergeTree` table being lost when a merge ran before the rename's mutation (for example with `apply_patches_on_merge = 0`): queries returned the old value, and the mutation then dropped the update from disk. [#124653](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124653) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a wrong result of a mutation of a `MergeTree` table when several pending mutations are executed as a single task and a patch part, created by a lightweight `UPDATE`, has a version in between: the patch was applied before the commands that precede it, so they were evaluated over data they must not see. [#119127](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119127) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `ReplicatedMergeTree` TTL processing stalling indefinitely when a TTL merge that drops fully expired parts was larger than half the free disk space. Such a merge deletes every row and therefore needs no room for its source parts, but it was postponed on their size, and the postponed entry kept occupying the only `max_replicated_merges_with_ttl_in_queue` slot, so no TTL merge ran and no space was ever freed. It is now admitted when the table's only TTL is an unconditional rows TTL. Closes [#119775](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/119775). [#119983](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119983) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ATTACH PARTITION` on `ReplicatedMergeTree` failing to rename a broken part in `detached/` aside, so every later `ATTACH PARTITION` for that partition kept failing on it. [#116734](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116734) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the replication queue never draining when a part is present locally but its node in `ZooKeeper` is missing: the `GET_PART` entry for such a part re-downloaded it and failed with `DUPLICATE_DATA_PART` on every retry. [#119189](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119189) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Writing the empty covering part of a replicated table now happens before the covered parts are removed from the working set, so an exception during the operation no longer leaves the table with unexpected data parts and readonly after reload. [#122610](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122610) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Background Keeper users now retry a `get_zookeeper_lock_acquire_timeout_ms` timeout like other Keeper errors, so a `ReplicatedMergeTree` table attached during a transient Keeper outage no longer stays read-only. [#124327](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124327) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A table read by a scalar subquery inside a `CONSTRAINT` is now a loading dependency of the table that declares the constraint. Previously such a table could be dropped, after which the server did not start, because attaching the dependent table executes the subquery. [#114923](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114923) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a server startup failure after creating a table with a `quantile*` state column whose level is a `Decimal`, a wide integer, a `UUID`, an `IPv4`, or an `IPv6`. Such a level was persisted into the metadata as a quoted string, which reparsed as a `String` and was rejected, so metadata loading failed with `CANNOT_CONVERT_TYPE` and the server did not start. The same `CREATE` also failed outright on a `Replicated` database. [#119810](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119810) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A table whose stored definition carries a column `TTL` inherited from its source table now loads again on engines that do not support `TTL` (`Merge`, `Buffer`, `Distributed`, `Remote`). Previously such a table could not be loaded, which failed the load of the whole database, and with `async_load_databases = 0` the server refused to start. The `TTL` stays in the definition and has no effect; `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN ... REMOVE TTL` removes it. [#122273](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122273) ([Mohammed Sohail](https://github.com/msohail22)).
* Fix a `Remote` or `Cluster` database without a local shard making `SELECT count()` through the `merge` table function return `0`, and making the server fail to start when a dictionary reads one of its tables. Closes [#122612](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122612). [#122661](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122661) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `Part ... intersects previous part ...` when attaching a `MergeTree` table after `REPLACE PARTITION`, if parts outdated by an earlier `DROP PART` were still on disk. The server could fail to start because of it. [#120980](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120980) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `Part ... intersects next part ...` when loading a `MergeTree` table after `REPLACE PARTITION` into it ran concurrently with `MOVE PARTITION` from it. The server could fail to start because of it. [#123780](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123780) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `S3`, `HDFS`, `AzureBlobStorage`, and `URL` tables over a named collection with `format = 'auto' NOT OVERRIDABLE` failing to load with `Override not allowed for 'format'`, which could stop the server from starting. The format that ClickHouse inferred and stored at `CREATE` time is now accepted when the table is loaded again; a format written by the user over the locked value is still refused. [#124215](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124215) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Restored compatibility with Azure Blob Storage endpoints that omit the `ETag` header. [#120419](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120419) ([alesapin](https://github.com/alesapin)).
* Fix `Part ... intersects previous part ...` when loading a `MergeTree` table after a `TRUNCATE`, `DROP PARTITION` or `MOVE PARTITION TO TABLE` failed to commit (e.g. because of a memory limit or a disk error) and a merge ran before the restart. The server could fail to start because of it. [#122375](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122375) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A `PROJECTION` that could not be analyzed when the table was loaded at server startup is no longer deleted from the table's persisted metadata by the next `ALTER`, which also orphaned its already-materialized data with no error at any step. While such a declaration is present, an `ALTER` that rewrites the table metadata is now refused (`ALTER TABLE ... DROP PROJECTION` and `CLEAR PROJECTION` still work), so it survives until the cause is removed or the user drops it. [#118378](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118378) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed non-deterministic conditions being moved to `PREWHERE` in `SELECT ... FINAL` when `optimize_move_to_prewhere` is enabled. [#109538](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109538) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Fixes wrong results with `query_plan_optimize_lazy_final` when the primary key is nullable and holds a `NULL`: the `NULL`-keyed row was silently missing from the result of a `FINAL` query. [#112857](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112857) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed rows silently dropped by index analysis when a `DateTime64` column is compared with a constant of another scale and the sorting or partition key contains a `CAST` of that column, such as `ORDER BY d::String`. [#118688](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118688) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix rows silently dropped by partition, primary-key, and skip-index pruning, and by `Iceberg` partition pruning, when a string constant is compared with a `DateTime` or `DateTime64` column whose type name carries no time zone and the effective `session_timezone` differs from the one that column's type was built with. [#120114](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120114) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `minmax` skip index, a `set` skip index, part-level minmax pruning, and statistics-based part pruning dropping rows that hold a `NaN` when the query negates a float comparison, for example `WHERE NOT (val BETWEEN 0 AND 3)`. Closes [#106533](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/106533). Closes [#106948](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/106948). [#107074](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107074) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed silently missing rows for a range predicate on `toDayOfWeek(key, mode)` with the Sunday-first modes `2` and `3` over a date primary key. [#117683](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117683) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `ngrambf_v1`, `tokenbf_v1`, and `sparse_grams` skip indexes silently dropping matching rows when an `IPv6` column is compared with a string literal, as in `WHERE ip = '2001:db8::'`. [#113157](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113157) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `set` skip index whose expression has type `Dynamic` or `Variant`, which includes an undeclared `JSON` path such as `j.a`, silently returning no rows for `!=` and `IS NULL`. [#120780](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120780) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results when a query converts a `Date` or `Date32` column to `DateTime` and the column's values leave the range of day numbers that fits into 32-bit seconds. Key analysis, statistics part pruning, and reading in sorting-key order assumed the conversion preserves order, so a filter could skip parts holding matching rows or answer `count` from metadata without filtering, and `ORDER BY` could return rows out of order. Affects `toDateTime`, `toDateTime32`, `CAST(... AS DateTime)`, and `accurateCast`. [#117031](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117031) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix rows silently dropped by primary-key pruning when `toDayOfWeek` is applied to a `DateTime` column whose time zone differs from the session one, or over a `Date32`/`DateTime64` key that leaves the `Date` range. `toDayOfWeek` with a Sunday-first `week_mode` can use the primary key again. [#118655](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118655) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed rows silently missing from a query over a table whose primary key is a `Tuple` with a `Nullable` element holding `NULL`: primary-key analysis dropped whole granules of matching rows. [#118945](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118945) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a `JSONAllPaths` skip index silently pruning every granule for a filter on a subcolumn inside a declared typed path of a `JSON` column (for example `json.a.b` for `JSON(a JSON)` or `JSON(a Tuple(b Int64))`). Such a filter no longer uses the index. [#119321](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119321) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix rows silently dropped by primary key and partition pruning when a `MergeTree` key expression applies a timezone-observing function such as `toYYYYMM`, `toString` or a `CAST` to `String` to a `DateTime`/`DateTime64` column and a compared constant declares a different timezone. Both a scalar comparison constant and an `IN`-set element are affected, including when the differing timezone sits under an `Array`, `Map`, `Tuple`, `Nullable`, `LowCardinality` or `Variant` wrapper that only one side carries, when a custom type name such as `SimpleAggregateFunction` wraps that composite on one side only, and when a sibling leaf differs only by a custom type name (`Bool` against `UInt8`). Also fix the same loss when a compared `Tuple` and the key column's disagree on explicit element names, which `toJSONString` renders as an object against an array; no timezone difference is needed. [#113781](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113781) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed rows silently dropped, or counted without being filtered, when a `DateTime64` constant of a finer scale than the column is compared with `!=`, `NOT IN`, or `IN`: index analysis truncated the constant and treated the truncated value as the exact point the query asked for, pruning the granule or partition that held a matching row. [#118957](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118957) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed index analysis for a condition that wraps the primary key in a widening integer cast such as `toInt64` and then applies another monotonic function such as `negate` or `abs`. Such a condition could silently drop matching rows, or fail with `LOGICAL_ERROR: abs's argument does not match the expected data type`. Present since 26.5. [#116699](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116699) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results from primary-key analysis for predicates such as `toDate(dt64_key) >= ...` and `toDateTime(date32_key) >= ...`: at the default `date_time_overflow_behavior = 'ignore'` the conversion wraps for values the result type cannot hold, so matching rows silently disappeared and `count()` could overcount. [#117394](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117394) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed partition pruning skipping a partition whose rows match a filter like `NOT (c <=> f(partition_key))` when `f` returns `NULL` for that partition's key value (for example `moduloOrNull` or `nullIf`). Such queries returned fewer rows than with `use_partition_pruning = 0`. [#124041](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124041) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `use_skip_indexes = 0` also disabling part pruning by the partition-key min-max index since 26.5, which made such queries read every part of the table. [#123403](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123403) ([Ashish Kumar Choubey](https://github.com/aschoube)).
* Fixed missing rows in queries mixing `AND` and `OR` when a `minmax` or `set` skip index over an expression with `mortonEncode` or `hilbertEncode` is combined with other skip indexes (`use_skip_indexes_for_disjunctions`, enabled by default). Closes [#124452](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124452). [#124784](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124784) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a query that compared a `Dynamic` or `JSON` column with a constant, such as `d = '2024-01-05'` or `d = toDate('2024-01-05')`, and returned fewer rows when the table was ordered by a transform of that column, such as `ORDER BY d::String`. Such a comparison no longer uses the primary key; compare the key expression itself, as in `d::String = '2024-01-05'`, to keep using it. [#124476](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124476) ([Ashish Kumar Choubey](https://github.com/aschoube)).
* Fixed a `text` index dropping matching rows when a `FixedString` is involved in the comparison. [#118972](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118972) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed wrong results of `hasPhrase`, `hasToken`, `hasAnyTokens`, and `hasAllTokens` over a text index with a `postprocessor` or `preprocessor` when direct read from the text index could not answer the function, for example `NOT hasPhrase` with a phrase whose tokens are all removed by a stop-word postprocessor. [#125118](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125118) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed text-index search functions returning wrong results, including silently dropped rows, on a column whose text index declares a preprocessor. [#117774](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117774) ([Elmi Ahmadov](https://github.com/ahmadov)).
* Fixed wrong results after a mutation of a wide data part that does not store a column added by a metadata-only `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN`: the mutation wrote a column statistic for that column, so statistics-based part pruning could drop a part holding matching rows and `min` and `max` could be answered with the pre-`ALTER` default. [#121936](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121936) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results for `min`, `max`, and filtered `count` on a `MergeTree` column that carries a column `TTL`. When the `TTL` emptied the column, the part kept a statistic for it and queries were answered from that stale statistic instead of from the rows the part stores. [#122058](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122058) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results from `use_query_condition_cache_for_top_k`: `SELECT ... SAMPLE ... ORDER BY ... LIMIT n` no longer reuses cache entries written by the same query without `SAMPLE`, and Top-K query condition cache entries are no longer written for reads with on-the-fly mutations or patch parts. Closes [#122564](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122564). [#122538](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122538) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)). [#122617](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122617) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)).
* Fix an exception `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` and, in other queries, wrong results when a lambda argument has the same name as an identifier of an expression that is bound to an alias outside of that lambda, for example `SELECT number + 1 AS n, arrayMap(number -> number + n, [1, 2]) FROM numbers(2)`. [#115544](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115544) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed wrong results when a `GROUP BY`, `DISTINCT`, `LIMIT BY`, or window `PARTITION BY` key was `plus` or `minus` of a partition column with a non-integer constant. [#120182](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120182) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a regression in 26.8 where columns of a parameterized view could not be qualified by the view name (`SELECT pv.col FROM pv(p = 1)` failed with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER`) and a parameterized view used as a `JOIN` operand required an explicit alias. Closes [#119837](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/119837). [#112481](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112481) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#120698](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120698) ([Dmitry Novik](https://github.com/novikd)).
* Fixed `CANNOT_INSERT_NULL_IN_ORDINARY_COLUMN` and `CANNOT_PARSE_TEXT` when a `PREWHERE` guard predicate and a throwing expression, such as a `CAST` or `toUInt64(substring(...))`, end up in the same read step: either because they read subcolumns of the same column, for example a `JSON` or `Map` column, or because a JOIN runtime filter's key computation was merged into the guarded subquery. Such a query used to fail because the throwing expression was evaluated on rows the guard had rejected. [#111954](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111954) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix wrong window function results when a `QUALIFY` condition, or a comparison derived from it by `optimize_and_compare_chain`, turns into an `indexHint` that prunes rows below the window. Closes [#121177](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121177). [#122975](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122975) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Fixed wrong aggregate values with `optimize_aggregation_in_order_limit` when the `ORDER BY` is a strict prefix of the `GROUP BY` key and a group's rows span multiple parts. Closes [#116849](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/116849). [#117573](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117573) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Fixed wrong results of `sum`, `min`, `max`, and `avg` over arithmetic with a `Decimal` operand and a constant, for example `min` greater than `max`, a changed `sum` of a division, or a missing `DECIMAL_OVERFLOW`: `optimize_arithmetic_operations_in_aggregate_functions` no longer moves such arithmetic out of the aggregate. [#117779](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117779) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `<agg>IfOrNullIf` and `<agg>IfOrDefaultIf` ignoring the outer condition when used without `GROUP BY` keys or as a window function with non-`Nullable` arguments. [#122908](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122908) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed a possible hang of a distributed `GROUP BY` query with `uniqExact` and `distributed_aggregation_memory_efficient = 0` when merging large states of two-level aggregation. [#123352](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123352) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed missing rows of an empty grouping set `()` in `GROUP BY GROUPING SETS` queries without aggregate functions. For example, `SELECT number FROM numbers(3) GROUP BY GROUPING SETS ((number), ())` returned 3 rows instead of 4, and `GROUPING SETS (())` returned one row per input row. Closes [#61461](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/61461). [#123539](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123539) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `uniq` aggregate function returned garbage (for example `18446743978444128518`) for cardinalities above roughly thirty billion, because its state kept only 32 bits of each hash and the correction formula overflowed. The state now additionally keeps a small sample of full 64-bit hashes (state version `1`) which the estimate switches to at about two billion elements: `SELECT uniq(number) FROM numbers_mt(100000000000)` returns `99360630405` (−0.64%), and the estimate stays accurate (about 1%) up to trillions. Below two billion nothing changes: the estimates, the memory usage, and the state format are exactly as before, and states written by older versions remain readable and merge correctly in mixed-version clusters. [#115393](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115393) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix wrong results for `uniq*`, `GROUP BY`, `LIMIT n BY`, `ORDER BY` after `GROUP BY`, and window `PARTITION BY` over `toString` of a `DateTime`/`DateTime64` in a time zone with a daylight-saving transition, and over `toFixedString` of a `String`. Both functions claimed to be injective while mapping distinct arguments onto the same result, and the default-on optimizations that take that claim as a proof then eliminated the wrapper. [#117238](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117238) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `optimize_rewrite_aggregate_function_with_if` (enabled by default) turning a short-circuit-guarded aggregate such as `sum(if(number = 0, 0, intDiv(42, number)))` into a query failure: the rewritten `sumIf(intDiv(42, number), number != 0)` evaluates its argument for every row, so the guarded expression ran on the rows the condition excluded and threw. The rewrite is now refused when the lifted branch relies on short-circuit evaluation. Closes [#85784](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/85784). [#118697](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118697) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results and errors of `min`, `max`, `sum`, and `avg` over an arithmetic operation with a constant with `optimize_arithmetic_operations_in_aggregate_functions` enabled (the default): on an `Array` or `Tuple` operand (`min(arr * -1)`), on an `IPv4` or `IPv6` operand (`sum(ip * 1)` threw `ILLEGAL_TYPE_OF_ARGUMENT`), for `avg` of a date, time, or interval (`avg(t + INTERVAL 500 MILLISECOND)`), for multiplication or division by zero, infinity, or `NULL` (`min(x / -0.0)`), and for a day or longer added to a `DateTime` or a `Time64` across a DST change or a month end. Closes [#122647](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122647). [#123908](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123908) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `hasAll`, `hasAny`, `hasSubstr`, `startsWith`, and `endsWith` returning wrong results on `Array(Nullable(T))`. A `NULL` element was compared as whatever value was physically stored beneath it, so these functions disagreed with `has` on identical input: `hasAll([1, 2, NULL, 4], [0])` returned `1` while `has([1, 2, NULL, 4], 0)` returned `0`. Closes [#68310](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/68310). [#116239](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116239) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results from JIT-compiled conversions between integers and `Decimal`. With the default `compile_expressions = 1`, a conversion the destination could not hold silently wrapped instead of raising `DECIMAL_OVERFLOW` once the expression crossed `min_count_to_compile_expression`. Closes [#118958](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/118958). [#118994](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118994) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `if`, `multiIf`, and `CASE` returning a wrapped value instead of raising `DECIMAL_OVERFLOW` once JIT-compiled, when a `Decimal`, `DateTime64`, or `Time64` branch is lifted to a larger scale (with default settings, after the query had run a few times). [#123186](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123186) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of `extract` and `replaceRegexpAll` (and its alias `regexp_replace`) under the default-enabled `optimize_rewrite_regexp_functions`: `extract` could return a different occurrence when a leading greedy `^.*` was stripped, or a truncated result for a pattern ending with `.*$` whose only parentheses are an inline flag group, such as `(?i)b.*$`; `replaceRegexpAll` could silently lose replacements when a pattern that can match more than once (with an inline `(?m)` flag, or a tail that can match the empty string, such as `o*$`) was rewritten to `replaceRegexpOne`; and both mishandled a trailing `.*$` or `$` that is literal text inside an unterminated `\Q`. [#117228](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117228) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)). [#117227](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117227) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)). [#118361](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118361) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed rows silently missing from the result of `match` with a pattern that has a zero-allowing quantifier behind an inline flag group or an empty `\Q\E` quote. [#118351](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118351) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed the best-effort date/time parsers reading one byte past the end of the value. The parsed result of a row of a `String` column could depend on the text of the following row: a valid value was rejected as `NULL` or zero, or `addMonths` and the rest of the add-interval family returned date and time fields taken from the neighbouring row. [#122246](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122246) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `toStartOfMinute`, `toStartOfHour`, `toStartOfDay`, `toStartOfFiveMinutes`, `toStartOfTenMinutes`, `toStartOfFifteenMinutes`, `timeSlot`, `toTimeWithFixedDate`, `toStartOfInterval` and `dateTrunc` returning a value one second later, possibly in the next interval, for a `DateTime64` before 1970 with a fractional part. For example, `toStartOfMinute(toDateTime64('1969-12-31 23:58:59.500', 3))` returned `23:59:00`. Also fixed `toStartOfInterval` with an origin and a `MONTH`, `QUARTER` or `YEAR` interval returning a value after its argument when the argument was earlier in the day than the origin, and rounding a bucket start clipped to the end of a shorter month to the previous bucket. [#122138](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122138) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed accepting a column whose two serialization streams map to the same file name (for example ``Nullable(JSON(`null` Int64))`` or ``Tuple(`a` Tuple(`b` UInt64), `a.b` UInt64)``). Such a table was created successfully, but its parts were written with both streams in one file and could not be read, merged, or checked. Creating such a column now fails with `BAD_ARGUMENTS`, as a collision between two different columns already did. Existing tables still attach, with the collision logged. [#115632](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115632) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Fix a valid `JOIN ON` query rejected with `TYPE_MISMATCH` by `optimize_redundant_comparisons`, which is enabled by default since 26.7. It affects a join expression whose `AND` has a `Nothing`-typed operand, for example a join key compared against an `ARRAY JOIN []` alias. [#117476](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117476) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results, or an `ILLEGAL_COLUMN` error, of a `JOIN` whose condition equates both a column and an expression of that column (for example `ON t1.k = t2.k AND intDiv(t1.k, 2) = intDiv(t2.k, 2)`) when a filter on that expression is pushed down to the other side of the join: the pushed-down copy applied the expression twice. Closes [#122613](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122613). [#122686](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122686) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SAMPLE` on the first table of a `JOIN` being applied to the other joined tables too, which returned too few rows, or failed with `SAMPLING_NOT_SUPPORTED` when a joined table had no sampling key. [#124767](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124767) ([awss](https://github.com/awss1i)).
* Fixed missing rows from a `Distributed` or `remote` query whose `WHERE` has a `NOT` over an `AND` containing a conjunct that cannot be converted to an AST, such as a lambda or `dictGet`: the pushed-down predicate became stronger than the original one. [#117620](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117620) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix wrong results when a query selects an `ALIAS` column together with a window function with `enable_analyzer = 1`, over a `Distributed` table (or `remote`) with two or more shards, or over a table read with parallel replicas. Values could be returned in the wrong columns with no error, or the query could fail with `NUMBER_OF_COLUMNS_DOESNT_MATCH` or `CANNOT_PARSE_DATETIME`. [#116498](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116498) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a distributed `SELECT` silently returning a partial result when `connection_pool_max_wait_ms` is set to a finite value and `skip_unavailable_shards = 1`. A shard whose replica the initiator had no free connection slot for was reported as an unavailable shard and dropped, so a two-shard query could return one shard's rows with exit code `0`. Such a shard now fails the query with `ALL_CONNECTION_TRIES_FAILED`, the same error the default `skip_unavailable_shards = 0` already produces. Closes [#120832](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120832). [#120969](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120969) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed asynchronous TLS connections occasionally failing with an unrelated OpenSSL error left by another connection on the same OS thread. This could terminate distributed queries with a misleading network exception. [#116692](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116692) ([Dmitriy Borisenko](https://github.com/otselnik)).
* Fixed a query over a `Distributed` table silently returning too few rows when its `WHERE` calls `joinGet`: the condition was sent to the shards, and each one answered the call against its own local `Join` table rather than the initiator's. `joinGet` is now treated as non-deterministic and stays on the initiator. [#121822](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121822) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Fix `THERE_IS_NO_COLUMN` for a `Distributed` query that has an `IN` over the sharding key outside its `WHERE`, in the `SELECT` projection, in `GROUP BY`, in `ORDER BY`, or in `LIMIT BY`. The default-enabled `optimize_skip_unused_shards_rewrite_in` rewrote that `IN` per shard, which changed the result column's name, so the initiator could not bind the column the shard returned. [#117252](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117252) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed cancelling a distributed, `s3Cluster` or parallel-replicas `SELECT` taking up to `receive_timeout` (300 s by default) to take effect, and never reaching some of the remote sources, when one of them was draining the packets left over after a `LIMIT` was satisfied. [#118696](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118696) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server abort (`vector[] index out of bounds`) when reading a `Distributed` table with `prefer_global_in_and_join = 1` and an `IN` expression that the analyzer shares between two parent expressions. The local to `GLOBAL IN` rewrite now rewrites the `IN` function in place, so every reference to a shared node is promoted. [#120681](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120681) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `INSERT INTO <replicated table> SELECT ... FROM url(...)`, and the same shape with `s3` or `azureBlobStorage`, with parallel replicas enabled inserting the data once per replica. Closes [#120485](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120485). [#120488](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120488) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `INSERT INTO <Distributed table> SELECT ... FROM url(...)` (and `s3`, `azureBlobStorage`, ...) with parallel replicas enabled inserting the data once per shard of the destination cluster: the query forwarded to the shards now names the `*Cluster` table function with the cluster of the `Distributed` table, so every shard reads only its share of the files. Such an `INSERT` also no longer writes into the shards' local tables when the destination has a deterministic sharding key, because the rows a cluster table function hands to a shard cannot satisfy that key and the resulting placement made `optimize_skip_unused_shards` return partial results; it falls back to the ordinary `INSERT ... SELECT` instead, which places the rows through the `Distributed` sink. [#121070](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121070) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Fixed duplicated rows returned by `ANY RIGHT JOIN` with `any_join_distinct_right_table_keys = 1` when parallel replicas are enabled. [#123683](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123683) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW ... POPULATE` and `CREATE TABLE ... AS SELECT` in a `Replicated` database with parallel replicas enabled: each replica sent its population to other replicas, so its rows landed in other replicas' tables (duplicated there, missing on the replica itself), or the query failed with `UNKNOWN_TABLE`. The population now ignores the query's parallel replicas and `parallel_distributed_insert_select` settings, so each replica fills only its own table. Closes [#124446](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124446). [#124477](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124477) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `S3` client refresh overwriting static authentication settings and breaking subsequent queries. [#120433](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120433) ([alesapin](https://github.com/alesapin)).
* Fix a server abort when an Azure Blob Storage endpoint reports an asynchronous blob copy as completed without the optional `x-ms-copy-source` or `x-ms-copy-status-description` header. [#120935](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120935) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Reads from Azure Blob Storage now verify that the returned range starts at the requested offset, so an endpoint or proxy that ignores the request `Range` fails the read with `HTTP_RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE` instead of delivering the wrong bytes. [#120955](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120955) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A bounded read from Azure Blob Storage whose response ends before the requested right bound is now reopened and, if the endpoint keeps answering short, fails with `UNEXPECTED_END_OF_FILE` instead of silently returning a truncated file. [#120963](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120963) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a buffer over-read on the sequential read path from Azure Blob Storage: an endpoint that answers a ranged request with more data than was requested could deliver bytes from outside the requested range to the caller. The end of a read is now decided by the locally set right bound, not by the `Content-Length` of the response, and a read is pinned to the listed generation of the blob with `If-Match`, so an object replaced during a read fails with `AZURE_OBJECT_CHANGED_DURING_READ` instead of being truncated (new setting `azure_validate_etag_on_read`, enabled by default). [#120910](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120910) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a disk with `object_storage_type = 'local'` and a relative `path` removing its own root directory once the last blob in it was deleted. Anything else stored in that directory was lost with it; in particular, a disk whose `path` pointed at the directory of its own `local` metadata became unusable. [#119119](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119119) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `S3` requests that keep failing with a retryable error being attempted `(s3_retry_attempts + 1)^2` times instead of `s3_retry_attempts + 1`. Fixed an error that a plain-HTTP `S3` endpoint sends before reading the whole request body (for example `NoSuchUpload` for an expired multipart upload) being retried as a `Broken pipe` network error instead of being reported. Closes [#124326](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124326). [#124557](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124557) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a heap use-after-free in the `s3`, `azureBlobStorage`, `hdfs`, and similar object-storage table functions and engines when an exception such as `MEMORY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` was thrown while creating the reader of a file in a row input format such as `CSV`. [#125090](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125090) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed exceptions such as `UNKNOWN_CODEC` when reading `MergeTree` tables through the userspace page cache (`use_page_cache_for_disks_without_file_cache = 1` with `remote_filesystem_read_method = 'read'`, or `use_page_cache_for_local_disks = 1`): files kept inside packed archives (statistics, skip indices, parts in packed storage) were read from the wrong offset. Closes [#122157](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122157). [#122949](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122949) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix Parquet `time32`/`time64` columns being read as `DateTime64` and then silently shifted by `session_timezone` when inserted into a `Time64` target column. Parquet `TIME` logical types now map directly to `Time64`, and the missing `Time64 -> Time` cast is added. [#106019](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/106019) ([Shaohua Wang](https://github.com/tiandiwonder)).
* Fixed a `Parquet` reader bug where a filter on an integer column read with a type hint that reorders the file's statistics (for example `UInt32` over a plain `INT32` column, `Enum8` over a `UINT_8` one, or `IPv4` over a `UINT_64` one) could silently return fewer rows than the file holds. Such statistics are no longer used for row group or page pruning, and a `DATE` value outside the requested type's range now raises the same error with pruning as without it. Closes [#118376](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/118376). [#121033](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121033) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an infinite loop in the Parquet reader on a ZSTD or Brotli compressed page that decompresses to fewer bytes than its header declares. Such a query could not be stopped by `max_execution_time` or `KILL QUERY`; it now fails with `INCORRECT_DATA`. Closes [#123274](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123274). [#123298](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123298) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `Unexpected number of rows in column subchunk` (`LOGICAL_ERROR`) when filtering a Parquet file whose `Bool` column is RLE-encoded (pyarrow's default for data page v2). The filter is now applied while decoding RLE-encoded and dictionary-encoded pages, which also makes `PREWHERE` over dictionary-encoded columns of Parquet files without a page index faster (ClickBench q31 1.92 s to 1.21 s). Closes [#123483](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123483). [#123618](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123618) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results when reading a `Parquet` file with a structure hint whose type differs from the stored one. Row-group and page pruning, bloom filters, and dictionary filters now hash and interpret the query constant in the stored physical type rather than the hinted one, which fixes cases such as narrowing, signedness flips, `Enum`, `Date`/`Date32`/`Time`, length-mismatched `String`/`FixedString`, and `Bool` over an integer column. Reading a `UINT_8` column with a `Bool` hint now also treats every nonzero value as `true`, so `x = true`, `x IN (true)`, and `WHERE x` agree. [#123864](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123864) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#121077](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121077) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#124285](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124285) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `Parquet` output failing with `Capacity error: array cannot contain more than 2147483646 bytes` or `Uncompressed page is too big` when a batch of rows held more than 2 GiB of `String` or `FixedString` data. A batch is now bounded by bytes as well as by rows, which also lowers the peak memory of the write. [#122482](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122482) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `SELECT ... FORMAT Arrow` failing with `Method getDataAt is not supported` for `JSON`, `Dynamic`, and `QBit` columns, and silently exporting heap addresses instead of the aggregate state for `AggregateFunction` columns. Added the `output_format_arrow_unsupported_types` setting (`throw` / `text` / `binary`, default `binary`), which supersedes `output_format_arrow_unsupported_types_as_binary`. Such columns can now also be read back into their original type, which previously failed or silently corrupted the value. `INSERT INTO ... ENGINE = ArrowFlight` now follows this setting too; it previously rejected such columns unconditionally, so an insert that used to fail may now write an opaque column. Set `output_format_arrow_unsupported_types = 'throw'` to keep rejecting it. [#115250](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115250) ([Yakov Olkhovskiy](https://github.com/yakov-olkhovskiy)).
* Fix `O(n^2)` decompression of gzip/zlib/deflate streams whose single DEFLATE block spans the whole stream, the shape zlib-ng produces at compression level 1 (the default of the official .NET SDK): a 24 MiB gzip HTTP body that took 11 s to ingest in 26.7 now takes 0.12 s, identical to a multi-block body, with memory bounded by the buffer size instead of the block size. [#114059](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114059) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a segmentation fault or `std::length_error` when reading `TSV` formats with `input_format_tsv_crlf_end_of_line = 1` if an escape sequence ended exactly at the end of a read buffer. [#123861](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123861) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed lost rows when a query over a `MySQL`, `PostgreSQL`, `SQLite`, or ODBC/JDBC table compares a column, string, number, or `Decimal`, with an `Enum` constant, also inside a tuple, an `IN` list, `Nullable`, `Variant`, or `Dynamic`, at any depth. The filter sent to the external database used the `Enum`'s number, while ClickHouse compares a string column with the `Enum`'s name, and `PostgreSQL` rejected such a query. An `IN` list or row of `Variant` or `Decimal` constants is no longer sent as one quoted string. [#123843](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123843) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of a negated `IN` with a `NULL` in the list, e.g. `x NOT IN (1, NULL)` or `NOT (x IN (1, NULL))`, over `MySQL`, `PostgreSQL`, `SQLite`, and `ODBC` tables: the filter pushed to the external database now leaves the `NULL` members out of the list, the way ClickHouse evaluates the set. [#124339](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124339) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed silent data loss when an `EmbeddedRocksDB` table is renamed. `RENAME TABLE` now moves the table's `RocksDB` directory to the table's new location, so the rows are still there after a server restart. Previously the directory was left behind, and after a restart the table opened a fresh empty `RocksDB` while the real rows stayed orphaned under the old path. [#112224](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112224) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `optimize_inverse_dictionary_lookup` (enabled by default) no longer rewrites `dictGet(dict, attr, key) = const` when a `Variant`, `Dynamic`, or `JSON` appears in the type of `key` or in the dictionary's declared key type. The rewritten predicate is not equivalent there, so the query could fail with `CANNOT_INSERT_NULL_IN_ORDINARY_COLUMN`, `ILLEGAL_TYPE_OF_ARGUMENT`, or `NO_COMMON_TYPE`, or return wrong results. [#120888](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120888) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed SQL injection through identifiers in the `PostgreSQL` integration: relation, schema, column, publication, and replication-slot names used by the `PostgreSQL` table engine, the `postgresql` table function, `PostgreSQL` dictionary sources, and `MaterializedPostgreSQL` were escaped with backslashes, which PostgreSQL does not honour inside a quoted identifier, so a remote name containing a double quote could end the identifier and let the rest execute on the PostgreSQL server. Relations and columns whose names contain a double quote or a backslash can now be read and replicated, including those listed in `materialized_postgresql_tables_list`; an empty element in that setting is now rejected. [#119216](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119216) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#119103](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119103) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `PARALLEL WITH`, `EXECUTE AS`, and the `QueryRunner` table engine skipping the access checks of the `CREATE` statements they execute, which allowed creating tables and databases without the corresponding privileges and defeated the `readonly` and `allow_ddl` settings. [#119848](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119848) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed an `HDFS` database serving a table from its internal cache without the `READ ON HDFS` grant. The grant and the `remote_url_allow_hosts` filter were checked only while a table name was resolved for the first time, so once any user holding the grant had read a name, every user with `SELECT` on the database was served the contents of the file, and that table kept being served after its host was removed from the allow-list. `EXISTS TABLE`, which requires only `SHOW TABLES`, answered from the same cache and so reported which names other users had resolved. Both checks now run before the cache is consulted, and the existence probe no longer consults it. [#119523](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119523) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed filesystem containment checks accepting a path with an embedded NUL byte. A `DeltaLakeLocal` or `IcebergLocal` table could be pointed outside of `user_files` this way. [#119846](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119846) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed several engines disclosing an `Alias` table's target schema to a user who holds no privilege on that target. Reading the metadata of an `Alias` through any of them now requires table-level `SHOW COLUMNS` on its target, which is what a `DESCRIBE` of the target already requires. [#117572](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117572) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `remote(<cluster name>, 'db', 'table', 'user', 'password')`, and the same form of `remoteSecure` and of the `Remote` and `RemoteSecure` table engines, no longer write the password into `system.query_log`, `system.text_log`, and the server log in cleartext. [#121474](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121474) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed SQL injection through identifiers when inserting into a table with the `SQLite` engine. It is now also possible to insert into a SQLite table whose name, or column name, legitimately contains a double quote or a backslash. [#117421](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117421) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed credential masking for `nats_url`, `rabbitmq_address`, the `jdbc` and `odbc` connection string, and the `format_avro_schema_registry_url`, `url_base`, and `s3_base` settings in `SHOW CREATE`, `system.tables`, and the query text in `system.query_log`. Closes [#121257](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121257). [#121425](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121425) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `Backup` database engine printing the credentials of its destination in plaintext. `SHOW CREATE DATABASE`, `system.databases.engine_full`, `system.query_log`, and the messages that reject a malformed locator no longer expose an `AzureBlobStorage` `account_key`, a connection string, or a presigned URL regardless of `format_display_secrets_in_show_and_select`. [#117429](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117429) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `KILL TRANSACTION ... ON CLUSTER`, `KILL PART_MOVE_TO_SHARD ... ON CLUSTER`, `SYSTEM STOP THREAD FUZZER ON CLUSTER`, `SYSTEM START THREAD FUZZER ON CLUSTER`, and `SYSTEM RESET COVERAGE ON CLUSTER` no longer run without checking any privilege. Each now requires what its non-`ON CLUSTER` spelling has always required. [#118360](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118360) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed metadata disclosure through a chain of `Alias` tables. `DESCRIBE`, `hasColumnInTable`, `system.columns`, `system.tables`, `system.projections`, `system.constraints`, and `system.data_skipping_indices` now require the privilege on every table an `Alias` resolves through, as reading its rows already did. [#118967](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118967) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a bug where `REVOKE GRANT OPTION FOR` was accepted but then omitted from `SHOW GRANTS`, from `system.grants`, and from the persisted access metadata whenever another revoked database, table, column, or named-collection name shared a prefix with it. The grant option therefore came back after a server restart, and the user could pass it on with `GRANT CURRENT GRANTS`. [#122078](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122078) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed settings constraints from a settings profile not being enforced against the other setting assignments in the same statement. `SET profile = 'p', <setting> = <value>` could override a `CONST`, `MIN`/`MAX`, or `readonly` constraint that profile `p` installs. The same escape existed through an in-query `SETTINGS` clause, HTTP URL parameters, and Arrow Flight session options. [#120376](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120376) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed row policies on `Memory` tables being ignored when a read is executed from a serialized query plan, such as a distributed plan or a parallel-replicas plan. Since `Memory` gained `PREWHERE` support, such reads could return rows the policy excludes. [#122503](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122503) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed an integer underflow in the `MySQL` protocol handshake handler that could cause the server to buffer an unbounded amount of data from an unauthenticated client. [#115776](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115776) ([Dmitrii Tishkin](https://github.com/dmi03)).
* Statistics-based part pruning is now disabled for tables with row policies. [#122035](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122035) ([alesapin](https://github.com/alesapin)).
* A row policy on a `Set`-engine table was accepted but never applied: the right side of `IN` read the prebuilt set directly and returned the rows the policy hides. Such a query now fails with `ACCESS_DENIED`, because the `Set` engine has no read path that could filter those rows, and `IN set_table` now requires `SELECT` on all columns of the table. On `Replicated` databases, `ALTER ... DELETE`, `ALTER ... UPDATE`, `DELETE`, and `UPDATE` now run the initiator's checks as the submitting user, so the same row-policy and access checks apply there too; with `ON CLUSTER`, run the query on a host that has the right-hand `Set` table, or enable `distributed_ddl_use_initial_user_and_roles` on every host and set `distributed_ddl_entry_format_version` to at least `8`. Queries that used a `Set` table covered by a database-wide row policy ran unfiltered before and now throw. [#120312](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120312) ([Harikrishnan Prabakaran](https://github.com/harikrishnan94)). [#122141](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122141) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed the `Kafka` and `NATS` table engines ignoring `remote_url_allow_hosts`: broker addresses from `kafka_broker_list`, `nats_url`, and `nats_server_list` are now checked against the remote host filter. A forbidden address is rejected at `CREATE TABLE` with `UNACCEPTABLE_URL`, as the `RabbitMQ` engine already does. [#121825](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121825) ([János Benjamin Antal](https://github.com/antaljanosbenjamin)).
* HTTP headers in `HTTP` dictionary sources are now hidden in `SHOW CREATE DICTIONARY`, `system.tables`, and query logs, like passwords. They can still be shown with `display_secrets_in_show_and_select`. [#122673](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122673) ([Matheus Nerone](https://github.com/Manerone)).
* Fixed secret settings of `S3Queue` and `AzureQueue` tables written with the legacy `s3queue_` prefix, such as `s3queue_after_processing_move_secret_access_key`, being shown in cleartext in `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, `system.tables`, `system.query_log`, and the server log. They are now hidden as they are without the prefix. [#123060](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123060) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Column-level `SELECT` privileges are now enforced for columns used only inside `indexHint` and for `ALIAS` columns referenced in `PREWHERE`. As in `WHERE`, referencing an `ALIAS` column in `PREWHERE` requires the grant on the alias name; a grant on its source columns is no longer sufficient. A metadata-only `ALTER` that changes a subcolumn read by a `MATERIALIZED` column through an `ALIAS` is now refused like a direct reference. [#120516](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120516) ([Shankar Iyer](https://github.com/shankar-iyer)).
* Fixed a row policy bypass: a direct `JOIN` with an `EmbeddedRocksDB`, `KeeperMap`, or `Redis` table in the legacy analyzer (`enable_analyzer = 0`) ignored the row policy of the right table and returned hidden rows, and `joinGet` on a `Join` table ignored its row policy in both analyzers. Such a table is now joined through its query plan, which keeps the filter, and `JOIN` or `joinGet` against a `Join` table under a row policy throws `ACCESS_DENIED`. [#121398](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121398) ([Vladimir Cherkasov](https://github.com/vdimir)).
* `CREATE OR REPLACE` in a `Replicated` database or with `ON CLUSTER` now requires the drop privilege for the kind of the table it replaces, so a user without `DROP DICTIONARY` can no longer replace a dictionary. [#122272](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122272) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* A view with `SQL SECURITY DEFINER` or `SQL SECURITY NONE` that can hide rows is now read as a whole and is opaque to the optimizations of the query that reads it; previously an expression of the outer query could be evaluated on the hidden rows and reveal them, for example through an exception message. `EXPLAIN` shows the plan of such a view only to users who could have created it or who may display secrets, and seeing the plan of such a sealed view now also requires the `CREATE VIEW` privilege. [#122330](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122330) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)). [#123325](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123325) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a credential written in cleartext to `system.query_log` and the server log when a `key = value` argument precedes the positional arguments of the `mongodb`, `redis`, `remote`, `arrowFlight`, and `ytsaurus` table functions or the `MongoDB`, `Redis`, `Remote`, `ArrowFlight`, `ExternalDistributed`, and `YTsaurus` table engines. A positional argument after a named one is now always hidden, and the value of a named-collection override whose key is written as an expression is hidden for every such engine. [#122770](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122770) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Rewriting the body of a view with `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY QUERY` now needs the same grants as creating it with its SQL security: `SET DEFINER` on the definer of a `SQL SECURITY DEFINER` view, or `ALLOW SQL SECURITY NONE` for a `SQL SECURITY NONE` view. An `ON CLUSTER MODIFY QUERY` fails with `UNKNOWN_TABLE` unless it runs on a host that has the view. [#121629](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121629) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fixed `GRANT ... ON db*.table` being silently rewritten to `db.table*`, which could cause incorrect privilege grants. The pattern `db*.table` is now rejected as a syntax error. `CREATE` / `ALTER` / `DROP ROW POLICY`, `SHOW POLICIES`, and `SHOW CREATE POLICY` now also reject prefix wildcards (`db*.*`, `db.table*`, `table*`), which were previously silently narrowed to the exact name (`db.*`, `db.table`, `table`). [#108063](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/108063) ([Aditya Kumar](https://github.com/adityaksolves)).
* Update `mongo-c-driver` to 2.5.6 and `mongo-cxx-driver` to r4.6.0, fixing an out-of-bounds write during MongoDB connection monitoring reconnects (CVE-2026-96746). [#119346](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119346) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)). [#124282](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124282) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)). [#124648](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124648) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Fixed access entities (users, roles, row policies, and others) of the `local_directory` access storage being lost after a restart, or a dropped row policy making every query fail with `FILE_DOESNT_EXIST`. This happened to entities created or dropped within a minute after `SYSTEM RELOAD USERS` if the server was then killed, or at any time after `SYSTEM RELOAD USERS` failed to write the storage's `.list` files. [#125058](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125058) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The web terminal and the SSH interface no longer reset the settings of the user (from settings profiles, roles, and `CREATE USER ... SETTINGS`) to default values. Previously, restrictions such as `readonly`, resource limits, and `additional_table_filters` were not applied in these interfaces. [#125515](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125515) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Update `expat` to 2.9.0, fixing a denial of service through a crafted backup manifest during `RESTORE` (CVE-2026-66046) and the other 2.8.3–2.9.0 security fixes. [#124255](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124255) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Fixed failures during pipeline expansion, such as external sorting or the merge stage of a `GROUP BY`, when the memory limit is exceeded. The query now reports the original exception instead of an inconsistent-pipeline error, a segmentation fault, or a read of freed memory. Closes [#125251](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125251). [#121502](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121502) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)). [#125283](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125283) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a stored query combining `UNION` with a parenthesized `INTERSECT`/`EXCEPT` group losing its parentheses when serialized, so `X UNION ALL (Y EXCEPT Z)` was read back as `(X UNION ALL Y) EXCEPT Z`. Views returned wrong results after a server restart, and a materialized view could end up with metadata the server refuses to load, which prevented it from starting. [#111931](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111931) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the `compatibility` setting: `compatibility = '26.8'` no longer reverts five defaults that 26.8 shipped or forces `MergeTree` `patch_parts_version` back to `v1`, and `compatibility = '26.1'` no longer disables `use_skip_indexes_on_data_read`, which 26.1 shipped enabled. [#118666](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118666) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `compatibility = '25.11'` incorrectly resolving `query_plan_max_limit_for_lazy_materialization` to `10` instead of `100`. [#122768](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122768) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Setting the obsolete `enable_analyzer` (`allow_experimental_analyzer`) to `0` is accepted again and replaced with `1`, instead of failing with `SETTING_CONSTRAINT_VIOLATION` or `INCORRECT_QUERY`. Queries, sessions, settings profiles, and client configurations that still set it keep working after an upgrade, always with the analyzer, including configurations that spell the value as a `Float64` literal. [#122401](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122401) ([Nikita Fomichev](https://github.com/fm4v)). [#122763](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122763) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed a server crash on AArch64 that could happen when the query profiler sampled a thread while it was connecting to a remote server, for example with `remote` or `remoteSecure`. Capturing stack traces, used for exceptions and by the query profiler, is also several times faster. [#122834](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122834) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed `listen_host` `::` on hosts without a global IPv6 address, such as a container on an IPv4-only Docker network: it failed with a DNS error, so the official Docker image listened on IPv4 only and `localhost` over `::1` was refused. The server now listens on the dual-stack wildcard there. gRPC and Arrow Flight use one listener when `::` is the only wildcard, and `host_regexp` / `HOST REGEXP` / `HOST LIKE` restrictions match IPv4 clients of a dual-stack listener. [#122785](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122785) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an issue where pressing Ctrl-R and then Ctrl-Z in the web terminal or the SSH interface suspended the whole server process. [#125512](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125512) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `TOO_LARGE_STRING_SIZE` (`Too large sizes of FixedString to deserialize`) when reading a correctly written part whose `Array(FixedString(N))` column holds more than 1 GiB of elements in one read span. Such a span is now read in bounded steps instead of being rejected, so the affected parts can be read and merged again without being rewritten. Closes [#116772](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/116772). [#117385](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117385) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `CollapsingMergeTree` returning wrong rows when a row's `sign` is neither `1` nor `-1`. [#119900](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119900) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Fixed a table that exceeds `max_table_size_rows` never being able to come back under the limit: every merge threw `TABLE_SIZE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`, so the table could not be compacted and its part count grew until inserts started failing. [#117229](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117229) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a shutdown stall where a background merge or mutation left in a `MergeTree` background executor's queue kept holding its table lock, so any table lock taken later during shutdown waited for the full `lock_acquire_timeout` and then failed with `DEADLOCK_AVOIDED`. [#113653](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113653) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `count()` returning the number of default values instead of the number of `NULL`s when `optimize_trivial_count_with_sparsity_filter` is used on a column widened to `Nullable` by a metadata-only `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN`. [#120245](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120245) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed `MergeTree` tables with `non_replicated_deduplication_window` on object storage failing to load their deduplication log after being detached and attached back without inserts in between, and fixed the deduplication log possibly dropping its active file. [#121670](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121670) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... COMMENT COLUMN` and `ALTER TABLE ... RENAME COLUMN` making reads of an `ALIAS` column's subcolumns, for example `arr.size0`, fail with `NO_SUCH_COLUMN_IN_TABLE` until the table was reloaded. Closes [#121421](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121421). [#121449](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121449) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `CANNOT_READ_ALL_DATA` (`while reading prefix of column ...`) during query planning when a `MergeTree` table has an active empty Wide part, left by a `TTL` drop or by a mutation that deleted all rows of a part, and the query filters on a subcolumn, such as a key of a `Map(LowCardinality(String), String)` column. [#122756](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122756) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ATTACH PART` failing with `CORRUPTED_DATA` for a Compact part whose `default_compression_codec.txt` is missing, for example a part copied or restored without that file. [#119114](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119114) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a `LOGICAL_ERROR` (`Sizes of nested column and null map of Nullable column are not equal after deserialization`) that made a `SELECT` of a `Nested` member fail with `Code: 49` until the server was restarted, after an `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN` on that member had been killed and a later mutation hardlinked the column into a new wide part. [#121561](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121561) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Wide parts no longer split a fixed-width value across two compressed blocks when `max_compress_block_size` is not a multiple of the value size. This improves the compression ratio of type-specific codecs on such tables and fixes `CANNOT_COMPRESS` from `ALP`. [#122841](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122841) ([Raufs Dunamalijevs](https://github.com/rienath)).
* After `ALTER TABLE ... RENAME COLUMN`, the subcolumns of the renamed column, for example `c0.null` or `a.size0`, stayed resolvable under the old name. Reading them through a `Buffer` or `Merge` table, or using them in `OPTIMIZE ... DEDUPLICATE BY`, read uninitialized memory, and once a new column reused the old name they returned wrong values instead of throwing `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER`. [#122527](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122527) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed excessive memory usage when loading a data part with a corrupted `columns_substreams.txt`; such a part is now detached as broken. [#124157](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124157) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Fixed a `LOGICAL_ERROR` and silent data corruption when a column is named into the `skp_idx_` prefix so that its on-disk stream base equals a skip index's, making both share one marks file. Such a write is now rejected with `INCORRECT_FILE_NAME` naming both owners. [#113093](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113093) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reads of a renamed `Nested` leaf column returning default values from wide parts while the `RENAME COLUMN` mutation was still pending. [#119134](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119134) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `CANNOT_COMPRESS` on merge or insert for a `Map` column with `with_buckets` serialization and `CODEC(T64)`. [#124695](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124695) ([Raufs Dunamalijevs](https://github.com/rienath)).
* Fixed a column added by `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN ... DEFAULT <expression>` being filled with the type default instead of the evaluated expression when it is read through a single-conjunct explicit `PREWHERE` on the expression's dependency. [#117533](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117533) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed the order of rows in a data part written by a merge or a mutation with `TTL ... GROUP BY ... SET` when the `SET` clause assigns a new value to a column the sorting key is calculated from. Closes [#108514](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/108514). [#119863](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119863) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when merging a table that has a rows TTL together with a move (`TO DISK`, `TO VOLUME`) or recompression (`RECOMPRESS`) TTL over a different column. [#121536](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121536) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Fixed `INSERT` into a table with a constant `TTL ... DELETE WHERE` whose condition is not exactly `UInt8`, for example `c > 10` over a `Nullable` column: a part where no row matched the condition was marked with the delete TTL and later rewritten by a needless TTL merge, and a condition that cannot be a filter, such as an `Array` column, was accepted instead of being rejected as in the other TTL code paths. [#122516](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122516) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `INSERT` into a table whose `TTL` expression changes its type under a session setting, for example a `CAST` of a `Nullable` column under `cast_keep_nullable = 1`, which failed with `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Unexpected type ... of result TTL column`. Under such a setting, `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY TTL` could also store a `TTL` that `CREATE TABLE` rejects, after which the table could not be attached again. A stored `TTL` is now analyzed with the server settings in `INSERT` and `ALTER`, as in `CREATE TABLE`. [#122973](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122973) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an unsorted part with a primary index that did not match the data after a `TTL GROUP BY ... SET` merge that rewrites the column a subcolumn sorting key (such as `ORDER BY n.a`) is taken from. Queries with a condition on the sorting key could return wrong results. [#122373](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122373) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY TTL` accepting a TTL whose subquery reads a table function (for example `numbers` or `remote`), a view over one, a parameterized view, a temporary table, or the table itself. After such an `ALTER` the table could not be loaded again, so a server with synchronous table loading did not start. Now the `ALTER` fails. [#123561](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123561) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed standalone `UPDATE ... SET` substituting a `WITH` expression alias into a nested subquery that cannot resolve it. Where that name is a table name, the statement read the alias instead of the table, or silently succeeded where the equivalent `SELECT` raises `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER`. [#120026](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120026) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `allow_non_metadata_alters = 0` now also rejects the `ALTER` commands that reach a `MergeTree` table as explicit mutations: `UPDATE`, `DELETE WHERE`, the five `MATERIALIZE` forms, `APPLY DELETED MASK`, `APPLY PATCHES`, and `REWRITE PARTS`. [#115287](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115287) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `Cannot convert NULL value to non-Nullable type` in `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` with a scalar subquery when the subquery result is `Nullable` and the updated column is not. [#119926](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119926) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... DROP COLUMN` and `ALTER TABLE ... CLEAR COLUMN` are now refused when they would leave a part with no columns at all, instead of wedging the mutation queue with a logical error or leaving an unreadable part. [#121104](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121104) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a mutation such as `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN` or `RENAME COLUMN` committing a data part with no columns when the part held only columns the table no longer has (for example a partition detached before `DROP COLUMN` and re-attached after it), after which every read of the table failed with a logical error. Such a mutation now fails with `NO_SUCH_COLUMN_IN_TABLE`, like reads of that part, and the part and its data are kept. [#122400](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122400) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` and `MATERIALIZE COLUMN` on a `Memory` table after `MODIFY COLUMN` of the updated column: later reads failed with `ILLEGAL_COLUMN` or returned wrong values, and `BACKUP` failed. [#123043](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123043) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `Found patch part ... that intersects mutation with version ...` on lightweight updates in a table created with `CREATE TABLE ... CLONE AS` or `ATTACH PARTITION ALL FROM` a table with lightweight updates. [#123620](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123620) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* A synchronous mutation (`mutations_sync`) on a `MergeTree` table no longer reports success when the table is detached before the mutation runs; it fails with `UNFINISHED`, and the mutation continues after the table is attached again. [#124161](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124161) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* `DELETE`, `UPDATE` and `ALTER ... DELETE/UPDATE` no longer refuse a `MergeTree` table of a database with `lazy_load_tables` that has not been accessed yet, and `SYSTEM DROP REPLICA ... FROM ZKPATH` no longer skips such a table when checking that the replica being dropped is not a live local one. [#118152](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118152) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
