* Fixed a `ReplicatedMergeTree` table whose metadata carries `table_readonly = 1` being unusable: its backup could not be restored, it could not be re-attached after `DETACH`, and `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY SETTING table_readonly = 0` was refused. Converting a table with that setting to replicated is no longer done, by either the `convert_to_replicated` flag or `ATTACH TABLE ... AS REPLICATED`. [#119224](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119224) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `CHECK TABLE` on a `ReplicatedMergeTree` replica whose Keeper session was lost now fails with `ABORTED` instead of returning `0`, which means that the check found broken data. Nothing was read in that case, so the healthy table was certified as broken. [#119381](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119381) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed the automatic `MergeTree` to `ReplicatedMergeTree` conversion silently not finishing for a table in a database with `lazy_load_tables`, which left the table read-only with no metadata in ZooKeeper and the conversion flag in place. [#118135](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118135) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix an integer overflow in the DDL log retention check of `Replicated` databases that could cause premature removal of DDL log entries and replicas being incorrectly declared lost for a very large `logs_to_keep`. `logs_to_keep` is now limited to `2147483647`, so replicas on older releases also stay safe during a rolling upgrade: out-of-range values are rejected on `CREATE` and clamped, with a warning, when replayed from existing metadata, server config, or Keeper, and existing databases that hold a larger value in Keeper are fixed automatically when a replica on the new release starts. [#118103](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118103) ([Kirill Shokhin](https://github.com/kirillshokhin)). [#123105](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123105) ([Kirill Shokhin](https://github.com/kirillshokhin)).
* `ALTER DATABASE ... MODIFY COMMENT` on a database with `ENGINE = Backup` no longer writes a definition the server cannot read back, which made it refuse to start; metadata already written in that form now loads too. Backward incompatibility: a backup taken by an older server of a database with `ENGINE = Backup` stores the locator quoted into a string literal, and such a definition is no longer accepted from a `RESTORE`. [#118674](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118674) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Tables in a database with `lazy_load_tables` were missing from `system.parts`, `system.replicas` and related system tables, and refused by replica commands, after a restart. [#121332](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121332) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* Fixed the server failing to start with `TABLE_ALREADY_EXISTS` or `Mapping for table with UUID=... already exists` after it was killed during `CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE`, `REPLACE TABLE`, or `CREATE TABLE ... AS SELECT` in an `Atomic` database whose metadata is on a `plain_rewritable` disk (`database_disk`). [#123902](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123902) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)). [#123832](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123832) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `EXISTS TABLE` returning `0` for a table that is still being loaded asynchronously after server startup. [#125729](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125729) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `SELECT ... FINAL` returning a superseded row when the partition expression distinguishes floating-point values that the `FINAL` merge comparator treats as equal, such as `-0.0` versus `0.0` or different `NaN` payloads. `enable_automatic_decision_for_merging_across_partitions_for_final` no longer engages for such a key. [#117482](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117482) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Row policies over floating-point sorting key columns are now applied after `FINAL`, with `apply_row_policy_after_final` enabled, and such columns are excluded from index analysis of deferred filters. Previously a policy condition distinguishing `-0.0` from `0.0` or `NaN` payloads could filter out the winning row version before the merge and return stale data. [#121782](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121782) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* `CREATE TABLE` and `ALTER TABLE ... ADD INDEX` now reject a skip index whose expression cannot be evaluated, such as `INDEX i c0 = c1 TYPE set(0)` over `String` and `Int8`, and an `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN` that would leave an existing index in that state is rejected too. Such a declaration used to be accepted, after which every `INSERT` and merge failed with `NO_COMMON_TYPE`, and `MATERIALIZE INDEX` retried that error forever. Closes [#69597](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/69597). [#118373](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118373) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SELECT count()` returning more rows than the filter accepts when a `MergeTree` primary key is a `Tuple` holding a floating point element and the key data contains a `nan`. For example, with `ORDER BY t` over `t Tuple(Float64, Int32)`, `SELECT count() FROM t WHERE t >= (5., 3)` returned `6` while `SELECT * FROM t WHERE t >= (5., 3)` returned `2` rows. A `nan` nested in a tuple is not visible in the granule bounds, so primary key analysis reported a granule of non-matching rows as wholly matching and the exact-count optimization counted its rows without evaluating the filter. Closes [#117819](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/117819). [#118683](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118683) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `WHERE f IN (nan)` silently losing the `NaN` rows of a floating-point column: statistics-based part pruning and the `minmax` skip index use ranges that exclude `NaN`, so a part or granule holding `NaN` was pruned even though `IN` matches `NaN`. [#117453](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117453) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed wrong results when a `String` primary key column is compared against a `FixedString` constant wrapped in `Variant` or `Dynamic`. [#114353](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114353) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results for `f = 0.0` and `f != 0.0` over a float column when the primary key is an injective transform of it (for example `ORDER BY toString(f)`): the index treated the transformed constant as exact, although the transform distinguishes `-0.0` from `+0.0` while the comparison does not. [#117722](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117722) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Comparing an `Enum` column with a string literal that is not one of its elements no longer throws `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM` when `validate_enum_literals_in_operators` is disabled (the default) and the column is part of the primary key, the partition key, or a `bloom_filter` index. `!=` with such a literal now returns true for every row instead of throwing. Closes [#122235](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122235), [#111545](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/111545). [#122672](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122672) ([Vladimir Cherkasov](https://github.com/vdimir)).
* Fixed an exception (such as `Division by zero`) thrown from index analysis when a monotonic function chain over a key column cannot be evaluated on a key range endpoint, for example `SELECT count() FROM t WHERE p != 1 AND intDiv(1, p - 1) > 0` with `use_skip_indexes = 0`. Also fixed a possible segmentation fault caused by a poisoned entry in the index analysis function result cache. [#117355](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117355) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a `bloom_filter` skip index on `mapValues` or `mapKeys` that dropped rows for `m['k'] IN (...)` when the map values are an `Enum` with negative values and the set contains the name of the value `0`. [#124232](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124232) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a wrong order (and wrong rows under `LIMIT`) from the read-in-order optimization when the `ORDER BY` expression maps a stored `inf` to `NaN`, as in `ORDER BY x / inf`. [#118720](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118720) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed primary key, partition key, and skip index analysis that could skip or over-count rows, and `ORDER BY` optimization that could return rows in the wrong order, for conditions on `timezoneOffset`, `toTimeWithFixedDate`, `toYYYYMMDDhhmmss`, `toHour`, and `toMinute` over a `DateTime` or `DateTime64` column when the key range spans a change of the UTC offset, for example a daylight saving time change in `America/New_York` or the 30-minute change in `Australia/Lord_Howe`. [#124474](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124474) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#124530](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124530) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of `ORDER BY` with `optimize_read_in_order`, `GROUP BY` with `optimize_aggregation_in_order`, and `LIMIT BY` with the in-order optimizations when a `String` sorting-key column is compared with a `FixedString` constant, also one held by a `Variant` or `Dynamic` constant (for example `WHERE s = toFixedString('a', 2)` or `WHERE s = CAST(toFixedString('a', 2) AS Dynamic)`), or when a `Variant` or `Dynamic` expression, such as a JSON path, is compared with a constant (for example `WHERE j.a = 1` over `Int64` and `Float64` values). Such a comparison matches several values that sort differently, but the optimization treated them as one value and skipped sorting by them. [#123677](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123677) ([Ashish Kumar Choubey](https://github.com/aschoube)). [#125378](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125378) ([Samuel Krempaský](https://github.com/NIKTONIKTO717)).
* Fixed `count()` counting rows that the filter rejects when a whole-tuple comparison against a `Tuple` primary key involves a `NULL` or a `NaN` nested in the constant, or a `NaN` nested in the key data. [#118944](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118944) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed wrong result order (and wrong rows under `LIMIT`) from the read-in-order optimization when `ORDER BY` is a monotonically decreasing function of a `Float` sorting key containing `NaN`, e.g. `ORDER BY negate(x)`. The opposite request for the same shape, `ORDER BY negate(x) ASC NULLS FIRST`, is now read in order instead of being sorted. [#118469](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118469) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed repeated reading of text index posting lists in multi-threaded queries when the global text index caches are disabled. [#120844](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120844) ([Anton Popov](https://github.com/CurtizJ)).
* The `text`, `tokenbf_v1`, `ngrambf_v1`, and `sparse_grams` skip indexes are now used for `IN` when `transform_null_in = 1` and the set contains no `NULL`. Previously such a query read every granule, and one using `force_data_skipping_indices` failed with `INDEX_NOT_USED`. [#121375](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121375) ([Jimmy Aguilar Mena](https://github.com/Ergus)).
* Fixed wrong results and the exception `Column ... already added for reading` for a `FINAL` query with `query_plan_optimize_lazy_final = 1` that reads a `text` index directly, when the `ReplacingMergeTree` sorting key is `Nullable`, floating-point, or mixes `ASC` and `DESC` columns. [#122534](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122534) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* On a column whose `text` index has a postprocessor, `hasPhrase` rejoined the index tokens with a space before matching, so it returned wrong results unless that space matched how the tokenizer splits. Allowing `hasPhrase` to accept an `Array(String)` removes joining tokens completely. [#120859](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120859) ([Elmi Ahmadov](https://github.com/ahmadov)).
* Fixed a `text` index with `tokenizer = 'japanese'` returning fewer rows than a full scan: over a `LowCardinality` column every second row of a run of equal values was not indexed, and two search terms of the same byte length in one query (for example `hasAnyTokens(s, 'エンジン', 'japanese') AND hasAnyTokens(s, '形態解析', 'japanese')`) matched no rows. Indexes built before this fix keep the missing rows until `ALTER TABLE ... MATERIALIZE INDEX` is run. [#123389](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123389) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a query failing with an exception like `Cannot parse UInt64 from String` on a table with a text index on `mapKeys` or `JSONAllPaths`, when the predicate cannot be evaluated on the value of a missing map key or JSON path. [#124612](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124612) ([Anton Popov](https://github.com/CurtizJ)).
* Fixed `count()` with a text index search function such as `hasToken`, `hasAnyTokens`, or `hasAllTokens` in `WHERE` and a constant `GROUP BY` key returning a row with `0` instead of no rows when nothing matches. [#124593](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124593) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `hasAnyTokens` with a `String` needle on a text index with the `sparseGrams` tokenizer returning fewer rows than the equivalent `Array` needle. Grams covered by a longer gram of the needle were dropped, which is only valid for `hasAllTokens`. [#124613](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124613) ([Jimmy Aguilar Mena](https://github.com/Ergus)).
* Fixed slow planning of queries with a text index on tables with many parts when other skip indices are stored in the packed `skp_idx.packed` archive. Checking for an absent optional text-index file no longer calls `stat` on disk for every part. [#125381](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125381) ([Anton Popov](https://github.com/CurtizJ)).
* Fix `ALTER TABLE ... MATERIALIZE STATISTICS` / `DROP STATISTICS` failing forever and wedging the table's mutation queue when a server profile sets `allow_statistics = 0` and the statement enables it per-session, the same opt-in `ADD STATISTICS` honours. Also close a hole where `MATERIALIZE STATISTICS` ignored `allow_statistics = 0` altogether under `validate_mutation_query = 0`. [#117351](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117351) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a mutation silently dropping one of two swapped columns' statistics when the part carried a composed `RENAME COLUMN` pair that exchanged their names. [#118977](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118977) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* When the query cache is unable to store results (with `enable_writes_to_query_cache = 0`, in `clickhouse-local`, or with a zero limit in the `query_cache` server configuration), queries with `use_query_cache = 1` no longer fail with `QUERY_CACHE_USED_WITH_NONDETERMINISTIC_FUNCTIONS`, `QUERY_CACHE_USED_WITH_SYSTEM_TABLE` or `QUERY_CACHE_USED_WITH_NON_THROW_OVERFLOW_MODE`: these checks protect against caching wrong results, which cannot happen then. When a `query_cache` limit is reconfigured to 0, the existing entries are now dropped instead of lingering. [#123604](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123604) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* `LIMIT [n] AFTER start_expr UNTIL end_expr` now checks `end_expr` only after `start_expr` first matches. Previously a match of `end_expr` anywhere before the first `start_expr` incorrectly made the result empty. [#120514](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120514) ([Nihal Z. Miaji](https://github.com/nihalzp)).
* Fixed `SELECT DISTINCT` and `LIMIT BY` over a floating point column returning a different number of rows depending on whether the in-order variant of the operator was used: `-0.0` and `0.0`, and distinct `NaN` payloads, are no longer merged by the in-order variant. [#118198](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118198) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a higher-order function with a non-deterministic lambda body, such as `arrayMap(i -> rand64(i), ...)`, being evaluated once per source row instead of once per output row after `ARRAY JOIN` when lazy columns replication is enabled. [#120796](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120796) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Fixed a performance regression where a query whose `WHERE` requires the same expression to equal two different constants stopped being recognized as always false when `compatibility` was set below `26.7`, or when `optimize_redundant_comparisons` was disabled explicitly. Closes [#121007](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121007). Closes [#121012](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121012). [#121065](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121065) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_AN_AGGREGATE` being thrown for a `GROUP BY` key referenced from a window function when `group_by_use_nulls` is enabled together with `ROLLUP`, `CUBE`, or `GROUPING SETS`, as in `SELECT rank() OVER (PARTITION BY k) FROM t GROUP BY k WITH ROLLUP`. [#118173](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118173) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `AMBIGUOUS_IDENTIFIER` for a column qualified with the alias of a table expression when another table of the same query is named like that alias. Such a qualifier now refers to the alias, as it does in the old analyzer, both in a join that reported the ambiguity and in a two-table join that resolved it to the other side. [#119082](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119082) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when a query tree pass rewrites a window function's aggregate in place. A query whose only reference to a `Nullable` column was a windowed `count`, such as `SELECT count(n) OVER () FROM t`, was rejected at default settings because `optimize_functions_to_subcolumns` rewrote it into `sum(not(n.null))` and lost the node's window kind. `optimize_uniq_to_count` corrupted `uniq(n) OVER ()` over a `SELECT DISTINCT` subquery the same way. Closes [#119635](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/119635). [#119681](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119681) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a wrong result from `optimize_injective_functions_in_group_by` on the two-argument form `toString(x, timezone)`. `GROUP BY toString(number, NULL)` could be rewritten to `GROUP BY number` and produce one group per row instead of one. [#117197](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117197) ([Ilya Yatsishin](https://github.com/qoega)).
* Fixed a server crash in `CREATE TABLE ... AS <table function>` when an argument of the table function is a scalar subquery with a `JOIN` and `enable_join_runtime_filters_index_analysis` is enabled in the default settings profile. [#122795](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122795) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed table names not being qualified with the database when a `WITH` name declared elsewhere in the same query happened to match them: a view whose `UNION` branch referenced a table with the name of a `WITH RECURSIVE` CTE of another branch could not be read from another database, and `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` with a common table expression referenced from a subquery failed with `UNKNOWN_DATABASE` or read a table of the session database. [#114908](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114908) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `IN` against a table-backed set (`ENGINE = Set`), or with `transform_null_in = 1`, throwing `CANNOT_CONVERT_TYPE` for probe values outside the set key type's range instead of evaluating them to false, which also made the query succeed or fail depending on how much the plan pruned. [#117606](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117606) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a crash and wrong results with `convert_query_to_cnf = 1` when `WHERE` or `HAVING` references the same `SELECT` alias more than once. [#123414](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123414) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results, `Unexpected return type from tupleElement` errors and `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` errors when a `Nested` column is read through `ARRAY JOIN` with a numeric `tupleElement` index (for example `tupleElement(n, 3)`) while another subcolumn is unused, and the same expression is reused by `ORDER BY ALL`, `GROUP BY ALL` or an alias. [#123647](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123647) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` in the analyzer when a table is referenced as `database.table` in `FROM` or `JOIN` and another table expression in the same query uses that database name as its alias, for example `FROM db.t1 AS db INNER JOIN db.t2 AS t ON ...`. Closes [#122239](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122239). [#124262](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124262) ([Dmitry Novik](https://github.com/novikd)).
* Fix a wrong result, a leaked totals row, and a `LOGICAL_ERROR` exception with `rewrite_in_to_join` when the subquery of `IN` uses `WITH TOTALS`. [#124096](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124096) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `Unexpected return type from __topKFilter` logical error raised by `ORDER BY ... LIMIT` over a `MergeTree` table with more than one part when the sort column contains a `Nullable` element, for example `Tuple(UInt64, Nullable(UInt64))`. `use_top_k_dynamic_filtering` is enabled by default, so no opt-in setting was required to hit this. [#113406](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113406) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reject a duplicate column alias name in a CTE or table expression, which previously could return an incorrect result instead of failing. Closes [#114427](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/114427). [#114505](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114505) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Fixed `APPLY lambda(tuple(x), ...)` followed by an operator, as in `SELECT * APPLY lambda(tuple(x), toString(x)) > '0'`, which failed with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER`. A view using `APPLY (lambda(...))` followed by an operator also stopped working after a server restart. [#124142](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124142) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a logical error `Equal values are not contiguous within the range assumed to be sorted` (and potential wrong `DISTINCT` results) when `optimize_distinct_in_order` is enabled and an `ORDER BY` over an `arrayJoin`-derived column is followed by another `ARRAY JOIN` that reproduces a column of the same name. [#110778](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110778) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results and a `LOGICAL_ERROR` (`Cannot capture column ... because it has incompatible type`) when a matcher such as `b.*`, `*`, `COLUMNS('...')`, or `b.* EXCEPT c` appears inside a lambda body. A matcher expanded inside a lambda kept its pre-`JOIN` column types, so `join_use_nulls = 1` did not turn columns from the `NULL` side of a `LEFT`/`FULL`/`RIGHT JOIN` into `Nullable` (unmatched rows produced a value computed over defaults instead of `NULL`), and `group_by_use_nulls = 1` with `WITH ROLLUP` / `WITH CUBE` / `GROUP BY GROUPING SETS` failed with `NOT_AN_AGGREGATE`. A matcher expanded inside a lambda now carries the same types it would have if the columns were written out explicitly, and a lambda inside `PREWHERE` is no longer promoted to `Nullable` by `join_use_nulls`. Also fixed wrong results for a matcher inside a lambda when `analyzer_compatibility_join_using_top_level_identifier = 1` makes a `JOIN ... USING` key resolve to a `SELECT` projection alias: the matcher selected the merged `USING` key instead of the right table's column. [#111993](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111993) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results and `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for three-argument `multiIf` and `if`-chain `GROUP BY` keys with `ROLLUP`, `CUBE`, or `GROUPING SETS` and `group_by_use_nulls = 1`. Also fixed constant-condition keys reused through aliases with `enable_identifier_resolve_cache`, lost grouping sets when a key collapses to another key, and a `Bad cast` exception for a correlated subquery reading that column. Closes [#122404](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122404). Part of [#103419](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/103419). [#123065](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123065) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#122512](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122512) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of `ORDER BY`, `LIMIT BY`, and `GROUP BY` with `optimize_aggregation_in_order` over a function of the sorting key with a `NULL` constant argument, such as `toString(dt, NULL)` or `toString(dt, {tz:Nullable(String)})` with a `NULL` parameter. Such a function returns `NULL` for every row, but reading in order treated it as strictly monotonic and kept the next sorting key column in table order. [#125042](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125042) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server crash, wrong results, or a rejected query in `ORDER BY ... WITH FILL ... INTERPOLATE` when one `INTERPOLATE` expression has to be converted to its target type and another item reads the same column or literal, for example `INTERPOLATE (a AS b, b AS a)` with targets of different types. [#125129](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125129) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A `WITH` list whose elements refer to the preceding ones, or a chain of common table expressions that read the previous one more than once, now fails with `TOO_BIG_AST` past `max_expanded_ast_elements` instead of exhausting the server's memory in `EXPLAIN AST optimize = 1`, `CREATE VIEW`, a full `ATTACH VIEW ... AS SELECT` and `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY QUERY`. [#122362](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122362) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix excessive memory usage for queries with `ARRAY JOIN` and filters on both rows and array elements: the row filter is now applied before expanding the array. [#125577](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125577) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Fixed `ORDER BY ... LIMIT` over a column of an `Interval` type (or `Nullable`, `LowCardinality`, `Tuple` or `Array` of one) failing with `TYPE_MISMATCH` when `use_top_k_dynamic_filtering` is enabled, which is the default. Closes [#125197](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125197). [#125221](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125221) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a heap-use-after-free when a correlated subquery reading a `Merge` table was planned inside `EXPLAIN`, a subquery in `FROM`, a view, or an `IN` subquery. [#122475](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122475) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a correlated scalar subquery used as the whole `WHERE`, `HAVING`, or `QUALIFY` condition, e.g. `WHERE (SELECT count() > 0 FROM t AS i WHERE i.k = o.k)`, being rejected with `ILLEGAL_AGGREGATION` or `NOT_AN_AGGREGATE`, or failing with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` (`NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` on older versions) when the outer column it uses is not read elsewhere in the query. Closes [#124499](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124499). [#124594](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124594) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` (`None of the algorithms enabled by the 'join_algorithm' setting ... can execute this SEMI/ANTI JOIN`) for a correlated `EXISTS`/`NOT EXISTS` subquery, for an `IN (subquery)` under `rewrite_in_to_join`, or for an `ANY LEFT JOIN` of an `ENGINE = Join` table under an always-false `WHERE`, when `join_algorithm` is set to `full_sorting_merge` or `partial_merge`. [#118315](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118315) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Comparing two deeply nested `Array`, `Tuple`, `Map`, or `Object` values doubled the work for every nesting level, because the ordering asked each equal element both whether it was less and whether it was greater. A single-value aggregate such as `min`, `max`, `argMin`, `groupArraySorted`, or an `-ArgMin` combinator over a 30-level nested argument therefore ran for minutes on a few hundred rows, and could not be interrupted by `max_execution_time`. Such a comparison now visits each element once. [#116455](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116455) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server double free when finalizing an aggregate function with the `-State` combinator nested under a combinator that transfers several substates in one call, such as `-Resample`, `-ForEach`, `-Map`, or `-Tuple`, if an exception is thrown part-way through the transfer. [#111287](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111287) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results from `quantileExactWeightedInterpolated`, `quantileInterpolatedWeighted`, and their `quantiles*` forms over integer-backed types. [#114571](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114571) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `topK`, `topKWeighted`, `approx_top_k`, `approx_top_count`, `approx_top_sum`, `uniqUpTo`, `uniqCombined`, `uniqCombined64`, `exponentialMovingAverage`, `stochasticLinearRegression`, `stochasticLogisticRegression`, and `groupNumericIndexedVector` state columns with a `Decimal` or wide-integer parameter. The parameter was persisted as a quoted string that reparsed as `String`, so after a restart the table was unreadable, uninsertable, and undroppable. [#121491](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121491) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server abort when the `flameGraph` aggregate function was constructed on a thread without a query context, for example while attaching a stored table whose column type names `AggregateFunction(flameGraph, ...)`. [#121894](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121894) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `runningAccumulate` returning an accumulation computed over only part of the rows when it reads a state column under `if`, `multiIf`, `and`, or `or` at the default `short_circuit_function_evaluation = 'enable'`. Inside a boolean predicate this also changed which rows passed the filter. An argument computed inline by another lazily evaluated function, whether it is the state or the grouping key, is still filtered. Closes [#117121](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/117121). [#117126](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117126) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `arrayIntersect` returning a `NULL` that is present only in its first argument, which made the result depend on the order of the arguments. `NULL` is now in the result only when every argument array contains one. [#118433](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118433) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `arrayUniq`, `arrayEnumerateUniq`, `arrayEnumerateDense`, and `arrayJaccardIndex` now distinguish `NULL` from the nested type's default value for nullable arrays that use generic hashing. [#120676](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120676) ([xiaohuanlin](https://github.com/xiaohuanlin)).
* Fixed an out-of-bounds heap write in `stem`. The output buffer now grows when the stemmed word is longer than the estimate from the input. [#121402](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121402) ([Jimmy Aguilar Mena](https://github.com/Ergus)).
* Fix array and map search functions returning a match for a `NULL` `String` needle when an empty string is present. [#118741](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118741) ([Minh Vu](https://github.com/fallintoplace)).
* Fixed `least` and `greatest` returning wrapped-around values for a `LowCardinality` numeric argument of mixed signedness, `intDiv`/`%`/`positiveModulo` throwing `Division by zero` on NULL rows of a `LowCardinality(Nullable(...))` denominator, and `fromModifiedJulianDay`, `runningConcurrency`, `hasPhrase`, `formatRow`, `now`, and `CAST` rejecting valid `LowCardinality` arguments. [#117450](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117450) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)). [#122418](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122418) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `has`, `notHas`, `indexOf`, `countEqual`, and `mapContains` silently finding nothing in a constant array or map with `Enum` keys searched for the name of an enum value, e.g. `mapContains(map('a', 1)::Map(Enum8('a' = 1), UInt8), 'a')` returned `0`. They now compare by the name of the enum value, consistently with `equals` and with the same functions over a non-constant argument. Also fix indexing a `Map` with `Enum` keys by a numeric value that is not in the `Enum` throwing `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_OF_ENUM` during query analysis instead of returning the default value, and an exception in the analysis of a `bloom_filter` index over `mapKeys` of a `Map` with `Enum` keys. Also fix a `bloom_filter` index over an `Array(String)` or `Array(FixedString)` column wrongly pruning the granules that match `hasAny` or `hasAll` with a constant `Array(Enum)` argument. [#116093](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116093) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed comparison of tuples with an untyped `NULL` element, such as `(1, 2) != (3, NULL)` or `(1, 2) != toNullable((3, NULL))`: the result was `NULL` (or `0` for a `Nullable` tuple) even when another element decides it. It is now the same as with a typed `NULL`: `0` or `1` when another element decides the comparison, `NULL` otherwise. A filter and its negation no longer both drop a row, e.g. with `WHERE NOT (_partition_value > toNullable((..., NULL)))`. [#123896](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123896) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `pointInPolygon` with a constant polygon returning wrong results for some points of ordinary polygons, for example `0` for the point `(0.76, 0.01)` inside the polygon `[(1, 0), (0, 1), (-1, 0), (0, -1)]`, or `1` for a point inside a small hole. The results now match those for a non-constant polygon. [#123767](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123767) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Arithmetic between a `Decimal` and an integer that does not fit the native width of the decimal result now raises `DECIMAL_OVERFLOW` instead of silently wrapping the integer operand. [#118721](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118721) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed wrong results of `pointInPolygon` for polygons with huge coordinates. With a constant array polygon, a filter on a table whose primary key covers the point columns could also return fewer rows than a full scan. `pointInPolygon` now throws `BAD_ARGUMENTS` where it would compute with a polygon coordinate larger than `1e150` in absolute value. [#123740](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123740) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `date_time_overflow_behavior` being ignored while parsing `Date` and `DateTime`. Closes [#113151](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/113151). [#116099](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116099) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* `toStartOfInterval` no longer widens `Date` and `DateTime` inputs to `Date32` and `DateTime64` when `enable_extended_results_for_datetime_functions = 1`; only `Date32` and `DateTime64` inputs keep the extended result types, matching the `toStartOfX` functions. This also removes the saturated result (`2106-02-07 06:28:15`) that `toStartOfInterval(<Date>, INTERVAL ... DAY)` returned under that setting for dates after `2106-02-07`. [#119806](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119806) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#121809](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121809) ([Kirill Kopnev](https://github.com/Fgrtue)).
* Fixed `age('week', ...)` sometimes being off by one week when the dates fall on different weekdays. [#122633](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122633) ([Samuel Krempaský](https://github.com/NIKTONIKTO717)).
* Fixed wrong results when an `Enum8` or `Enum16` value is converted to `DateTime` or `Date`. A negative member wrapped instead of saturating like the `Int8` or `Int16` it is stored as, so `toDateTime` of an enum returned a far-future timestamp and primary-key analysis discarded granules that did contain matching rows. [#118039](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118039) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `toDateTime64`, `toDateTime` with a scale and `toTime64` throwing `CANNOT_PARSE_DATETIME` for a `Nullable(String)` or `Nullable(FixedString)` argument that contains `NULL`; such rows now return `NULL`. For `toDateTime64` this was a regression in 26.5, where `cast_string_to_date_time_mode` became `best_effort` by default. [#123311](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123311) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `dateDiff` and `age` with unit `day` returning a result off by 65536 (for example `-65535` instead of `1`) when an argument is a `DateTime` on the local day 1969-12-31 in a time zone west of UTC, such as `toDateTime(0)` in `America/New_York`. If a table stores such a value in a sorting key, partition key, skip index, projection or `MATERIALIZED`/`DEFAULT` column, rebuild it. [#124555](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124555) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Setting `date_time_overflow_behavior` is now honored by `CAST` and the conversion functions for numeric conversions to `Date`, `Date32`, `DateTime`, `Time`, `DateTime64`, and `Time64`. For `DateTime64` and `Time64`, the same now applies to the `INSERT ... VALUES` expression fallback and the `values` table function, and for `DateTime64` sort keys to numeric `WITH FILL` bounds. Previously an out-of-range value was silently clamped even in the `throw` mode. `accurateCast`, `accurateCastOrNull`, and `accurateCastOrDefault` no longer clamp an unrepresentable value into `DateTime64` or `Time64`, and saturation now reaches the extreme representable tick of the target rather than the last whole second. [#117667](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117667) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a constant of `Variant` type reached through an `Array`, `Tuple`, or `Map` being mis-serialized to a secondary server, so a query that returned a result on a single node failed with `CANNOT_CONVERT_TYPE` through parallel replicas, `remote`, or `Distributed`. A `String` member of a multi-member `Variant` constant, scalar or nested, arrived there as whichever member its text parsed as, and an empty `Map` whose key or value is a `Variant` was rejected outright. [#117831](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117831) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `LOGICAL_ERROR` when reading a `sizeN` subcolumn of a shared-data `JSON` path, for example a `size1` subcolumn over data of type `Array(Array(Nullable(Int64)))`. It affected parts written with the `advanced` shared data serialization version, which is the default for merged parts. [#120039](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120039) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Fixed wrong results when reading the `null` subcolumn of a `Nullable` field of a `Variant`, `Dynamic`, or `JSON` element, for example ``value.`Tuple(a Nullable(UInt32), b String)`.a.null``. On rows holding a different alternative the subcolumn returned `0` although the field itself was `NULL`, so `optimize_functions_to_subcolumns` (enabled by default) rewriting `IS NULL` to that subcolumn silently dropped matching rows. Closes [#119935](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/119935). [#120099](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120099) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `JSONHas` and `JSONExtractRaw` returning an absent result for a typed `JSON` path whose literal value is `NULL` but which contains a non-empty sub-object when `type_json_skip_null_typed_paths` is enabled. [#120321](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120321) ([JasonLi-cn](https://github.com/JasonLi-cn)).
* Fixed `max_memory_usage` and `max_server_memory_usage` not being respected when reading a `JSON` column with the `FLATTENED` serialization version from the `Native` format. [#117730](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117730) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Fixed reading flattened `Nested` members next to a `JSON` member in `Wide` parts. [#122519](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122519) ([Mohammed Sohail](https://github.com/msohail22)).
* Allow the `JSON` type and the `JSON*` functions to parse documents whose `String` values hold bytes that are not valid UTF-8, which previously failed with `INCORRECT_DATA`. As a side effect, `isValidJSON` and the `JSONExtract*` family now accept such documents instead of reporting them invalid. [#119122](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119122) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix stack exhaustion when reading a deeply nested `Dynamic` or `JSON` value as a `Field` or printing it in a text format. Both paths now raise `TOO_DEEP_RECURSION` instead of terminating the server. [#121441](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121441) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix reading bucketed runtime `Map` values from `JSON` columns using `advanced` shared-data serialization. [#118577](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118577) ([Rory Shanks](https://github.com/rorylshanks)).
* Fixed a `Dynamic` constant losing the type of its value when a query is sent to other servers (`Distributed`, `remote`, parallel replicas): a constant holding `Enum8('7' = 3)` arrived as the number `3`, so `IN` and `NOT IN` matched the wrong rows and `=` failed with `NO_COMMON_TYPE`, and a `UInt64`, `IPv4`, or `Map` value arrived as a different type. Closes [#119745](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/119745). [#118988](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118988) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a crash on a query with a deeply nested `Dynamic` or `JSON` constant. [#120885](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120885) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Fix inconsistent results and missing type errors when `optimize_functions_to_subcolumns` rewrites a `JSON` path accessed with `tupleElement` or `arrayElement` using an unsupported key type. Such expressions now behave the same with and without the optimization. [#125471](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125471) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `TYPE_MISMATCH` when a `LowCardinality` join key is also selected from the NULL-extended side of a `LEFT`, `FULL`, or `ASOF LEFT` join with `join_use_nulls = 1` and `join_algorithm = 'full_sorting_merge'` or `'parallel_full_sorting_merge'`. Closes [#120424](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120424). [#120469](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120469) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ILLEGAL_PREWHERE` on sharded `GLOBAL RIGHT JOIN` queries that used `PREWHERE` on the left table. The filter is applied to the broadcast side before the join instead of being sent to a `Memory` temporary table that does not support `PREWHERE`. `PREWHERE` on a remote table in a join is also preserved when that table is read through a subquery wrap. [#119780](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119780) ([Anton Ivashkin](https://github.com/ianton-ru)).
* Fixed a `Pipeline stuck` exception when a query combined a merge join using `join_algorithm = 'parallel_full_sorting_merge'` with a step that glues independent streams together, such as reading from a `Merge` table, or a `UNION ALL` narrowed by `max_streams_for_union_step`. Such a query now runs instead of failing. [#121227](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121227) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a query failing with an error such as `CANNOT_READ_ARRAY_FROM_TEXT` when a subquery's `SELECT` list computes an expression that the same subquery's `WHERE` guards against, and that subquery is the probe side of a `JOIN` for which a runtime join filter is built (`enable_join_runtime_filters = 1`, enabled by default). The guarded expression was evaluated on rows that the `WHERE` had already removed. [#122143](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122143) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed lost matches on a `Dynamic` join key when outer joins are reordered with a join order conflict detector enabled (`query_plan_optimize_join_order_conflict_detector`) and `join_use_nulls = 1`. [#123113](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123113) ([Fisnik Kastrati](https://github.com/fkastrati)).
* Fix `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` error for a `LEFT JOIN` over a dictionary or other key-value storage under `join_algorithm = 'direct'`. [#123491](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123491) ([Hechem Selmi](https://github.com/m-selmi)).
* Fix exponential planning time in the transitive outer-to-inner JOIN conversion when the expression DAG has shared nodes, such as a column used twice (x + x) across nested subqueries. The analysis now visits each node once instead of every path. [#123509](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123509) ([Hechem Selmi](https://github.com/m-selmi)).
* Fix a performance regression where the join runtime filter was skipped for joins that combine an `=` key with a null-safe `<=>` (`IS NOT DISTINCT FROM`) key once hash table statistics were collected. [#122618](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122618) ([Manuel](https://github.com/raimannma)).
* Fixed `indexHint` over the non-preserved side of an OUTER join silently returning extra rows, and `join_algorithm = 'direct'` failing with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` when a column-free conjunct such as `materialize(1)` is added to the `WHERE` clause. [#117569](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117569) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` for an outer `JOIN` with `join_algorithm = 'full_sorting_merge'` when `optimize_and_compare_chain` derived a condition on the preserved side of the join from the `ON` clause. [#124233](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124233) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Fix `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` errors in `INSERT ... SELECT` from a parameterized view when `parallel_distributed_insert_select` is enabled. The view is now analyzed with the analyzer instead of the legacy interpreter. [#119495](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119495) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Fixed a query over a `Distributed` table (or `cluster`/`remote`/`clusterAllReplicas` with an inner `view`) spanning more than one shard that references no column of the table anywhere in the query, such as `SELECT count(*) OVER () FROM distributed_table`. Such a query silently returned no rows, and returned `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Invalid number of columns in chunk pushed to OutputPort` when the table produced a constant. The fix covers the analyzer; `enable_analyzer = 0` is fixed separately by [#113955](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/113955). [#113469](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113469) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server crash when a scalar or `IN` subquery inside a standalone expression, such as a `CHECK` constraint, a `TTL` expression, or a row policy, reads a `Distributed` table or the `remote` table function. [#121418](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121418) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#121639](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121639) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for `grouping` over a `Distributed` table with `optimize_skip_unused_shards = 1`: a constant that a query tree pass builds is now named on a shard exactly as the initiator names it. [#119273](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119273) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Refuse a query with `ORDER BY` over a `Distributed` table whose declared column type differs from the shard table's type in a way that does not preserve the order, for example `Int8` declared over a `String` column. The shards sorted by their own type and the initiator merged the streams as if they were sorted by the declared type, which returned rows in the wrong order, cut the wrong rows with `LIMIT`, and duplicated values under `DISTINCT`. The check runs when a shard is the local server, where the shard table's types are known, and only for the columns the query sorts by. Exact widenings (`Float32` to `Float64`, `Date` to `Date32`, `DateTime` to `DateTime64`, a `Decimal` with no smaller scale and no fewer integer digits, `FixedString` to `String`) are now recognized as order-preserving by both this check and the one of the `Merge` engine. [#118445](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118445) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed queries over a `Distributed` table when `optimize_skip_unused_shards` skips every shard and the query uses `distributed_group_by_no_merge` or reads the table through another `Distributed` table. Such queries failed with `NUMBER_OF_COLUMNS_DOESNT_MATCH`, `CANNOT_CONVERT_TYPE`, or `std::out_of_range` (with `ALIAS` columns) instead of returning an empty result or the aggregate of an empty set. [#122982](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122982) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed queries that go through another server (`remote`, `Distributed`, parallel replicas, `GLOBAL IN`, and `QueryRunner` jobs sent to a cluster) failing with `SYNTAX_ERROR`, `UNKNOWN_CODEC`, or `CORRUPTED_DATA` when `output_format_native_encode_types_in_binary_format` or `input_format_native_decode_types_in_binary_format` is enabled. The blocks of these queries now carry type names on the wire, and a shard with this change also works with an initiator without it. `clickhouse-client --query_kind secondary_query` follows the same rule. [#123649](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123649) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#124716](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124716) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` (or `THERE_IS_NO_COLUMN`) when a query sent to other servers (a `Distributed` table, `remote`, parallel replicas) has an `IN` over a parameterized view called without its database name inside an expression the servers return, for example `countIf(x IN (SELECT y FROM pv(p = 1)))`, a `GROUP BY` key, or a `SELECT` item. Also fixed `NOT_AN_AGGREGATE` when one query spells the same parameterized view call both with and without its database name. [#123662](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123662) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when reading a subcolumn (`Array` `.size0`, a `Tuple` element, `Map` `.keys`/`.values`, `Nullable` `.null`, a `JSON` path, or a `Dynamic` typed subcolumn) through a `VIEW` over a `Distributed` table. [#119293](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119293) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed wrong results of aggregations, `LIMIT`, `DISTINCT`, `LIMIT BY` and window functions over a `Buffer` table whose destination is a `Distributed` table that a single shard finishes (one shard, `distributed_group_by_no_merge`, or a `GROUP BY` by the sharding key): the rows still in the buffer and the rows of the destination were processed separately and both results were returned. [#124981](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124981) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix wrong results when a condition outside a distributed subquery with `QUALIFY` was pushed into it and evaluated before the window functions `QUALIFY` filters by. [#125491](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125491) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Fixed `THERE_IS_NO_COLUMN` and `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` errors in queries over `remote` or with parallel replicas that compare tuples where one pair of elements is constant and false, such as `(n, 1) = (n, 2)`, or where `NULL` is compared with a column, such as `(n, s) = (NULL, s)`. [#125195](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125195) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results with `parallel_replicas_plan_based = 1` when the plan fragment sent to the replicas has no output columns, for example `SELECT count() FROM (SELECT 1 FROM t LIMIT 500)`. Such a query returned `0`, or an empty result set, instead of the correct value. [#115871](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115871) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results and a `LOGICAL_ERROR` when a query reads with parallel replicas inside a distributed sub-query whose `cluster_for_parallel_replicas` numbers its shards differently from the cluster the query is distributed over: a `VIEW` carrying its own `SETTINGS cluster_for_parallel_replicas`, a read through `clusterAllReplicas` of that same cluster, a cluster built by cluster discovery or by a `Replicated` database when the two servers see a different set of shards under one name, or a plain `remote_servers` cluster whose per-server copies number the shards differently while a configuration change rolls out. The shard number shipped by the initiator was applied to the other numbering and selected the wrong shard. Parallel replicas are now declined for such a read. [#113589](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113589) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#124724](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124724) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Fixed `SELECT count()` returning a value multiplied by the number of replicas when executed inside a transaction with parallel replicas enabled. Trivial count optimization now is not rejected inside a transaction, so it is executed solely on the initiator. Followers no longer apply the trivial count optimization during query planning, which previously caused each of them to independently answer with the total row count read from the table's metadata whenever the query reached them. [#116365](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116365) ([Kirill Shokhin](https://github.com/kirillshokhin)).
* Fixed `max_bytes_to_read`, `max_bytes_to_read_leaf`, and read-speed and timeout limits being ignored for reads executed from a query plan shipped to another node, which happens with `parallel_replicas_plan_based` and `make_distributed_plan`. [#119958](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119958) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Fixed `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Cannot find a matching set in the map of sets from single-replica plan`, and silently lost parallel replicas, when automatic parallel replicas were enabled and an `IN` subquery carried its own `SETTINGS` clause naming `force_primary_key` or `automatic_parallel_replicas_mode`. [#120799](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120799) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `UNKNOWN_FUNCTION` on a shard or a replica for a higher-order function whose lambda body is an `AND` of comparisons when `serialize_query_plan = 1` or `parallel_replicas_plan_based = 1`: a JIT-compiled lambda body was serialized into the plan. [#119332](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119332) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix missing total rows in progress (and therefore a non-working progress bar) for queries with parallel replicas when `parallel_replicas_local_plan` is enabled (the default). [#123928](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123928) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed duplicate filter push-down in plan-based parallel replicas. [#117731](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117731) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Fixed wrong results and `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` errors when a query reads a `MergeTree` table after reading one of its projections with the `mergeTreeProjection` table function, for example in a `JOIN` or `UNION ALL` of the two. With parallel replicas such a query could also fail with `Got read request from replica N for unknown stream`. Closes [#102182](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/102182). [#123915](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123915) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `FINAL` on table functions now disables parallel replicas the same way as `FINAL` on tables, so a `TimeSeries` table function such as `timeSeriesTags` no longer returns duplicate rows under parallel replicas. [#122492](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122492) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed parallel replicas not being used when reading a `Distributed` table whose underlying table is another `Distributed` table and every shard of the outer cluster has a single replica. [#123566](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123566) ([Igor Nikonov](https://github.com/devcrafter)).
* Fixed `TOO_MANY_ROWS` being raised for a query that stays within its `max_rows_to_read` budget when `parallel_replicas_min_number_of_rows_per_replica` is set. The estimate sizing the replica set enforced the limit against whole granules at planning time. [#121269](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121269) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` exception for `JOIN` queries with `IN` subqueries when using plan-based parallel replicas. [#121508](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121508) ([Manuel](https://github.com/raimannma)).
* Parallel replicas now honor a `SETTINGS` clause that changes table eligibility when it is written inside a subquery or a view body. Such a clause was ignored, so a query over a view whose body sets `parallel_replicas_for_non_replicated_merge_tree = 0`, or sets `parallel_replicas_allow_view_over_mergetree = 0` and reads a further view, returned `max_parallel_replicas` times too many rows, and a `UNION` over a view overriding `parallel_replicas_allow_view_over_mergetree` per subquery could throw `Can't determine table for parallel replicas`. [#110020](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110020) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A query that reads a `MATERIALIZED` CTE more than once, for example inside an `IN` subquery, now works with parallel replicas when the initiator also reads locally (`parallel_replicas_local_plan = 1`). The other replicas no longer fail with `UNKNOWN_TABLE` on the CTE's temporary table. Closes [#112642](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/112642). [#123121](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123121) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `LOGICAL_ERROR` "Block structure mismatch in QueryPipeline stream" when selecting a computed column through the `merge` table function or the `Merge` engine. If constant folding folded that column for one matched table and not another, the query failed. [#115971](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115971) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the exception `No set is registered for key` for a `SELECT` that reads a `Distributed` table through a `Merge` table or the `merge` table function and whose `GROUP BY`, `DISTINCT`, or `LIMIT BY` key contains an `IN`-family function. [#117817](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117817) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix wrong results when reading a subcolumn of a `Merge` table column whose type differs between the source tables. Such a subcolumn exists only in the common type `Merge` derives, so it was filled with the default value for every row instead of being read. Most visibly, `variantElement` over a `Merge` table returned `NULL` for every row unless the call sat in the top-level projection next to another use of the whole column. [#121462](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121462) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a server crash when a `SELECT` with `serialize_query_plan = 1` read a `Merge` table through a table that forwards its reads, such as `Alias`, `Buffer` with a destination, or a `MATERIALIZED VIEW` with a `TO` target. Closes [#119899](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/119899). [#119387](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119387) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results when a `Merge` table (or the `merge` table function) reads a subcolumn of a column that one of its child tables does not have: for that child, `x.null` of a missing `Nullable` column `x` was read as 0, so `x IS NULL` returned no rows from it. Element subcolumns of `Tuple`, `Variant` and `JSON` columns and the sizes of a missing flattened `Nested` member were affected the same way. Closes [#122523](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122523). [#123633](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123633) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `Merge` table returning the value of another column, or failing with a `CAST` error, for a column that one of its child tables does not have, when that child has a row policy or an `ALIAS` column. Such a column is now filled with its default value. [#124674](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124674) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `merge` and `Merge` tables with a `Distributed` table among them returning the same `GROUP BY` group several times, each copy with a part of its aggregates, when the aggregation is two-level and a table lacks a grouping column or has it under another type than the common one. [#124720](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124720) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a use-after-free when a query reads through an `Alias` table whose target is dropped concurrently. [#120098](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120098) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW ... POPULATE AS SELECT count() FROM <MergeTree table>` counting rows inserted during the population twice when `materialized_views_populate_atomically` is enabled. [#123182](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123182) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Fixed a materialized view over a system log table such as `system.query_log` silently stopping to receive rows after the server renamed the table to `<name>_N` because its definition changed (a server upgrade, or a change of `<engine>` or `<ttl>` in the config). Closes [#124177](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124177). [#124217](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124217) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixes wrong results when a materialized view reads an ordinary view over a table with a row policy that contains a subquery (`x IN (SELECT ...)` or a scalar subquery). The policy's subquery now reads the real source table instead of the block being inserted into the materialized view. [#124875](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124875) ([Aditya Kumar](https://github.com/adityaksolves)).
* `SYSTEM WAIT VIEW` on a refreshable materialized view in a `Replicated` or `Shared` database no longer returns `Ok.` while a refresh requested on another replica has not run yet. Requested refreshes are now published in `Keeper`. [#120623](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120623) ([Samuel Krempaský](https://github.com/NIKTONIKTO717)).
* Fixed a segmentation fault when a refreshable materialized view is started up and shut down at the same time, for example when a failed `ATTACH DATABASE` rolls the database back, or when the server shuts down while tables are still loading asynchronously. [#121037](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121037) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a refreshable materialized view with `REFRESH DEPENDS ON` (or `REFRESH AFTER ... DEPENDS ON`) and no `REFRESH EVERY` re-running its refresh without limit after a failure used up `refresh_retries`. It now waits for its dependencies' next refresh. [#123369](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123369) ([Ashish Kumar Choubey](https://github.com/aschoube)).
* Fixed `plain_rewritable` disks leaving object storage and the in-memory filesystem in disagreement when a failed metadata transaction could not be reversed completely. Transient failures during reversal are now retried until object storage accepts the operation, and rollback runs for all exception types. Missing blobs are reported as `CORRUPTED_DATA`, and replacing a file with a missing source no longer deletes the existing target. The disagreement previously surfaced only after a restart, when a directory could appear under a name the server never committed. [#119235](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119235) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)). [#122696](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122696) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fixed a query reading from `file`, `s3`, `url`, `azure`, or `hdfs` with `session_timezone` set getting the `DateTime` schema inferred by an earlier session with a different `session_timezone`, which formatted and computed the values in that other session's time zone. [#117811](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117811) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed unnecessary object-storage reads, and `<Error>`-level exception messages such as `Code: 499 ... The specified key does not exist ... (S3_ERROR)`, while destroying a data part on an object-storage disk when its skip indices are packed into `skp_idx.packed`. Evicting the part's index marks from the mark cache no longer inspects the part's on-disk layout, so it performs no I/O. [#113454](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113454) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed hive partition columns of glob-path object storage tables being permanently disabled when the first query over the table ran while the path matched no files. [#120308](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120308) ([Zach Rippas](https://github.com/zatchet)).
* Fixed unbounded memory and CPU usage when a path passed to `file`, `s3`, `azureBlobStorage`, or `hdfs` contains many `{a,b,c}` glob groups or long `{N..M}` ranges. Such a path is now rejected with an error. [#118467](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118467) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `copyObjectToAnotherObjectStorage` copying an object twice when the source and destination are the same object storage, which doubled the data transferred by a copy between two disks backed by that storage. [#120953](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120953) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` for a `DEFAULT` column that depends on a column used by a row policy, in tables with the `File`, `URL`, `S3`, and other object-storage engines. [#122714](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122714) ([Nikolai Kochetov](https://github.com/KochetovNicolai)).
* Fix object storage tables with `partition_strategy = 'hive'` being silently empty for formats whose name is not the file extension real files carry. The read glob was built from the lowercased format name (`**.jsoneachrow`, `**.csvwithnames`), so pointing a `JSONEachRow` table at a lake of `.json` / `.jsonl` files — or a `CSVWithNames` table at `.csv` files — read zero rows with no error or warning. The glob is now built from every file extension registered for the format (and for its `WithNames`/`WithNamesAndTypes` base format), plus additionally registered read extensions: `JSONEachRow` matches `.json`/`.jsonl`/`.ndjson` files and `TabSeparated` matches `.tsv`. Files written by ClickHouse itself keep matching. [#120319](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120319) ([Zach Rippas](https://github.com/zatchet)).
* Fixed reads of Hive-partitioned files (`.../key=9/...`) that need no column of the data file, such as `count()` with `optimize_count_from_files = 0` or `SELECT key, _row_number`, returning no rows (`Native` threw an exception) when a partition column was smaller than the data columns. The wrong `0` was also cached and returned by later `count()` queries. The same reads of a structure that lists only partition columns returned no rows or threw an exception for most formats. Affects `file`, `url`, `s3` and other object storages. [#123381](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123381) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A query over an object storage table (e.g. `S3`) no longer waits for another query that is resolving the table's hive partitioning on its first use, so `KILL QUERY` and `max_execution_time` apply to it while the endpoint is slow or unreachable. [#123532](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123532) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `BAD_ARGUMENTS` (`Expected positional arguments to go before key-value arguments`) for a valid `S3` argument list that combines `headers(...)` with a key-value argument, in either order, and for the engines and table functions sharing that parser (`Iceberg`, `DeltaLake`, `Hudi`, `Paimon`, `GCS`, `S3Queue`). With `file_like_engine_default_partition_strategy = 'wildcard'` the server persisted such a list itself, so a created table could no longer be attached and the server refused to start; affected tables load again. [#119070](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119070) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix the logical error `Attempt to read beyond right offset` when the right bound of a read from Azure Blob Storage is lowered below bytes the reader has already buffered. [#120957](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120957) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a `WHERE` condition or a row policy on a Hive partition column (`use_hive_partitioning = 1`) returning too few rows when the `Parquet` data file also has a column with that name and other values: `Parquet` row-group, page, and dictionary pruning used the file's values instead of the path's. The same applied to a virtual column such as `_file` when the data file has a physical column with that name. Affects `file`, `url`, `s3`, and other object storages. [#124991](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124991) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed an exception when a `WHERE` condition on the `_file`, `_path`, or `_part` virtual column guards a branch with `if`, `and`, `or`, or `multiIf`, for example `WHERE _file LIKE 'events_%' AND toDate(substring(_file, 8, 10)) >= '2024-01-01'`. Pruning files and parts by virtual columns now respects `short_circuit_function_evaluation` instead of evaluating the excluded branch for every file or part name. The same path now also rebuilds copied `indexHint` expressions and reduced `and` expressions with the right arguments, avoiding argument-count exceptions during lazy evaluation. [#125033](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125033) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#125150](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125150) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#125126](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125126) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed loading, short `ATTACH`, and `UNDROP TABLE` for existing SQL-defined `S3` disks whose endpoints are absent from `remote_url_allow_hosts`; new full-definition `CREATE` and `ATTACH` still reject disallowed endpoints. [#124930](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124930) ([Alexey Bakharew](https://github.com/alexbakharew)).
* Fixed filesystem cache accounting when the directory for a new cache key cannot be created, for example because the cache disk is out of space or inodes. The failed reservation stayed counted in the cache until the query released the file segment. [#122211](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122211) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fixed `Parquet` writing size statistics twice for a column chunk that falls back from dictionary encoding, which made `unencoded_byte_array_data_bytes` and the level histograms too large. The `ParquetMetadata` format now also reports `size_statistics` for each column chunk. [#120027](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120027) ([Erez Rokah](https://github.com/erezrokah)).
* Fix two `JSON` object-size-limit bugs: bytes before the opening bracket are now counted toward the limit, and valid data is no longer rejected just because earlier rows in the same block already used the shared buffer. [#115795](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115795) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Schema inference for the `Freeform` format no longer exhausts memory on a wide row of strings: the search for the row structure is bounded by the setting `input_format_freeform_max_search_steps` (default `4096`, `0` means unlimited) and now also stops at `max_memory_usage`, `max_execution_time`, and `KILL QUERY`. [#122447](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122447) ([Mohammed Sohail](https://github.com/msohail22)). [#123238](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123238) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a crash when reading an ORC file whose root type is not a struct (for example, a file written with a `bigint` or `array<bigint>` root type). Such files are now rejected with an `INCORRECT_DATA` error. Closes [#123305](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123305). [#123337](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123337) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a possible out of bounds access when reading an ORC file. [#123464](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123464) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* Fix reading Avro files with a union whose named branches (records, enums, fixed) have the same structure. Before the fix, such files failed with `The number of (non-null) union types in Avro record (N) does not match the number of types in destination Variant type (M)`, even with the inferred schema. [#123236](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123236) ([Dmitry Kovalev](https://github.com/dk-github)).
* Avro schema parsing no longer overflows the stack on a deep chain of named types. [#124053](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124053) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a crash (stack overflow) on an Avro schema with a long chain of named type references, in schema inference (`DESC`, or `SELECT` without an explicit structure, for `Avro` and `AvroConfluent`), in reads into a `Variant` column and in Iceberg manifest reading. Such a schema now fails with an error instead. [#124644](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124644) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixes a crash when reading an ORC file whose metadata has fewer stripe statistics than the file has stripes (for example, after a corrupt `metadata_length`) with `input_format_orc_filter_push_down` enabled. The `ORC` input format now reads such files without stripe-level filtering. [#124315](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124315) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a crash (stack overflow) when reading an Avro file with an explicit structure that does not read a field nested through a long chain of named types, or a field of a recursive type holding deeply nested data such as a long linked list. Such a file now fails with `TOO_DEEP_RECURSION` instead. Closes [#124925](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124925). [#124964](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124964) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a stack overflow crash on a deeply nested `CapnProto` schema that the nesting depth check missed: a comment or a byte order mark between `import` and its path, an escape sequence in the import path, or a backtick text line. A backtick text line with many brackets is no longer rejected as nested too deeply. [#125487](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125487) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a crash (stack overflow) on a `CapnProto` schema with a long chain of type expression suffixes (`Int32.a.a...`, `Int32()()...`) or of declarations that refer to each other (constants, aliases, struct fields, annotations, imports). Such a schema is now rejected with `The CapnProto schema is nested too deeply`. Closes [#125782](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125782). [#125900](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125900) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a data race on the file list of a `File` engine table. Two concurrent `INSERT`s with `engine_file_allow_create_multiple_files = 1` could pick the same file name, which made `SELECT count()` over-report, rejected a valid `INSERT` with `CANNOT_APPEND_TO_FILE`, and could corrupt the heap. Closes [#121517](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121517). [#121598](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121598) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `count()` over `file('*.csv', 'CSVWithNames')` with `optimize_count_from_files = 1` over-counting a CSV file by one row in rare cases. [#120552](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120552) ([Knut-Aage-Hofseth](https://github.com/Knut-Aage-Hofseth)).
* Fixed concurrent `INSERT`s and `SELECT`s on a table with the `File` engine stalling for `lock_acquire_timeout` (120 seconds by default) and then failing with `TIMEOUT_EXCEEDED`. A single `SELECT` can open several readers on one `File` table, and an `INSERT` arriving between two of them made the query wait for itself. Closes [#115931](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/115931). [#121779](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121779) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
