* Fixed `TinyLog` returning fewer rows than the table contains when one of the data files being read ends before the others. An `Array`, `Map`, or `Nested` column contributes an elements file that holds one entry per element rather than per row, so it runs out early whenever the trailing rows hold empty containers, and a column holding only empty arrays contributes an elements file of zero bytes. The read stopped at the first data file by name to reach its end, cutting the result at a block boundary. [#120307](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120307) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `BACKUP` and `RESTORE` queries with `SETTINGS <name> = DEFAULT`: such a query either failed to parse or was formatted into text that could not be parsed back, and the other settings of the same clause were discarded. Repeated `async` items now use the last value, `async = '1'` no longer fails after the operation has started, and `compression` is rejected in the `SETTINGS` clause as in other queries. [#112978](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112978) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `BACKUP` writing a `.backup` manifest that is not well-formed XML when a value in it contained `&`, `<`, or `>`, for example `BACKUP ... SETTINGS id = 'a&b'`, or an incremental backup whose `base_backup` path contained one of those characters. The backup reported `BACKUP_CREATED`, but every later `RESTORE` from it failed with `SAXParseException: Invalid token`, leaving the data unreachable. Such values are now escaped. Backups already written with an unescaped value remain unreadable and have to be taken again. [#118898](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118898) ([Julia Kartseva](https://github.com/jkartseva)).
* Fixed `RESTORE` of `system.functions` failing, and aborting the whole restore, when the backup holds a SQL user-defined function whose definition the version that created it accepted but `CREATE FUNCTION` now rejects, for example `CREATE FUNCTION f AS lambda(identity(x), x)` created on 25.5 or earlier. [#122912](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122912) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixes `BACKUP ... ON CLUSTER` creating a backup that cannot be restored when a host names its data files differently from the initiator: with `data_file_name_generator = 'checksum'` and a `data_file_name_prefix_length` other than the hosts' `backups.data_file_name_prefix_length` (3 by default), or when a host's config sets `backups.data_file_name_generator = checksum` and the initiator uses the default generator. Backups already created this way still cannot be restored. [#122624](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122624) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `RESTORE` of a lightweight snapshot from `Azure Blob Storage` when the source disk endpoint includes a key prefix below the container name. [#122697](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122697) ([Garrett Thomas](https://github.com/garrettthomaskth)).
* Fixed column comments of a `ReplicatedMergeTree` table being lost in a backup when they were changed after the last metadata `ALTER`. [#121309](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121309) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* Fixed `BACKUP` / `RESTORE ... ON CLUSTER` hanging forever, unaffected by `KILL QUERY` and blocking server shutdown, when `compatibility` is set to `24.10` or older and a host fails on a `Keeper` error before it starts. Such a `compatibility` value no longer sets `backup_restore_finish_timeout_after_error_sec`, `backup_restore_failure_after_host_disconnected_for_seconds`, `backup_restore_keeper_max_retries_while_initializing`, and `backup_restore_keeper_max_retries_while_handling_error` to `0`, a combination no release ever used. [#123752](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123752) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `BACKUP` failing with `A concurrent backup writing to the same destination ... detected` when nothing else was writing there. The lock file is claimed with a conditional write, and a retry of a request that had already been applied made the backup mistake its own lock for someone else's. [#116376](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116376) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `BACKUP` and `RESTORE` to `S3` now retry requests that fail with `ExpiredToken` after refreshing credentials. The retry time limit is 120 seconds by default and can be changed with `backup_s3_expired_token_retry_timeout_ms`. [#123195](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123195) ([Nikolai Ovchinnikov](https://github.com/kolsha)).
* `BACKUP ... TO File(...)` / `Disk(...)` now fsyncs the backup data files, the `.backup` manifest and the containing directories before reporting `BACKUP_CREATED`, so an acknowledged backup to local storage survives power loss. The guarantee covers `backups.allowed_path` and everything the backup writes below it. On a `Disk(...)` over object storage that keeps its metadata in local files, those metadata files are fsynced instead, since the backup is reached only through them. Controlled by the new backup setting `fsync_backup_files` (default `true`). `S3(...)`/`AzureBlobStorage(...)` destinations are unaffected. [#111394](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111394) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `Iceberg` tables whose manifest file headers carry a schema that conflicts with the schema registered for the same `schema-id` in `metadata.json` are now readable: the `metadata.json` schema is preferred and the divergent manifest header copy is ignored with a warning, no matter which of the two copies is read first, matching the behavior of other query engines such as Spark, Trino, PyIceberg, and DuckDB. Controlled by the new setting `iceberg_tolerate_conflicting_manifest_schemas` (default `true`); `compatibility` below `26.9` restores the previous strict behavior, which failed such queries with `ICEBERG_SPECIFICATION_VIOLATION`. [#119743](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119743) ([Melvyn Peignon](https://github.com/melvynator)).
* Fixed `Iceberg` manifest statistics recording the wrong file count for added files. [#120858](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120858) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Fixed `Iceberg` `INSERT` writing no `lower_bounds` or `upper_bounds` at all into the manifest entry when the table has a column whose type cannot be serialized into a statistics bound, or a column that is entirely `NULL`. Such a field is now omitted individually and the bounds of every other column are still written, so min/max file pruning keeps working. Closes [#112706](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/112706). [#112934](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112934) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `Iceberg` date partition transforms being computed in the server time zone instead of UTC. [#118583](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118583) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Fixed `SHOW TABLES` and `system.tables` failing on a database with the `DataLakeCatalog` engine over an `Iceberg` REST catalog when an unrelated namespace was dropped by another client while the listing was in flight. [#113740](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113740) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed validation of `Iceberg` manifest-list entries during writes. [#122829](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122829) ([Arsen Muk](https://github.com/arsenmuk)).
* Fixed metadata of empty `Iceberg` tables created by ClickHouse, so that Spark and other `Iceberg` Java-based tools can open them before the first insert and evolve their partition spec. [#122077](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122077) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* Prevent loss of newer committed `Iceberg` data by rejecting `OPTIMIZE` when `iceberg_metadata_file_path`, `iceberg_snapshot_id`, or `iceberg_timestamp_ms` is explicitly set. [#122683](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122683) ([Arsen Muk](https://github.com/arsenmuk)).
* Fixed reading an Iceberg table partitioned by `DateTime64` when a data file does not store the partition column: the query failed with `DECIMAL_OVERFLOW`. Decimal partition values stored in a manifest in their raw form are decoded as well, and `system.iceberg_files.partition` now shows partition values in a readable form instead of raw bytes. [#117090](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117090) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `CREATE TABLE` and `DROP TABLE` in Iceberg REST catalog databases: the catalog now writes the initial metadata file, `ORDER BY` and table properties are sent to the catalog, multi-level namespaces are created correctly, and `DROP TABLE` uses the correct endpoint. [#123631](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123631) ([Asya Shneerson](https://github.com/asya-ch)).
* Fix `SELECT count()` over `Iceberg` tables returning too few rows after compaction leaves dangling position-delete files. Closes [#123494](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123494). [#123851](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123851) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Fixed reading `Iceberg` tables through the `DataLakeCatalog` database engine when the catalog reports a column type in a spelling the Iceberg type parser did not know: `varbyte` (Glue's name for the Iceberg `binary` type), `list<...>` (the Iceberg spelling of `array<...>`, also written by Glue), and `timestamp with local time zone` (Hive 3's name for the Iceberg `timestamptz` type). Such tables were rejected with `Unknown Iceberg type`. Closes [#121850](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121850). Closes [#87716](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/87716). [#121974](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121974) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `iceberg_metadata_staleness_ms` setting is now respected when parsing `Iceberg` metadata, so `Iceberg` table functions and `ON CLUSTER` workers no longer list the whole `metadata/` directory on every query. [#124113](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124113) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Fix a server abort when reading an `Iceberg` table whose sort order names a transform ClickHouse cannot parse, e.g. `bucket[xyz]`; ClickHouse now ignores that sort-order hint and keeps the table readable. [#121687](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121687) ([Ilias](https://github.com/iliasabk)).
* Fixed `OPTIMIZE TABLE` for OSS Iceberg tables so compaction no longer removes `version-hint.text` and breaks hint-based reads. [#122833](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122833) ([Arsen Muk](https://github.com/arsenmuk)).
* Fixed a crash (SIGSEGV) when reading a malformed deletion vector with the `Puffin` input format, for example one whose roaring run container has zero runs. Structurally invalid roaring bitmaps are now rejected with an error instead of crashing or returning wrong deleted-row positions. Closes [#124688](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124688). [#124735](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124735) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an `INSERT` into a `Parquet` `Iceberg` table failing with `Logical error: 'Iceberg data file has 1 columns while its schema has 2 fields'` when `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN` committed while the `INSERT` was running. [#121821](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121821) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `Iceberg` v3 virtual columns `_row_id` and `_last_updated_sequence_number`: filters on them no longer return no rows for a data file rewritten by copy-on-write when the manifest has statistics for the table columns but not for the row-lineage columns, and rows of files written before the table was upgraded to format version 3 no longer return `NULL` for these columns. Closes [#124271](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124271). [#124591](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124591) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#124598](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124598) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `OPTIMIZE TABLE ... MANIFEST` failing with `BAD_ARGUMENTS` on an `Iceberg` table whose current snapshot summary omits an optional `total-records`, `total-files-size`, or `total-data-files` metric, which made manifest compaction permanently unavailable on tables written by engines that omit those metrics. Closes [#117000](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/117000). [#117012](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117012) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `Iceberg` min/max pruning skipping a data file that holds matching rows when the file's `uuid` bound was written by an external engine such as Spark, Trino, or PyIceberg. Such a bound now prunes only when its lower and upper bounds are equal. This fixes the `Iceberg` manifest bounds only: the `uuid` statistics of the `Parquet` data file itself are a separate defect, so a query at default settings can still miss rows. Closes [#118366](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/118366). [#118380](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118380) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `Iceberg` writes storing ClickHouse unsigned integers in a narrower `Iceberg` integer type than the source. [#118596](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118596) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... EXECUTE remove_orphan_files` on `Iceberg` tables now rejects an `older_than` that is less than `iceberg_orphan_files_min_retention_seconds` (default 1 day) before now, so it no longer deletes the files of a write that started within that window and has not committed yet. Setting `iceberg_orphan_files_min_retention_seconds` to `0` restores the previous behavior. Closes [#125331](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125331). [#125376](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125376) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `DROP TABLE` in a `DataLakeCatalog` database with an `Iceberg` REST catalog ignoring `iceberg_delete_data_on_drop` from the server's default profile: ClickHouse always sent `purgeRequested=False`, so the catalog never deleted the table's data, and Microsoft OneLake rejected the drop with HTTP 405. ClickHouse now sends `purgeRequested=true` or `purgeRequested=false` (lowercase, as the `Iceberg` REST spec defines), chosen by that setting; a session-level `SET` does not reach this drop. Closes [#125410](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125410). [#125469](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125469) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `add.modificationTime` in Delta Lake commit logs being written in seconds instead of the milliseconds the Delta protocol requires, so a data file written by ClickHouse appeared to Spark, `delta-rs`, and `delta-kernel-rs` as last modified in 1970. Closes [#120523](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120523). [#120564](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120564) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong values and a `LOGICAL_ERROR` when reading a partitioned `DeltaLake` table with `allow_delta_kernel_rs = 0` and column types that differ from the ones in `_delta_log`. [#121544](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121544) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix reading Delta Lake tables with column mapping where a field name with a dot, like `a.b`, equals the path of a nested field (the field `b` of a struct `a`). Such a field returned the other field's values or `NULL`, `SELECT *` failed with `COLUMN_QUERIED_MORE_THAN_ONCE`, and a partitioned table failed with a `LOGICAL_ERROR`. [#123102](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123102) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when a query reads a struct column of a `DeltaLake` table with column mapping together with one of its fields, such as `SELECT s, s.x`, `SELECT *, s.x`, or `SELECT s ... WHERE s.x = 1`. Closes [#123811](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123811). [#123852](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123852) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `INSERT` into `DeltaLake` tables through a Unity `DataLakeCatalog`: the catalog now requests write credentials instead of reusing read-only ones. [#123543](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123543) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Fix DeltaLake statistics pruning for filters that compare a `Date32` column through `CAST`, `toDate`, or `toDate32`, including a wrong-results case where `1 >= d` was treated as `d >= 1`. [#119184](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119184) ([Arsen Muk](https://github.com/arsenmuk)).
* Fixed `INSERT` into a `DeltaLake` table with a `timestamp_ntz` column writing the `Parquet` data file with `isAdjustedToUTC=true`, which made the committed table unreadable by Spark and other Delta engines. Such a column is now annotated `TIMESTAMP(isAdjustedToUTC=false)`, as the Delta protocol requires. Closes [#120653](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120653). [#120863](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120863) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SHOW TABLES`, `system.tables` scans, and table resolution hanging indefinitely against a `paimon_rest` catalog whose listings paginate. [#115534](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115534) ([JIaQi Tang](https://github.com/JiaQiTang98)).
* Fixed data lake tables created on top of a server disk (`SETTINGS disk = '...'`): they no longer overwrite the disk's object storage settings with the table's or the query's settings, and they follow the disk's configuration on `SYSTEM RELOAD CONFIG` instead. [#117398](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117398) ([alesapin](https://github.com/alesapin)).
* Reading an Apache Paimon primary-key table now throws `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` instead of silently returning wrong results. Merge-on-read is not implemented, so the reader returned the raw union of the snapshot's data files, including the row versions superseded by later upserts. Closes [#113360](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/113360). [#113460](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113460) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SHOW CREATE DATABASE` and `BACKUP` of a `DataLakeCatalog` database omitting its `COMMENT`, and `ALTER DATABASE ... MODIFY SETTING` erasing that comment from the stored metadata, so that it was lost after a restart. [#122942](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122942) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reject `Paimon` manifest and data paths that escape the table directory. A crafted table could previously read files outside its own directory through an absolute path or `..` components in metadata paths. [#123099](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123099) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Fixed a data race between concurrent queries on one `Hudi` table: the list of the table's data files was filled on first use without synchronization. [#119137](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119137) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed the Paimon incremental read silently duplicating and dropping data. Advancing the consumed-snapshot watermark in Keeper is now fenced by the `processing_lock` and monotonic, so a read that lost the lock can no longer commit over another consumer's progress. A cursor left ahead of the warehouse, and a snapshot that fails to load for any reason other than Paimon expiration, now raise an error instead of being silently skipped. [#117630](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117630) ([JIaQi Tang](https://github.com/JiaQiTang98)).
* Fixed two ways a table with the `Redis` engine mishandled an `MGET` reply. Reading the whole table stopped at the first key without a string value and silently dropped every key scanned after it, so one key of another Redis type was enough to truncate the result. A reply carrying a different number of elements than keys requested could terminate the server; such a reply is now rejected with `INTERNAL_REDIS_ERROR`. [#120230](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120230) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a broker-side queue leak when dropping a `RabbitMQ` table. `DROP TABLE` asked the broker to delete the table's queues without waiting for its own consumer channels to close, so the broker could still see a registered consumer and refuse the deletion with `PRECONDITION_FAILED`. [#116050](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116050) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed spurious `NATS` connection failures when the vendored `nats-io` client gets `EINTR` while waiting for a socket, and picked up the upstream JetStream pull-subscription deadlock fix in the vendored client. [#120202](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120202) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `arrowFlight` and the `ArrowFlight` table engine hanging forever when the remote Flight server accepts a request and never answers it. Requests are now bounded by the new `arrow_flight_request_timeout_sec` setting (300 seconds by default, `0` disables the bound), and a stalled read honors `KILL QUERY` and `max_execution_time`. [#120231](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120231) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SELECT` queries on an `EmbeddedRocksDB` table with a composite `PRIMARY KEY` returning fewer rows than match the predicate when the predicate combines `IN` sets with `OR` and one set's element type cannot be converted to the key type. Such a set no longer narrows the candidate keys. [#122261](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122261) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `FileLog` table that stopped reading all of its files forever after a single read error (such as a line that failed to parse or a partially written last line): every later poll reported `Another select query is running on this table` and every direct `SELECT` failed with `CANNOT_SELECT` until the server was restarted. Related to [#123368](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123368). [#123395](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123395) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `NATS` engine table reading from a JetStream stream (`nats_stream`) silently stopping to consume messages after the NATS server restarted or the connection was re-established. [#115343](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115343) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)). [#119301](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119301) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a rare server crash (`SIGSEGV` in `mongoc_log`) when a MongoDB dictionary source or `MongoDB` table was created while another thread created the first MongoDB connection in the process. Messages logged by the MongoDB driver now go to the server log. [#124235](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124235) ([Vladimir Cherkasov](https://github.com/vdimir)).
* `RENAME DATABASE` is now refused when the database holds a loaded `S3Queue` or `AzureQueue` table whose `keeper_path` expanded the `{database}` macro when the table was loaded. Previously the rename succeeded silently, the table bound to a different `Keeper` path at the next server start and reprocessed every file it had already consumed. Not checked: detached tables, tables not yet materialized under `lazy_load_tables = 1`, and a `keeper_path` whose macros a config reload redefined after the table was loaded. [#120103](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120103) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `SELECT count()` and `system.tables.total_rows` of a `Join` table that keeps every row of a key, `ALL`, or `ANY` / `SEMI` / `ANTI` with `RIGHT`, returned the number of distinct keys instead of the number of rows. [#124099](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124099) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed reading `Enum8` and `Enum16` columns of `PostgreSQL` and `MySQL` tables, `Nullable(Enum8)` and `Nullable(Enum16)` columns of `PostgreSQL` tables, and `Enum` range columns of a `PostgreSQL` dictionary source. Values are now returned as the declared `Enum` type, parsed by element name or by number, instead of the raw remote text typed as `String` or an `Unexpected value N in enum` error. A remote value that is not an element of the enum is now rejected with an error. [#125198](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125198) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixes `RabbitMQ` tables with `rabbitmq_num_consumers > 1` that insert into materialized views with `parallel_view_processing = 0` (the default) falling behind: the consumers were read one after another, all but the first inserted a single message per flush interval, and their messages waited many intervals (up to about 28 s at 100 msg/s with a 1 s interval). They are now read in parallel. [#125106](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125106) ([Sven Eliasson](https://github.com/comino)).
* Fixed a server crash (segmentation fault) in a direct `SELECT` from a `NATS` table while the table replaces a connection that the NATS client library closed for good, for example after the broker rejected rotated credentials. Closes [#120199](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120199). [#125494](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125494) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a heap-use-after-free when an `EmbeddedRocksDB` table with a composite `PRIMARY KEY` was filtered by a tuple equality whose tuple has a `LowCardinality` element, e.g. `WHERE (k, id) = (SELECT (name, id) FROM t LIMIT 1)`. Closes [#120527](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120527). [#125509](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125509) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the Arrow Flight interface returning garbage values for a `MergeTree` column stored in sparse serialization, which is the default for a column that holds mostly default values. Affected `DoGet` with a SQL ticket, `GetFlightInfo` and `PollFlightInfo`. [#125553](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125553) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server crash when a `RabbitMQ` table with the `headers` exchange type had a routing key without `=`, or when `rabbitmq_routing_key_list` was empty. Routing keys without `=` for the `headers` exchange type are now rejected with an exception. The problem was found and reported by [@dstesk](https://github.com/dstesk). [#125661](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125661) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `StorageKafka2` now counts only live replicas when distributing partition locks, so a replica that lost its `Keeper` session no longer leaves some partitions unconsumed. [#120111](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120111) ([johnjing](https://github.com/JingYanchao)).
* Fixed memory growth in `Kafka` tables with offsets stored in `Keeper` when partitions move between consumers: prefetched messages for released partitions are now freed rather than retained until restart. Closes [#125077](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125077). [#125076](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125076) ([Aparajita Pandey](https://github.com/aparajita31pandey)).
* Fixed spurious `TLS/SSL error: Interrupted system call` failures in `mysql`, the `MySQL` table engine, the `MySQL` database engine, and `MySQL` dictionaries by updating `mariadb-connector-c` to v3.4.10, with a patch that clears the OpenSSL error queue before every TLS operation. [#120526](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120526) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a durability bug in the `MySQL` and `PostgreSQL` database engines. The marker file recording whether a table is detached or dropped was created and removed without fsyncing the metadata directory, so a power loss could silently revert an acknowledged `DETACH TABLE ... PERMANENTLY`, `DROP TABLE`, or `ATTACH TABLE` and change the table's visibility on restart. It is now fsynced when `fsync_metadata` is enabled (the default). A failed `ATTACH TABLE` no longer leaves the table half-attached, which made a retry report `TABLE_ALREADY_EXISTS`. [#112170](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112170) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Using the `PostgreSQL` engine, inserts now quote table and column names so PostgreSQL does not fold them to lowercase. The destination table can therefore use any identifier casing. [#120435](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120435) ([Thomas Sileghem](https://github.com/mastilver)).
* Fixed `KILL QUERY` and `max_execution_time` having no effect on a query reading from `PostgreSQL` when the remote host stops sending data, and `DROP` of a refreshable materialized view whose refresh reads from `PostgreSQL` hanging for the same reason. [#118995](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118995) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* Fixed `PostgreSQL` connections losing `send_timeout` after the handshake. [#123094](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123094) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* HTTP dictionary sources (`SOURCE(HTTP(...))`) now respect the `max_http_get_redirects` setting, so dictionaries can be created from URLs that redirect. Previously such dictionaries always failed with `TOO_MANY_REDIRECTS`. [#101768](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/101768) ([Yash ](https://github.com/Onyx2406)).
* Fix `optimize_inverse_dictionary_lookup` rewriting `dictGet` comparisons incorrectly: complex-key dictionaries with a single key column called with a `tuple`-wrapped, possibly `Nullable`, key expression failed with `ILLEGAL_TYPE_OF_ARGUMENT`; key expressions that rely on `dictGet`'s implicit key type conversion, e.g. a `String` expression for a `UUID` key, failed with `NO_COMMON_TYPE`, and lossy ones silently returned results where `dictGet` throws; a match on a dictionary key containing `NULL` produced `NULL` instead of `false`; a single matching floating-point `NaN` or zero key produced wrong results; and mismatched key shapes were folded to a result instead of the `dictGet` error. Key expressions that would need a conversion are no longer rewritten and keep the `dictGet` lookup, except a signed integer probe of a simple-key dictionary, which is converted to the `UInt64` lookup type the way `dictGet` converts it. [#114865](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114865) ([Artur Beglaryan](https://github.com/artbeglaryan)).
* Fixed a dictionary with a local `CLICKHOUSE` source that stopped updating with `ACCESS_DENIED` and kept serving stale data after a new role was granted to its source user and the old role was dropped. Every query of such a source now uses the current default roles of the user, like a new session of that user. Closes [#122663](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122663). [#124051](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124051) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed ClickHouse Keeper terminating when a client sent a `multi` request with no subrequests. Such a request was written to the Keeper changelog before it failed, so the termination repeated on every restart. [#119847](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119847) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed ClickHouse Keeper discarding a `world:anyone` ACL entry with all permissions when the request contained other entries. Such a node was stored more restrictively than requested and could end up repairable only with a `super` credential; with `<path_acls>` configured and no `<identity>`, a `ReplicatedMergeTree` under the configured path could not be created at all. These nodes now get the requested permissions, which grants every session full access to them, as ZooKeeper does. Closes [#120177](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120177). [#120265](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120265) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `Keeper` persistent recursive watch events to report the path of the changed node, matching ZooKeeper behavior. Previously, they reported the path where the watch was registered. [#121333](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121333) ([Kirill](https://github.com/kirillgarbar)).
* Fix a segmentation fault in `ClickHouse Keeper` when preallocating space for a changelog file fails because the disk is full. [#122337](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122337) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* ClickHouse Keeper no longer sends the same watch event to one session several times when more than one of the session's watches matches a change, for example persistent recursive watches on `/a` and `/a/b` for a `create /a/b/c`, or a single persistent watch on a removed node. Like ZooKeeper, Keeper now sends each event to a session once. Closes [#122291](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122291). [#122706](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122706) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a Keeper durability gap: with `force_sync` enabled, the directory entry of a new changelog segment, or of a segment continued after a restart, was never fsynced, so acknowledged writes in that segment could be lost after a power loss on filesystems where a file fsync does not persist the file's name. Keeper now fsyncs the log directory before the first entries of such a segment are reported durable, and stops if that fails. [#122755](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122755) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed Keeper silently giving a new session to a ZooKeeper client, for example the Java client or `kazoo`, that reconnects and asks to continue its session. Such a client now gets `SessionExpired`, ZooKeeper's answer for a session that cannot be continued, instead of losing its ephemeral nodes later without notice. Keeper still does not restore sessions. Closes [#116690](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/116690). [#122767](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122767) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the Keeper HTTP storage API (`/api/v1/storage`) storing a truncated value and reporting success when a `POST` or `PUT` body was larger than `max_request_size`. Such requests are now rejected with `413`, and the node is left unchanged. Closes [#122286](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122286). [#122928](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122928) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix Keeper recovery with `remove_orphaned_nodes_on_startup` leaving an unrepaired snapshot on disk. Persist the repaired snapshot so followers can install it successfully and the recovered node can restart with orphan recovery disabled. [#123206](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123206) ([Seva Potapov](https://github.com/seva-potapov)).
* Fixed a Keeper node that restarted after a crash sometimes giving a new client the session ID of a client that had connected to it before the restart, after which the new client received that client's responses. [#124307](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124307) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* ClickHouse now rejects relative paths in `checksums.txt`. [#120356](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120356) ([alesapin](https://github.com/alesapin)).
* Encryption codecs (`AES_128_GCM_SIV`, `AES_256_GCM_SIV`) now also cover the structural substreams of a column. [#120636](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120636) ([Raufs Dunamalijevs](https://github.com/rienath)).
* Rejected a compressed block whose codec stores data verbatim, such as `NONE` or `Quantized`, when its declared uncompressed size does not match the body length. Previously that passthrough read path accepted such a block while every other decompression path rejected it. [#114344](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114344) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reject invalid HTTP header names supplied to SQL-facing HTTP clients (the `url` table function and engine, HTTP dictionaries, `S3`/object storage, and `DataLake`/REST catalogs). A header name must be a valid RFC 9110 token (letters, digits and ``!#$%&'*+-.^_`|~``); any other byte, `:`, whitespace, control characters, or separators such as `/` and `=`, is rejected. Such names could previously bypass the `<http_forbid_headers>` restriction. [#118830](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118830) ([Sergei Trifonov](https://github.com/serxa)).
* Fixed `ALTER QUOTA` silently not taking effect when a quota is changed to `KEYED BY client_key`: a user whose quota context was already cached kept the previous, more permissive limits, and the failed refresh also stopped every other cached context from picking up unrelated quota changes. [#120240](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120240) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Kafka producer and consumer trace logs now show secret `librdkafka` properties, such as `sasl.password`, as `[HIDDEN]` instead of their cleartext values. [#119244](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119244) ([János Benjamin Antal](https://github.com/antaljanosbenjamin)).
* `EXPLAIN AST` no longer prints secret function arguments, such as the key of `encrypt`, in cleartext, including with `optimize = 1` for the body of a view the user may only `SELECT` from. [#120313](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120313) ([Harikrishnan Prabakaran](https://github.com/harikrishnan94)).
* ClickHouse now masks credentials in `system.query_log` even for invalid URLs of external table engines. [#122057](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122057) ([alesapin](https://github.com/alesapin)).
* The value of `role_session_name` is now hidden in `query_log`. [#122423](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122423) ([Alexey Bakharew](https://github.com/alexbakharew)).
* `BACKUP ... TO S3(...)` and `RESTORE ... FROM S3(...)` now enforce `remote_url_allow_hosts` on the initial `S3` URL, instead of checking only redirect targets. Endpoints that the `s3` table function would reject now fail with `UNACCEPTABLE_URL` here as well. [#123114](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123114) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* A password or presigned signature in a URL is no longer printed in clear by the `ConfluentSchemaRegistry` trace log, by the `URL ... is not allowed in configuration file, see <remote_url_allow_hosts>` error, or by the `Unsupported scheme in URI` error. These now show `scheme://[HIDDEN]@host` and `X-Amz-Signature=[HIDDEN]`, as `system.query_log` already does for `format_avro_schema_registry_url`. [#123226](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123226) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SYSTEM SYNC TRANSACTION LOG` (local, non-`ON CLUSTER` form) not checking the `SYSTEM_SYNC_TRANSACTION_LOG` privilege. The `ON CLUSTER` form already required it, so a user without the grant could run the local form when experimental transactions were enabled. [#112441](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112441) ([Azhar Izzannada E](https://github.com/azharizz)).
* The values of MongoDB connection options that carry a secret (`tlsCertificateKeyFilePassword`, `sslClientCertificateKeyPassword`, `AWS_SESSION_TOKEN` in `authMechanismProperties`) are now hidden in `SHOW CREATE`, `system.query_log` and `system.dictionaries.source` for the `MongoDB` table engine, the `mongodb` table function and the MongoDB dictionary source, `system.dictionaries.source` no longer shows the MongoDB password, and a named-collection `uri` override after another override is no longer shown as written. [#123240](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123240) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A `SELECT` row policy on the `mergeTreeProjection` table function (`_table_function.mergeTreeProjection`) is now also applied to a read shipped to another server as a serialized query plan (`serialize_query_plan = 1`, with `process_query_plan_packet` enabled there), as it already is for a local read or with `serialize_query_plan = 0`. [#123609](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123609) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `SYSTEM DROP DATABASE REPLICA` without a database name is now denied for a user without the `SYSTEM DROP REPLICA` privilege, instead of silently doing nothing and reporting success. This makes it consistent with `SYSTEM DROP REPLICA`. [#117964](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117964) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY SETTING disk = disk(...)` could register a disk defined in SQL whose local path is outside `custom_local_disks_base_directory`, and create its directory, bypassing the check that `CREATE TABLE` applies. Now it is rejected the same way, and a statement rejected this way leaves no disk and no directory behind. [#119850](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119850) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... ATTACH PARTITION ... FROM ...` and `CREATE TABLE ... CLONE AS ...` requiring the `ALTER DELETE` privilege on the destination table. These statements only add data, so `INSERT` is now enough. `REPLACE PARTITION`, which does drop the destination data, still requires `ALTER DELETE`. A session temporary table used as the source of `ATTACH` or `REPLACE PARTITION ... FROM` also no longer wrongly requires `SELECT` on a same-named table of the current database. [#117936](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117936) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed the server terminating when an argument of a `disk(...)` setting value is a key without a value, such as `disk(equals(name))`. A malformed `disk(...)` argument no longer causes the whole query, including its other secrets, to be logged unmasked. [#123847](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123847) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Mask secrets in the queries that a cluster-mode `QueryRunner` table writes to `system.query_log` as well as in queries executed as startup scripts. [#118978](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118978) ([Miсhael Stetsyuk](https://github.com/mstetsyuk)).
* A `RESTORE TABLE ... AS <name>` of a backup whose engine arguments were stripped now validates the Keeper path it derives from `default_replica_path`. Previously the `{database}` and `{table}` substitutions were expanded against the restored name without being checked, so a name containing `/` resolved into another table's Keeper subtree and left an inactive replica there. [#121546](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121546) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Hide secrets in the `system.mutations` table. [#122717](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122717) ([alesapin](https://github.com/alesapin)).
* Creating a `FileLog` table now requires the `READ` and `WRITE` privileges, as for a `File` table. [#125701](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125701) ([Robert Schulze](https://github.com/rschu1ze)).
* Fixed an out-of-bounds read when reading a `Variant` column from a truncated `Native` stream using compact discriminators serialization. [#120252](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120252) ([Raufs Dunamalijevs](https://github.com/rienath)).
* Reject malformed PostgreSQL startup messages whose parameter data exceeds the declared payload size. [#112780](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112780) ([Minh Vu](https://github.com/fallintoplace)).
* Reading settings from the Native protocol no longer sizes a string from the length a peer declares before the payload arrives, so a Query packet or a server hello can no longer turn a few bytes on the wire into a large memory allocation. This covers settings and query parameters, on the server and in the client. [#118612](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118612) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#120931](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120931) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a crash when a client sends a block over the native TCP protocol that selects the internal `Detached` serialization kind for a column. [#117699](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117699) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* `COPY` options are no longer silently ignored on the PostgreSQL wire protocol: `WITH (FORMAT csv)` and the legacy `WITH CSV` are now applied, unsupported options are reported instead of skipped, and TSV header auto-detection no longer drops the first data row of a `COPY ... FROM STDIN`. [#119376](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119376) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Bound the header column count in `RowBinary` formats to avoid excessive memory allocation from a crafted header. [#120282](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120282) ([George Larionov](https://github.com/george-larionov)).
* Reject malformed `Native` blocks or corrupted parts that could make ClickHouse read past the end of a `JSON` or `Dynamic` column, for example because of duplicate types or paths, a truncated stream, or a part-only serialization version sent over the wire. [#117765](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117765) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Fixed `handshake_timeout_milliseconds` not bounding a connection that stops sending during the handshake, which held a thread for `receive_timeout` (300 seconds by default) instead. The setting now also covers the MySQL and PostgreSQL handshake phases and the TLS negotiation; previously a peer that dribbled bytes during a TLS handshake, or stayed silent on the PostgreSQL port, could hold a thread indefinitely. HTTP header reading keeps its own `http_headers_read_timeout` budget, now enforced by the same mechanism. On `tcp_port_secure`, ClickHouse no longer writes an exception to a client whose TLS handshake has already timed out, so such a peer no longer holds a handler thread for twice the configured budget. [#121547](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121547) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)). [#123101](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123101) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Reject malformed binary input with out-of-range VarInt, integer, or enum values, including settings, client info, query plans, distributed task descriptions, and text indexes, instead of silently truncating them. [#113944](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113944) ([sunliqiang](https://github.com/Pyolar)). [#123242](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123242) ([Sergei Trifonov](https://github.com/serxa)).
* The collections of the `Native` protocol, the settings and query parameters of a query, the client information, the granted roles, and the table-status lists, are now bounded, so a small packet can no longer make the server allocate memory for data that has not arrived. [#117015](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117015) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `Code: 102. UNEXPECTED_PACKET_FROM_SERVER (... got Progress)` aborting an `INSERT` that requests a table structure from the server, when a subquery is evaluated while the server analyses the query. The `Progress` and `ProfileEvents` packets sent in that window are now drained instead of rejected, both by the client and by the initiator of an `INSERT` into a `Distributed` or `remote` table, where rejecting them also hid the shard's own error. Affects `INSERT ... SELECT`, `input` inserts, and `INSERT ... VALUES` with `async_insert = 0`. [#118946](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118946) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the PostgreSQL wire protocol rejecting a simple query that consists only of comments, such as the `-- ping` that the Go driver `pgx` v5 sends from `Ping`, with `Empty query`. Such a query is now answered with `EmptyQueryResponse`, as in PostgreSQL. Closes [#84085](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/84085). [#125161](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125161) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `http_max_multipart_form_data_size` silently truncating oversized multipart data instead of failing the query, and made it span a form's external-table parts instead of resetting per part. [#120036](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120036) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* `KILL QUERY`, query timeouts, and client disconnects now stop a query that reads over HTTP, for example over the `url` table function, while it is retrying a request, instead of waiting until all retry attempts are exhausted. [#112930](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112930) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed gRPC queries with transport compression (`gzip`, `deflate`) crashing the server, as they could in official builds since 26.4. Closes [#115121](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/115121). [#122668](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122668) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Prevented asynchronous inserts that only need to buffer data from being blocked by another insert waiting for a flush worker while holding their shared queue lock. `SYSTEM FLUSH ASYNC INSERT QUEUE` now also waits for batches removed from the queue that are still waiting for a worker. [#118352](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118352) ([RJ](https://github.com/VrtxOmega)).
* Fixed asynchronous `INSERT` under a quota `KEYED BY client_key`: the flush ran without the client's quota key, so it failed with `QUOTA_REQUIRES_CLIENT_KEY` (or charged the wrong bucket). The quota key is now carried through the async batch and restored on flush, and background quota recomputes no longer log a spurious `QUOTA_REQUIRES_CLIENT_KEY` error. [#122296](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122296) ([Mikhail f. Shiryaev](https://github.com/Felixoid)).
* Fixed `max_memory_usage_for_user` being exceeded by memory the user no longer holds. Memory that a query allocates but does not free itself, such as the data of in-memory table engines, cache entries and data waiting in the asynchronous insert queue, is now accounted to whoever owns it, and whatever a query leaves behind is settled when it ends. `max_memory_usage_for_user` is also applied when a query hands over memory it allocated before it had a user. [#113647](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113647) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Queries that send data over pooled connections, such as `INSERT` into a `Distributed` table, are no longer charged for connection buffers that stay in the pool after they finish, which could count against `max_memory_usage_for_user` while another query of the same user was running. [#125573](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125573) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix a possible hang of a query under workload CPU scheduling with `cpu_slot_preemption` enabled: when all its threads were preempted, the query could keep CPU slots it had already used and wait forever for one of them. [#123640](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123640) ([Sergei Trifonov](https://github.com/serxa)).
* Fixed vector search returning too few rows for a query with `arrayJoin` below `ORDER BY <distance> LIMIT`. A row policy now counts as an additional filter for vector search, so `vector_search_filter_strategy = 'prefilter'` is honored and the index fetch multiplier is applied for a query filtered by a row policy. Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for a non-rescoring vector search query when a `WHERE` filter or a row policy reads the vector column. [#114074](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114074) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix a vector search returning fewer rows than its `LIMIT`, and missing true nearest neighbors, after a lightweight `DELETE` (or with a delete mutation applied on the fly): the vector index is no longer used for a part while it still holds deleted rows. [#119371](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119371) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `CREATE TABLE` now rejects a `SETTINGS` name that is neither a setting of the table engine nor a query setting for the `File`, `URL`, `S3` (with `COSN`, `OSS`, `GCS`), `AzureBlobStorage`, `HDFS`, `Log`, `TinyLog`, and `StripeLog` engines, with `UNKNOWN_SETTING`, as `MergeTree` already did. The `name = DEFAULT` and `param_name = value` spellings are rejected too, except where the whole clause is dropped before any engine sees it. Such a name used to be accepted, stored in the table metadata, and never read, so a typo like `input_format_csv_delimiter` for `format_csv_delimiter` silently did nothing. A table whose stored definition already names a non-setting still loads. Closes [#120705](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120705). [#120898](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120898) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `arrayJoin` in a projection or in a `Distributed` sharding key is now rejected at `CREATE`/`ALTER` time. It changes the number of rows, which made every insert into a table with such a projection fail with an exception, and misrouted or failed inserts into such a `Distributed` table. [#117791](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117791) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY ORDER BY` accepting a key expression over a column that the same `ALTER` retypes through a subcolumn, for example `MODIFY COLUMN x Tuple(a UInt32, b UInt32), MODIFY ORDER BY (k, x.a)`, or renames, for example `RENAME COLUMN x TO y, MODIFY ORDER BY (k, y)`. Existing parts are not sorted by such a key, so queries that rely on the sort order could return wrong results. Such an `ALTER` is now refused with `BAD_ARGUMENTS`. Closes [#122685](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122685). [#122765](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122765) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY QUERY` deleting the column comments of a materialized view with a `TO` table. A column that the new query still produces now keeps its comment. [#122775](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122775) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE <mv> COMMENT COLUMN <col> '...'` and comment-only `ALTER TABLE <mv> MODIFY COLUMN <col> COMMENT '...'` returning success and storing nothing on a materialized view with an inner table, which left no supported way to change a column comment on such a view after `CREATE`. [#119469](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119469) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#123033](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123033) ([Pedro Ferreira](https://github.com/PedroTadim)).
* Fix `DROP NAMED COLLECTION` succeeding while a `MongoDB` table uses the collection, after which the server could not attach the table on restart. The drop is now refused with `NAMED_COLLECTION_IS_USED`, as for other table engines. [#123636](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123636) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A sharding key of a `Distributed` table, a `Remote` table or the `remote`/`cluster` table functions that contains `IN` with a subquery or with a non-`Set` table is now rejected when the table is defined. The set of such an `IN` was never built, so every `INSERT` failed with a `Not-ready Set` logical error and `optimize_skip_unused_shards` could prune to the wrong shard and return wrong results. A table defined earlier with such a key still loads, but an `INSERT` that needs the key fails with `BAD_ARGUMENTS` and shard pruning does not use it. Pruning by a key with `IN` over a `Set` table, which can change after rows are placed, now needs `allow_nondeterministic_optimize_skip_unused_shards`, as for `joinGet`. [#124646](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124646) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Reject a `RENAME DATABASE` that would make the table dependency graph cyclic, for example one that leaves an `Alias` table referring to itself. Such a rename put a cycle into the server-wide table dependency graph, after which every unrelated `CREATE` that adds a dependency edge failed with `INFINITE_LOOP` and the renamed database stopped being listable after a restart. [#117253](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117253) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed formatting of a function named `any` with a single subquery argument: ``SELECT 1 = `any`((SELECT 1))`` lost its backticks, and because bare `ANY` before a subquery is the SQL quantifier, formatting the query changed its meaning to `IN`. This affected round-trips such as stored view definitions and `SHOW CREATE`. [#120175](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120175) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed parsing of a column named `from` inside parentheses, such as `WHERE (from IN ('a'))`, which since 26.8 was read as a subquery starting with the `FROM` clause and failed with `Unknown table function IN`, `Unknown table expression identifier`, or a syntax error. [#120950](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120950) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Preserve quoting for identifiers named `cube`, `rollup`, and `recursive` so formatted queries can be parsed back correctly. [#121896](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121896) ([Jaysurya  Ray](https://github.com/Jaysurya85)).
* Fix `SET <setting> = DEFAULT` ignoring an active `compatibility` setting: the setting now gets the value of that version, unless the settings constraints forbid it, and `SET compatibility = DEFAULT` reverts the settings it changed. Closes [#116883](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/116883). [#116933](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116933) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed direct config substitutions via `from_env` failing when XML-1.0-legal values contain XML special characters like `&`, `<`, `>`, or the `]]>` sequence; existing `<include from_env="..."/>` XML-subtree substitutions are unchanged. An explicit `<include from_zk="..." yaml="true"/>` substitution can read a YAML subtree wherever the generic `<include>` form is used, including inside a leaf element, so it must not be used for a literal secret or setting. Without the explicit `yaml="true"` attribute, a non-`<` value is literal text, but direct non-`<` leaf `from_zk` substitutions are recommended for exact-byte secrets or settings, because surrounding whitespace of an `<include>` becomes part of a leaf value. Values beginning with `<` continue to be interpreted as XML fragments for backward compatibility. Upgrade note: a direct `from_env` value or a non-`<` leaf `from_zk` value that was previously XML-entity-encoded, for example `a&amp;b`, now resolves to its exact literal bytes and must be stored raw as `a&b` instead. [#101773](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/101773) ([Yash ](https://github.com/Onyx2406)).
* Fixed the server refusing to start with `UNKNOWN_ELEMENT_IN_CONFIG` when the definition files of executable user-defined functions or of their drivers are placed in `config.d` (for example with `<user_defined_executable_functions_config>config.d/*_function.xml</user_defined_executable_functions_config>`), which also merges their `<function>` or `<driver>` elements into the server config. Closes [#123308](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123308). [#123586](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123586) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Queries over `system.parts`, `system.parts_columns`, `system.projection_parts`, `system.projection_parts_columns`, and `system.dropped_tables_parts` now respect cancellation and `max_execution_time` while the result is being built. [#113909](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113909) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a data race on the exception message for a cancelled query: threads no longer append context concurrently to one shared exception object. [#119077](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119077) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed unbounded memory growth from time zone names that were not names of the time zone database. A time zone name now has to be one that `system.time_zones` lists, or a fixed UTC offset spelled `Fixed/UTC±HH:MM:SS`. [#120288](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120288) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a server abort during transaction rollback: internal exception-reporting helpers could call `std::terminate` while no exception was being handled. [#122891](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122891) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Fixed a crash when synchronous logging (`logger.async = 0`) had no log sink that accepted a message. [#123336](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123336) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a permanent hang of the server on macOS, where threads that format a time (for example while signing `S3` requests, reporting an exceeded quota or reloading a dictionary) could wait forever on the C library's internal time zone lock while the query profiler was enabled. [#125369](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125369) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` on a table that has a physical column named `_part` shadowing the virtual column, for queries such as `SELECT count() FROM t WHERE isNull(_part > 0)` and `SELECT count() FROM t WHERE _partition_id = 'all'`. [#117539](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117539) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* The statistics cache is now refreshed for read-only `MergeTree` tables. [#119559](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119559) ([Rory Shanks](https://github.com/rorylshanks)).
* A `MergeTree` table with `table_disk = 1` mounts the parts already present at the disk root. If it declares a sorting key those parts were not written with, reading it raised `LOGICAL_ERROR` and aborted the server in builds with assertions enabled. It now reports `INCORRECT_DATA` and names the offending part. [#119295](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119295) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a refresh of a table whose disks are all read-only (`SYSTEM RESTART DISK`, or the background `refresh_parts_interval` task) failing with a `LOGICAL_ERROR` and hiding committed parts when a rolled-back part covers them. Follow-up to [#100992](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/100992), which fixed the same case on the startup part-loading path. [#111167](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111167) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `SYSTEM SYNC MERGES` no longer returns while the part covering the scheduled merges is still uncommitted: it now waits until a new transaction can see that part. [#123396](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123396) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Reject empty part names in `CHECK TABLE`. [#123625](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123625) ([Minh Vu](https://github.com/fallintoplace)).
* Partition commands on a `DateTime64` key accept a literal with `.` as the date separator again, such as `'2024.02.19 00:00:00.500'`. [#122995](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122995) ([DeviousCardi](https://github.com/DeviousCardi)).
* A `CHECK TABLE` running while a part writes `txn_version.txt` no longer fails with `Unexpected file txn_version.txt.tmp in data part`. [#124158](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124158) ([Murphy](https://github.com/murphy-4o)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... MATERIALIZE TTL` failing with `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` and being retried forever on a table with `ttl_only_drop_parts = 1` when a part still holds a column that the table dropped while that part's partition was detached. Mutating such a partition after cloning it into another table with `ATTACH PARTITION FROM` no longer fails with a logical error either. [#115962](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115962) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a member of a `Nested` added by `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN` to a `Memory` table being read as empty arrays for the rows inserted before the `ALTER`, unless another member of the same `Nested` was read at the same time. It now gets the array sizes of the other members, like in `MergeTree`. This fixes wrong results of `SELECT`, `WHERE`, `PREWHERE`, `ARRAY JOIN`, and row policies over such a column, and `SIZES_OF_ARRAYS_DONT_MATCH` errors. [#122991](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122991) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SELECT` from `system.part_moves_between_shards` and `KILL PART_MOVE_TO_SHARD` failing with `Code: 999 ... No node, path .../part_moves_shard` when any replicated table on the server is attached without its Keeper tree. [#122132](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122132) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an exception (`BAD_GET` or `NOT_IMPLEMENTED`) when a filter on a table with a `_part_offset` projection had a `Float` or `Nullable(Nothing)` condition over a column the projection does not store. [#124568](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124568) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a skip index recording the wrong expression type when `ALTER TABLE` ran in a session whose settings change how that expression resolves. `toString(v)` over a `Dynamic` column is `Nullable(String)` under `cast_keep_nullable` and `String` at the server baseline, so such an `ALTER` stored `Nullable(String)` while the writers resolved `String`, and the next index rebuild failed an internal type assertion. [#119799](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119799) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `hasPhrase` rejecting `FixedString` input for some tokenizers, such as `icu` and `splitByRegexp('\W+')`. Closes [#123426](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123426). [#123585](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123585) ([Elmi Ahmadov](https://github.com/ahmadov)).
* Fixed statistics about the number of selected rows in trivial `count` queries answered from a text index. [#125380](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125380) ([Anton Popov](https://github.com/CurtizJ)).
* `ALTER TABLE ... RESET SETTING auto_statistics_types` now rebuilds the automatic column statistics from the default types immediately. Previously the table kept the statistics of the old setting value until the server was restarted, so `system.columns` and the statistics written into new parts disagreed with the table definition. [#123599](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123599) ([Rion Bora](https://github.com/GoldenEmperor1177)).
* Fix `basic` statistics of parts written by 26.6 or 26.7: after `ALTER TABLE ... UPDATE` or a lightweight `DELETE`, non-`Nullable` columns reported a default-value count of 0, which made selectivity estimates wrong. Query results were not affected. To repair parts that are already affected, run `ALTER TABLE ... MATERIALIZE STATISTICS ALL`. [#123536](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123536) ([Samuel Krempaský](https://github.com/NIKTONIKTO717)).
* Fixed `indexHint` with a `Nothing`-typed argument, such as `indexHint(assumeNotNull(materialize(NULL)))`, which threw `Cannot create non-empty column with type Nothing` instead of only narrowing the read. [#117635](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117635) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `arrayReduce` over a non-deterministic aggregate such as an unseeded `groupArraySample` being treated as deterministic, which pushed a `HAVING` predicate below the aggregation and collapsed per-row draws into a single one. [#118971](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118971) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed an internal error when two `*` matchers whose column transformers differ in kind at the same position are compared, for example two lambdas sharing an alias with bodies `* EXCEPT (a)` and `* REPLACE (1 AS a)`. Such a query now reports `MULTIPLE_EXPRESSIONS_FOR_ALIAS`. [#120234](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120234) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `LOGICAL_ERROR` (`Can't set alias of ... of QualifiedAsterisk`) when a column transformer that assigns a name, such as `APPLY` with a name prefix or `REPLACE`, is applied to an expression that expands to an asterisk or a `COLUMNS` matcher. Such queries now return a clear `BAD_ARGUMENTS` exception. [#109223](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/109223) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for `LIMIT BY` on an injective function of a `GROUP BY` key, such as `GROUP BY toString(c0) LIMIT 1 BY toString(c0)`. [#120070](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120070) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `LOGICAL_ERROR: Incorrect table expression` when `EXPLAIN AST optimize = 1` or `EXPLAIN SYNTAX` reads from the experimental table function `eval`. [#121272](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121272) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A non-finite `FROM`, `TO`, `STEP`, or `STALENESS` value in `ORDER BY ... WITH FILL` (`nan`, `inf`, or `-inf`) is now rejected with `INVALID_WITH_FILL_EXPRESSION`. Previously such a value either made the query generate fill rows endlessly (`TO nan`, `TO inf` ascending, `TO -inf` descending, and the same values for `STALENESS`) or produced a wrong result: a non-finite `FROM` emitted the bound itself as a row and dropped the fill rows that `TO` asked for, while a non-finite `STEP`, or a `TO` bound the fill runs away from, returned the rows unfilled. Pass a finite value instead, or omit it. [#118434](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118434) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed exception `Trying to compare constants of incompatible types` for a query with an `IN` list over a column of a table that has a `CONSTRAINT ... ASSUME`, when both `convert_query_to_cnf` and `optimize_using_constraints` are enabled. [#118278](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118278) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `EXPLAIN AST` no longer throws when a `Backup` locator name looks like a `COLUMNS` matcher. [#122870](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122870) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `ORDER BY ... WITH FILL ... INTERPOLATE` of the fill column itself is now rejected over a `Distributed` table as well, with the same exception a local table already reported, instead of returning rows whose fill column was overwritten by the interpolated expression. [#118304](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118304) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when a query over the `filesystem` table function combines `indexHint` with an ordinary condition on the same column. [#118671](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118671) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed wrong results from `optimize_using_constraints` with a `CONSTRAINT ... ASSUME NOT (...)`: a negated comparison could be added to the constraints graph as if it were positive, making predicates such as `a < b` appear always true and `a >= b` always false. [#121255](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121255) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* A `HAVING` clause referencing a `SELECT` alias whose quoted name contains a dot, as in ``SELECT sum(t1.c1) AS `t1.c1` FROM t1 AS t1 HAVING `t1.c1` > 0``, is no longer rejected with `NOT_AN_AGGREGATE`. The identifier resolution cache conflated that quoted one-part name with the qualified `t1`.`c1`, which could also return a wrong result instead of an error when both spellings met in one query. Default settings were affected from 26.6, where `enable_identifier_resolve_cache` became enabled by default. [#122017](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122017) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The analyzer now rejects `arrayJoin` in `INTERPOLATE` and in `HAVING` with `WITH TOTALS`. Before, `INTERPOLATE` used only the first array element, and `HAVING` failed only during execution. [#123538](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123538) ([Yarik Briukhovetskyi](https://github.com/yariks5s)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when a comparison of single-element tuples whose elements are tuples, such as `tuple((a, b)) = tuple((c, d))`, is part of an alias used in `GROUP BY` and also in `ORDER BY`, `HAVING`, or `WHERE`. Such comparisons are now rewritten completely, so `WHERE tuple(tuple(x)) = tuple(tuple(y))` over a `JOIN` no longer returns rows where `x` and `y` are both `NULL`, the same as `WHERE tuple(x) = tuple(y)`. [#124546](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124546) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the exception `Not-ready Set is passed as the second argument for function 'in'` when the `additional_result_filter` setting contains an `IN` subquery. Such a query now applies the filter instead of failing. [#117719](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117719) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A nested correlated subquery that cannot be decorrelated now reports `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` instead of the internal error `Cannot find column ... in actions DAG input`. [#118317](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118317) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` for a correlated subquery in the `SELECT` list of a query that has a `WHERE`, when the correlated column is not also selected, and `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for a correlated subquery in `HAVING` of a query with `WITH TOTALS`. [#118402](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118402) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` for a correlated subquery that reads a tuple element of an outer column by position (`o.p.1`), and for the same shape on the left of `IN (subquery)` under `rewrite_in_to_join`. [#118287](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118287) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `Cannot evaluate correlated scalar subquery` for a query that uses one correlated scalar subquery twice in the same expression, for example in both branches of `if(1 = 1, ..., ...)`. [#118321](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118321) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed an out-of-bounds read when finalizing a crafted `analysisOfVariance` aggregate state whose internal arrays have mismatched lengths. [#107860](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107860) ([uwezkhan](https://github.com/uwezkhan)).
* Fixed an out-of-bounds read of the parsed pattern in `sequenceMatch`, `sequenceCount`, and `sequenceMatchEvents`. When the trailing part of a pattern consisted only of actions that a zero-length match satisfies (`.*` and `(?t...)` conditions), the matcher read one element past the end of its action list, so `sequenceMatch` and `sequenceCount` could return `0` for a pattern that does match. [#119871](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119871) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix an out-of-bounds read when deserializing a `uniqTheta` state shorter than 24 bytes, for instance from `CAST(<string> AS AggregateFunction(uniqTheta, ...))`. [#119598](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119598) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `regr_r2` returning `1` instead of `nan` when the data contains a NaN or an infinity. `regr_sxx` and `regr_syy` returned `0` in the same case. [#123249](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123249) ([mosya415](https://github.com/mosya415)).
* Reject malformed serialized `groupBitmap` states with `INCORRECT_DATA` instead of failing later during bitmap operations. [#124049](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124049) ([Elmi Ahmadov](https://github.com/ahmadov)).
* Fixed wrong results in the `largestTriangleThreeBuckets` aggregate function for `DateTime64` arguments close to the type's upper bound: the maximum `DateTime64(9)` value was returned as `1677-09-21 00:12:43.145224192`. Such values are now saturated instead of wrapping, and a deserialized state whose `Date`, `Date32`, `DateTime` or `DateTime64` coordinates are out of range or fractional is rejected with an error. [#120284](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120284) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a wrong result, or an exception in debug builds, when `optimize_aggregators_of_group_by_keys` eliminated `any`, `min` or `max` of a `GROUPING SETS` key with `group_by_use_nulls`. [#125575](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125575) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed several URL-parsing bugs in `domainRFC` and `portRFC` for bracketed IPv6 hosts: an embedded IPv4 tail (for example `[::ffff:192.168.0.1]`) was rejected, non-IPv6 bracket contents and garbage following the closing bracket were silently accepted, and userinfo state could leak into host validation. Also fixed `domainRFC` and `portRFC` resolving the wrong host when a URL's userinfo contains more than one `@`, rejected a host starting with a literal dot in `domain` and `domainRFC`, and fixed `firstSignificantSubdomainRFC` and `cutToFirstSignificantSubdomainRFC` returning a bogus subdomain for a bracketed IPv6 host instead of an empty result. [#118979](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118979) ([Luís Lizardo](https://github.com/lizardoluis)).
* Fixed `IPv4StringToNum`, `IPv6StringToNum`, `isIPAddressInRange`, `parseDateTime`, `parseDateTime64`, `parseDateTimeInJodaSyntax`, `parseTimeDelta`, `parseReadableSize`, `toModifiedJulianDay`, `JSONMergePatch`, `stringToH3`, and the H3 index functions throwing an exception on entirely valid `LowCardinality` data. [#117359](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117359) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed the exception `Cannot convert out of range floating point value to integer type` when a logical function (`and`, `or`, `xor`) receives a constant floating point argument outside `{0, 1}`, such as `SELECT 1 OR 2.0`.
