[#117965](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117965) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `timeSeriesRange` and `timeSeriesFromGrid` now throw `ARGUMENT_OUT_OF_BOUND` when the requested number of points would exceed `function_range_max_elements_in_block`, matching the behavior of `range`. Previously an unbounded request would run for tens of seconds and allocate hundreds of gigabytes before hitting the memory limit. [#121056](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121056) ([Ilya Yatsishin](https://github.com/qoega)).
* `bitShiftLeft`, `bitShiftRight`, `bitRotateLeft`, and `bitRotateRight` no longer answer, once the expression is compiled, for the arguments their interpreted implementation rejects: a big-integer or negative shift amount. [#118716](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118716) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `mapPopulateSeries` keeping the wrong value when duplicate keys are present. [#119832](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119832) ([Minh Vu](https://github.com/fallintoplace)).
* Fix `format` handling of escaped braces around replacement fields. [#120704](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120704) ([Minh Vu](https://github.com/fallintoplace)).
* `jaroSimilarity` and `jaroWinklerSimilarity` now return `0` when one input is empty. [#119710](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119710) ([Minh Vu](https://github.com/fallintoplace)).
* Fixed `plus` and `minus` over an array of dates and an array of tuples holding `NULL`, for example `toString([toDateTime64(1, 3)] + [(2, NULL)])`, and the exception `ColumnTuple cannot have ColumnConst as its element` when adding a constant interval to a non-constant interval of another unit, for example `toIntervalDay(number) + INTERVAL 1 HOUR`. [#122451](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122451) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `timeSeriesCopyTags` and `timeSeriesCopyTag` when both group arguments are constant. They returned a single row regardless of the number of input rows, so such a call over several rows failed with `Invalid number of rows in Chunk` (`LOGICAL_ERROR`), and a single-row query printed an extra row in `clickhouse-client`. [#122990](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122990) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `if` and `CASE` over tuples failing with `ColumnTuple cannot have ColumnConst as its element` when an element is `NULL` in both branches, for example `if(number = 0, (1, NULL), (number, NULL))`. [#123268](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123268) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `geometryIntersectCartesian` and `geometryIntersectSpherical` returning an arbitrary result (usually `1`) for an empty `LineString`, or a `MultiLineString` with an empty element, against a `Ring`, `Polygon`, or `MultiPolygon`. For such a `MultiLineString`, `polygonsDistanceCartesian` and `polygonsDistanceSpherical` could return `0`. [#124101](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124101) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an infinite, uncancellable loop in functions `hop`, `hopStart` and `hopEnd` when subtracting the hop interval does not move the window end, for example `hopEnd(toDateTime(0, 'UTC'), toIntervalQuarter(413844), toIntervalQuarter(413844))`, where the date saturates at the calendar limit. Such arguments now throw the `Time overflow` error. [#124570](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124570) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the result type of `randConstant`, `rand`, `rand32`, `rand64`, `randCanonical`, `generateUUIDv4`, `generateUUIDv7` and `generateULID` when their optional ignored argument has type `Nothing`: they declared `Nothing` rather than their documented type. `randConstant` with more than one argument also raised `LOGICAL_ERROR` during query analysis instead of reporting the wrong argument count. [#116072](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116072) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `KILL QUERY` and `max_execution_time` (including `timeout_overflow_mode = 'break'`) now stop AI functions in the middle of a block, instead of only after all requests for the block have finished. [#125698](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125698) ([George Larionov](https://github.com/george-larionov)).
* Fixed the exception `Provided time zone must be non-empty` when the time zone argument of `formatDateTime`, `formatDateTimeInJodaSyntax`, `fromUnixTimestamp`, or `fromUnixTimestampInJodaSyntax` is a `LowCardinality` column. [#117006](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117006) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `toWeek` and `toYearWeek` with mode `8` returning week `135` for `Date32` and `DateTime64` values in the year 1900 and earlier. The wrong values also made the primary key analysis drop matching rows for predicates on `toYearWeek(key, 8)`. [#117694](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117694) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* An out-of-range integer constant in an `IN` set for a `DateTime64` or `Time64` column no longer wraps to a negative timestamp and matches an unrelated row. [#118656](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118656) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `makeDate` and `makeDate32` with two arguments to accept day 366 in leap years. [#119363](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119363) ([Minh Vu](https://github.com/fallintoplace)).
* Fixed `CAST` of an empty container to a container of `Variant` or `Dynamic`, which was rejected with `Cannot convert type Nothing to Variant(...)` or `Variant type should have at least one nested type`. This also fixes a query returning an empty `Array(Variant(...))` failing once part of it runs on a secondary server, where the folded constant is re-rendered as a `_CAST` of an empty container. [#118038](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118038) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `TYPE_MISMATCH` (`CAST AS Array can only be performed between same-dimensional array types`) when casting an `Array(Map(...))` to an array whose element type contains the `Map` inside a `Variant`, or to `Array(Dynamic)`, `Array(JSON)`, or `Array(String)`. [#120948](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120948) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `arraySlice` and `arrayElement` on a `QBit` column replicated by `ARRAY JOIN` or a `JOIN`: debug builds raised `LOGICAL_ERROR`, while release builds could return wrong results. [#120224](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120224) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a function over a `Dynamic` column reporting `LOGICAL_ERROR` when an unrelated exception, such as `MEMORY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`, was raised while converting the nested result to the declared result type. Such exceptions now keep their own error code, message, and stack instead of being reported as an internal error. [#121280](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121280) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `JSON` type and the `JSON*` functions now also accept `String` values holding bytes that are not valid UTF-8 on builds where simdjson uses its scalar `fallback` backend (`s390x`, `riscv64` and `loongarch64` without RVV / LSX, `amd64compat` on CPUs without SSE4.2 or PCLMULQDQ), which previously failed with `INCORRECT_DATA`. [#123945](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123945) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed an exception when a function applied to a `Dynamic` column returns a result of the `Nothing` type, for example `arrayElement` on an empty array literal. Such queries now return `NULL` for every row instead of failing with `std::out_of_range` or `Variant type should have at least one nested type`. [#115982](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115982) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `CAST AS QBit can only be performed from String, Array or another QBit` (and `Unsupported types for IN`) errors when a query with a `QBit` constant, such as `WHERE q = CAST([1, 2], 'QBit(Float32, 2)')` or `q IN (...)`, runs over `remote`, a `Distributed` table, or parallel replicas. [#125038](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125038) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `LOGICAL_ERROR: Column identifier ... is already registered` when `analyzer_compatibility_join_using_top_level_identifier = 1` and a `JOIN ... USING` key resolves to an `ALIAS` column of a table function such as `merge` or `remote`. `NATURAL JOIN` now also matches `ALIAS` columns of a table function, as it already did for a table, and no longer takes a non-selectable `EPHEMERAL` column as a join key on either side; with no other shared name it behaves as a `CROSS JOIN` rather than failing with `Code: 16`. [#118734](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118734) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* With `analyzer_compatibility_join_using_top_level_identifier = 1`, a `JOIN USING` identifier of the outermost `JOIN` is now also resolved from an alias defined in the `WITH` clause or in another clause of the query, not only from the `SELECT` list, and duplicated aliases with identical definitions no longer prevent the resolution; an inner `JOIN` of a query with several joins resolves it from its left table, as the old analyzer did. This fixes `UNKNOWN_IDENTIFIER` for such queries and a `USING clause expected identifier` error when an inner `USING` join is placed below an aliased outermost `USING` key. Closes [#121401](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121401). [#121975](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121975) ([Vladimir Cherkasov](https://github.com/vdimir)). [#123103](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123103) ([Vladimir Cherkasov](https://github.com/vdimir)).
* Fixed a null dereference while formatting the `LOGICAL_ERROR` for a `USING` column with no expression; the query now reports the column name instead. Closes [#123292](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123292). [#123296](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123296) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix the AST predicate push-down into distributed subqueries treating non-deterministic `EXECUTABLE` and `WASM` user-defined functions as deterministic. It pushed a predicate over such a function's result to the shard, which evaluated the function again. [#119865](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119865) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a `LOGICAL_ERROR` (`Initiator received more initial requests than there are replicas`, or `Duplicate announcement received for replica number N`) when a `mergeTreeAnalyzeIndexes` predicate contained a correlated subquery and parallel replicas were enabled. [#119860](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119860) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Parallel replicas are kept enabled for cluster engines when `automatic_parallel_replicas_mode` is set. Also, from now on, with `enable_parallel_replicas = 2` parallel replicas are forced and the automatic mode is not applied. [#122306](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122306) ([Nikita Taranov](https://github.com/nickitat)).
* Fixed `NO_SUCH_DATA_PART` in a `make_distributed_plan` bucketed read when a worker's own index analysis prunes a part that the coordinator selected, for example with `use_index_for_in_with_subqueries = 0` and an `IN (subquery)` predicate. Such parts are now skipped instead of producing a false "part ... selected by the coordinator is not available on this replica" error. Added `DistributedPlanWorkerPartsReceived`, `DistributedPlanWorkerPartsScanned`, and `DistributedPlanWorkerPartsPruned` profile events. [#115514](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115514) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A serialized query plan carrying `max_block_size = 0` is now rejected when it is read, instead of constructing steps with a zero block size that could make the receiving server hang. [#120606](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120606) ([Nihal Z. Miaji](https://github.com/nihalzp)).
* Fixed a case where `make_distributed_plan` fell back to local execution for the outer query but still built inner `IN` subquery sets as distributed plans inside views, scalar subqueries, `GLOBAL IN` subqueries, `Distributed` shard plans, materialized CTEs, and `TimeSeries` reads. [#119986](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119986) ([Antonio Filipovic](https://github.com/antoniofilipovic)).
* `make_distributed_plan` queries that resolve dictionary functions, `joinGet`, or `MergeTree` column defaults only on the initiator now fall back to local execution, avoiding worker-side `Dictionary (...) not found` failures and similar unresolved-reference errors. [#121198](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121198) ([Antonio Filipovic](https://github.com/antoniofilipovic)). [#121567](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121567) ([Antonio Filipovic](https://github.com/antoniofilipovic)).
* A cancelled or failed distributed query with `make_distributed_plan = 1` no longer waits for the handshake timeout of a streaming exchange whose producer does not answer. Closes [#122395](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/122395). [#122422](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122422) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed distributed query plans losing a `LIMIT` early-stop signal on an in-memory exchange when a port closed concurrently. Such queries could keep reading whole inputs or wait for exchange streams after the `LIMIT` was already satisfied. [#122453](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122453) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Reading from materialized CTE '...' before its materialization completed - DelayedPortsProcessor gate is missing in the query plan` on a table read with more than one stream, raised by a lightweight `UPDATE` or `DELETE` whose predicate passes an `IN` subquery that defines a `MATERIALIZED` CTE to a function such as `ignore`, and by a lightweight `UPDATE`/`DELETE` or a `SELECT` when an `IN` or other set subquery defines a `MATERIALIZED` CTE that two other `MATERIALIZED` CTEs of that subquery read. [#119715](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119715) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)). [#121010](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121010) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when a predicate on a `Merge` table's `_table` or `_database` virtual column is wrapped in `indexHint`. [#117612](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117612) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix ``Column `__array_join_exp_1` not found in table`` for `ARRAY JOIN` with a filter on the array-joined column over a `Merge` table whose child is a `Distributed` table. [#119307](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119307) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Unexpected return type` when reading a `Merge` table whose underlying table is another `Merge` table with different column types, with `optimize_prewhere_after_pushdown = 1` and `move_all_conditions_to_prewhere = 0`. [#124037](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124037) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `TRUNCATE TABLE` failing with `DEADLOCK_AVOIDED`, leaving the data in place, while the table is being read concurrently, for a `MergeTree` table in a database with `lazy_load_tables = 1` and for an `Alias` table whose target is a `MergeTree` table. [#119105](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119105) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#121703](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121703) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#113634](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113634) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `TRUNCATE ALL TABLES FROM` and `TRUNCATE TABLES FROM ... LIKE ...` now skip `Alias` tables, which hold no data of their own. Previously such a statement could fail with `LOGICAL_ERROR`, truncate a target in another database, or fail when the alias target was missing. [#121134](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121134) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, `system.tables.create_table_query`, and metadata files of `Alias` tables including a column list, which is invalid for `ENGINE = Alias` and goes stale when the target table changes. [#115141](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115141) ([Enric Calabuig](https://github.com/eclbg)).
* Fixed a logical error when a non-`APPEND` refreshable materialized view was created in a non-`Atomic`/`Replicated` database via a full-definition `ATTACH`, or via a `RESTORE` of a view that had a UUID into such a database. `CREATE` already rejected this combination; `ATTACH` and `RESTORE` bypassed the check and produced a broken view with no UUID. Backups of views that never had a UUID stay restorable, and refresh now fails cleanly instead of aborting in debug and sanitizer builds. [#111928](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/111928) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SELECT count()` never terminating, or returning an invented row count, on an `Avro` file with a corrupted block header. The bundled `ClickHouse/avro` now rejects a block row count larger than its payload can hold wherever it can derive a lower bound for the schema, so `SELECT count()` over `snappy`-compressed `Avro` files still uses the block header row count instead of decoding every row. [#116156](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116156) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#121211](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121211) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `rows_read` and `rows_before_limit_at_least` in the statistics of the `JSON` and `XML` output formats being reported as 0 or too small when a query with `LIMIT` reads from remote servers (parallel replicas or the `remote` table function), because the output format could be finalized before the remaining progress was drained from the connections. Also fix a query broken off by `timeout_overflow_mode = 'break'` answering with a truncated response - or with no response body at all - instead of a complete document with the statistics, and, over the native protocol, dropping the rows that had already been produced when the time limit was checked. [#118000](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118000) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `CANNOT_SEEK_THROUGH_FILE` when reading a subset of columns from an `ArrowStream` through a pipe, for example with `clickhouse local` reading stdin. [#125689](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125689) ([Mishalski](https://github.com/wywywywycloud)).
* Fixed `FileLog` on macOS re-reading a file from offset `0` when it is renamed during a directory rescan. [#122448](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122448) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `FileLog` silently skipping the first rows of a file moved into the watched directory from elsewhere (for example a staging file renamed into place) when that file got the inode of a file just deleted from the directory. Such a file is now read from the beginning. [#123593](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123593) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A `FileLog` file that cannot be opened (removed, a symlink whose target was removed, not readable by the server) no longer stops the table: the other files keep being consumed, and it is skipped, logged once and retried until it can be opened while the table is loaded, unless the directory reports that it was removed, renamed or replaced. With the default `handle_error_mode`, a record that cannot be parsed is now logged and skipped while the table streams into materialized views, instead of failing the whole insert together with the rows read from the other files; a direct `SELECT` still throws. [#123595](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123595) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the warning about a setting duplicated in the definition of an `S3Queue` or `AzureQueue` table, which said that the first value is used while the table uses the last one. [#124916](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124916) ([Vadim Lopatin](https://github.com/buggins)).
* Fix the `KafkaConsumersWithAssignment` metric drifting negative: it was decremented twice for every consumer-group rebalance. [#113919](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113919) ([kalavt](https://github.com/kalavt)).
* Fixed the histogram metric `keeper_read_wait_for_write_time_milliseconds` and the OpenTelemetry span `keeper.read.wait_for_write` always reading zero and never being emitted when `use_new_dispatcher` is enabled, which is the default. [#115252](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115252) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* `TestKeeper` no longer treats nodes that only share a name prefix as descendants: `removeRecursive` of `/q` keeps `/q2`, and `listRecursive` and `listWithOptions` of `/q` return its children when a sibling such as `/q-x` exists. [#122769](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122769) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed Keeper `ProfileEvents` `KeeperPacketsSent`, `KeeperPacketsReceived`, `KeeperRequestTotal`, `KeeperRequestTotalWithSubrequests`, and `KeeperLatency` being counted twice. After upgrading, these counters grow at half the previous rate. [#122811](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122811) ([myeongjun](https://github.com/myeongjjun)).
* Fix unbounded recursion when decoding a deeply nested value inside a binary-encoded type, for example an `AggregateFunction` parameter in a `Dynamic` value read from `RowBinary` or `Native`. [#122667](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122667) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Fixed `SHOW COLUMNS` failing with `Cannot parse string 'Decimal' as Int8` (`CANNOT_PARSE_TEXT`) for `Nullable(Decimal(P, S))` columns when the connection is made through the MySQL wire protocol. [#75694](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/75694) ([ultrabig](https://github.com/ultrabig)).
* Fix a logical error `Cannot write to canceled buffer` in the PostgreSQL wire protocol handler when a client disconnects while the server is still sending the result of a query. [#118369](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118369) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed a signed overflow in the workload throttler that made `system.scheduler.throttling_us` report a negative value for a workload whose `max_bytes_per_second`, `max_queries_per_second`, or `max_cpus` is small enough to postpone a request for longer than a year. Such a postponement is now saturated at one year. [#113697](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113697) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the `throughput` column of `system.scheduler` reporting a small fraction of the real value (about 0.05x initially and 0.3x after 4 seconds) for about 20 seconds after a scheduling node is created, for example right after server start or `CREATE WORKLOAD`. The client's CPU usage indicator, which uses the same `EventRateMeter`, was affected the same way on its first samples. Closes [#121962](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121962). [#122006](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122006) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a logical error `Cannot add step Limit to QueryPlan because it has incompatible header with root step Sorting` when a vector search query with `vector_search_with_rescoring = 0` was executed with the experimental `make_distributed_plan` setting. [#114457](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/114457) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fix `assignCentroid(vec, 'dict')` with an unqualified dictionary name over a `Distributed` table, `cluster`/`remote`, or parallel replicas. Remote shards and replicas resolved the name in their own `default` database, so the query failed with `Dictionary (dict) not found` or silently used a dictionary with the same name from `default`. The name is now resolved in the current database of the initiator, as for `dictGet`. [#123711](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123711) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `UNDROP TABLE` failing with `LOGICAL_ERROR` (`Failed to get metadata path from database`) instead of `UNKNOWN_TABLE` when the target database engine stores no table metadata on disk, for example a `Memory` database that reused the name of a dropped `Atomic` one, or the default database of `clickhouse-local`. [#120945](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120945) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY SETTING name = DEFAULT` skipping the checks an engine makes on a setting reset. [#119475](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119475) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed `TRUNCATE ALL TABLES FROM <db>` failing with `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` for the whole database when it held a table whose engine stores no rows of its own, such as `Merge`, `Null`, `GenerateRandom`, `Executable`, `ExecutablePool`, `FuzzQuery`, `FuzzJSON`, or `URL`. Such tables are now skipped, as views and dictionaries already were. [#121248](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121248) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A table created without a column list over a source that carries a per-column `TTL` no longer inherits that `TTL` when its own engine does not support one, such as `Merge`, `Buffer`, `Distributed`, or `Remote`. [#121335](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121335) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the logical error `Cannot find column <name> in ColumnsDescription` when a single `ALTER` statement on a materialized view applies `COMMENT COLUMN` or `MODIFY COLUMN ... COMMENT` after a `MODIFY QUERY` whose new query no longer produces that column. Such a statement now reports the missing column instead of aborting in debug and sanitizer builds. [#121469](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121469) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Cannot capture column N because it has incompatible type` when a materialized view with a lambda over an `Array(Tuple(...))` column receives a block while the source table's column type is being changed by `ALTER TABLE ... MODIFY COLUMN`. [#122345](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122345) ([Jimmy Aguilar Mena](https://github.com/Ergus)). [#123514](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123514) ([Jimmy Aguilar Mena](https://github.com/Ergus)). [#124740](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124740) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* In a `Memory` database, the name of a detached table, view or dictionary is now reserved, as in `Atomic` databases. A statement that would create a new object under it (`CREATE`, `CREATE OR REPLACE [MATERIALIZED] VIEW`, or `ATTACH` with a full definition) fails with `TABLE_ALREADY_EXISTS`, `IF NOT EXISTS` makes it a no-op, and a short `ATTACH` restores the detached object. Previously these statements were accepted, but `SHOW CREATE TABLE` and the table dependencies kept describing the detached object, and a later `ALTER` of such a view failed with a `LOGICAL_ERROR` exception. [#122670](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122670) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a window definition that places a frame before the parent window name, as in `WINDOW x AS (ROWS UNBOUNDED PRECEDING w)`. That order is now a syntax error, while a parent window named after a frame keyword, as in `WINDOW x AS (rows)`, now parses. [#120156](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120156) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Formatting `EXPLAIN AST (<query>) FORMAT ...` or `... SETTINGS ...` now keeps the parentheses for `INSERT`, `DELETE`, `CREATE`, a nested non-`SELECT` `EXPLAIN`, other non-`SELECT` queries, and a `SELECT` or `EXPLAIN` with output options of its own, and `EXPLAIN SYNTAX SYSTEM ... FORMAT ...` is no longer formatted with parentheses that do not parse, so the formatted query parses back with the same output options. [#123189](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123189) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed formatting of PostgreSQL `COPY` queries so table names, columns, direction, format, and header options are preserved. [#123601](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123601) ([Minh Vu](https://github.com/fallintoplace)).
* Keep empty `LIKE` and `ILIKE` patterns in `SHOW` statements, including `SHOW FUNCTIONS`. [#123624](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123624) ([Minh Vu](https://github.com/fallintoplace)). [#123626](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123626) ([Minh Vu](https://github.com/fallintoplace)).
* A one-argument `tuple` in the `ORDER BY` of a projection, such as `ORDER BY tuple(c0)`, is now kept when the definition is formatted, so the formatted query parses back to the same definition. [#123162](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123162) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed formatting of the tuple element access operator when its index carries an alias. `SELECT ((1,2) AS t).(1 AS a)` was formatted as `SELECT ((1, 2) AS t).(1) AS a`, which re-parses with the alias attached to the whole `tupleElement` instead of to the index. This changed the column names of a query passed through `formatQuery` or sent to a remote shard, and raised `Inconsistent AST formatting` on builds with assertions enabled. [#120217](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120217) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `formatQuery` and `formatQueryFromJSON` dropping `FULL` from `SHOW FULL DATABASES` and the other supported `SHOW FULL` forms. `SHOW FULL DICTIONARIES` no longer selects a non-existent `engine` column, and builds with assertions enabled no longer fail with `Inconsistent AST formatting`. Closes [#123967](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123967). [#124036](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124036) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a crash and a `LOGICAL_ERROR` when a `lambda` function reached query analysis without the `lambda(tuple(...), body)` shape the SQL parser always produces. An AST deserialized from JSON (`dialect = clickhouse_json`) can no longer mark an arbitrary function as a lambda, and `CREATE FUNCTION f AS g(tuple(x), x)` is now rejected with `BAD_ARGUMENTS` instead of failing when `f` is called. Closes [#120380](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120380). [#120483](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120483) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed server termination on `CREATE TABLE`/`ALTER TABLE` when a constraint expression restored through the `clickhouse_json` dialect held a function without an `arguments` list. Such a payload now fails with `BAD_ARGUMENTS` while the AST is deserialized, so shapes that used to be accepted and fail later at `INSERT` (for example `notEquals` with no argument list) are rejected at parse time inside that dialect. [#120507](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120507) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `formatQueryFromJSON` accepting enumerator names that name no value (an enumeration bound, or an unset default), such as `{"type":"SystemQuery","query_type":"UNKNOWN"}`, which raised `LOGICAL_ERROR: Unknown SYSTEM command` instead of reporting bad input. Such names are now rejected with `BAD_ARGUMENTS`. Closes [#120741](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120741). [#120886](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120886) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed two segfaults in `formatQueryFromJSON` when a node omits a child that formatting dereferences unconditionally. Closes [#120739](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120739). [#120876](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120876) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server crash when a `clickhouse_json` payload restores an expression holding a function with no `arguments` list, a shape the SQL parser cannot produce. Such a payload is now rejected with `BAD_ARGUMENTS`, as is an empty `TTL` list, which was persisted as metadata that no longer loaded. [#120882](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120882) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `formatQueryFromJSON` raising `LOGICAL_ERROR: Unknown element in AST: Foreign Key` for a payload of type `ForeignKeyDeclaration`. That node type has no formatter and no query can produce one, so it is now rejected with `BAD_ARGUMENTS`. Closes [#120738](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/120738). [#120915](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120915) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed stack exhaustion when deserializing an AST from JSON through `formatQueryFromJSON` or the `clickhouse_json` dialect. Such payloads now fail with `TOO_DEEP_RECURSION`. [#121693](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121693) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a silently corrupted query when a node restored from the AST JSON format (`formatQueryFromJSON`, `dialect = 'clickhouse_json'`) carried a shape the parser cannot produce, such as a bare select query argument or an expression list where an argument was expected. Such a node is now rejected with `BAD_ARGUMENTS` instead of formatting into SQL that parses back as a different query. [#120725](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120725) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed stack exhaustion in `parseQueryToJSON` when serializing a query nested deeper than the serializer could follow, reachable by raising `max_parser_depth` above its default. Such a query now fails with `TOO_DEEP_RECURSION` instead of terminating the server. [#121856](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121856) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A `CREATE DICTIONARY` sent in the AST JSON format (`dialect = 'clickhouse_json'`) is now rejected with `BAD_ARGUMENTS` when the brackets of a key-value argument do not match its value, a bracketed list holds something other than key-value pairs, the `SOURCE` has no brackets, the primary key is empty or not a list of identifiers, a `DEFAULT` is not a literal, or the attributes or the definition are missing or empty, and so is any other query that carries them. Such queries could crash the server or prevent it from starting. [#122981](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122981) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a server crash (SIGSEGV) when a `CREATE TABLE ... PROJECTION` / `ALTER TABLE ... ADD PROJECTION` statement is restored from a `clickhouse_json` AST whose projection `query` subtree contains a function node without an `arguments` list. The malformed payload is now rejected with `BAD_ARGUMENTS` during AST JSON deserialization. [#121113](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121113) ([Ilias](https://github.com/iliasabk)).
* A `clickhouse_json` payload that restores a function without an `arguments` list into an expression slot read by the pre-analyzer stack is now rejected with `BAD_ARGUMENTS` while it is being deserialized. Previously such a payload was accepted and could reach a visitor that dereferences the missing list, terminating the server. [#121119](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121119) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `parseSize` and `parseTime` logic to correctly distinguish between size thresholds and time-based rotation schedules such as `12:00`. Fixes [#103108](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/103108) and [#100910](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/100910). [#103127](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/103127) ([Jianmei Zhang](https://github.com/zhangjmruc)).
* A configuration file can be written in XML or in YAML, but only `config.xml` was looked up as the default configuration file. Now `clickhouse-server`, `clickhouse-keeper`, `clickhouse-local`, `clickhouse-keeper-client` and `clickhouse-disks` also find `config.yaml` and `config.yml` (`keeper_config.yaml` and `keeper_config.yml` for Keeper). Previously, `clickhouse-server` silently started with the configuration embedded into the binary when only a `config.yaml` was present. [#118422](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118422) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* `clickhouse install` (and therefore `apt`/`dpkg` package upgrades) no longer prompts interactively to set up a password for the `default` user when that user has been explicitly removed from configuration via `<default remove="1" />` in `users.d`. Previously the installer only checked whether the `default` user already had a password configured, missing the case where the user itself does not exist in the merged configuration at all, so it kept asking for a password on every upgrade even though there was no user to set it for. [#110438](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/110438) ([Eduardo Gómez](https://github.com/edugomz)).
* Fixed the per-query `MemoryAllocatedWithoutCheck` and `MemoryAllocatedWithoutCheckBytes` values in `system.query_log`. A thread's untracked memory balance is flushed when the thread is attached to a query, and that flush was reported as the query's, so memory allocated before the query existed was charged to it. [#116505](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116505) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `AsynchronousInsertQueueSize` and `AsynchronousInsertQueueBytes` in `system.metrics` growing permanently after `SYSTEM FLUSH ASYNC INSERT QUEUE`. [#120215](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120215) ([Sema Checherinda](https://github.com/CheSema)).
* Fixed a trailing NUL byte in the `notation` column of `system.unicode`, which made `length` return a wrong value and comparisons such as `notation = 'U+0041'` never match. [#122119](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122119) ([David Meng](https://github.com/davidmenggx)).
* Fixed `CGroup*` asynchronous metrics being silently missing on cgroup v2 systems that run ClickHouse in a nested cgroup, such as a typical `systemd` service. Closes [#103968](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/103968). [#122918](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122918) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed `NO_COMMON_TYPE` for `use_variant_as_common_type = 0` in the jemalloc fragmentation dashboard. [#121621](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121621) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Fixed `TYPE_MISMATCH` being counted in `system.errors` on every execution of a `SELECT` comparing a `DateTime64` column against a `DateTime`, `Date` or `Date32` constant inside `AND`. A date or time constant with a part the column type cannot hold is no longer pushed down to MongoDB as a truncated bound. [#121078](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121078) ([Navneet Kumar](https://github.com/navneet83)).
* Fixed reading `system.parts`, `system.parts_columns` and `system.projection_parts` for a table whose `PARTITION BY` contains a `modulo` with an unsigned left and a signed right operand: the query aborted with `Bad get: has Int128, requested UInt128 (BAD_GET)` when the partition value was wider than 8 bytes, and otherwise showed a reinterpreted value that `ALTER TABLE ... DROP PARTITION` rejects. The same value is rendered while a merge or mutation is scheduled, while a `system.part_log` row is written, and while a partition is pruned, so on such a table merges failed to start, `part_log` rows were lost, and a filtered read could abort during planning. [#119333](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119333) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `system.server_settings` reporting the async logger queue size under the misspelled key `logger.async_queye_max_size` instead of `logger.async_queue_max_size`. [#123578](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123578) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fix `SELECT` from `system.stack_trace` failing with `Cannot read from file /proc/<tid>/status ... No such process` when a thread exits while the table is being scanned. [#123805](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123805) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `system.jemalloc_sampled_allocations` and the collapsed output of `system.jemalloc_profile_text` on sampled allocations whose backtrace jemalloc could not unwind: the allocation is now reported with `trace = []` or under `[unknown]` instead of failing with `CANNOT_PARSE_TEXT` or being omitted. [#123818](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123818) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix per-protocol asynchronous metrics such as `TCPThreads` and `HTTPThreads` not being reported for listeners configured through the `<protocols>` section. Such a listener is now reported under the same metric as the equivalent built-in port. Metrics are also now reported for the `tcp_with_proxy` and `tcp_ssh` listeners, which previously had none. [#112977](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/112977) ([Chester](https://github.com/superdiaodiao)).
* Fix a deadlock when an obsolete union system log table is replaced with synchronous drops. [#124610](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124610) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Fixed the `max_local_read_bandwidth_for_server`, `max_local_write_bandwidth_for_server`, `max_remote_read_network_bandwidth_for_server` and `max_remote_write_network_bandwidth_for_server` rows of `system.server_settings` reporting the reading session's own bandwidth limit instead of the server-wide one. `getServerSetting` on those names is fixed the same way. [#119556](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119556) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix tables of the `clickhouse-local` default database becoming invisible (`UNKNOWN_TABLE`) when the same `--path` is later used with another `default_database`. [#123124](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123124) ([Nikolay Degterinsky](https://github.com/evillique)).
* Fixed missing crash reports of `clickhouse-local` when neither `stdin` nor `stderr` is a terminal: a fatal signal printed nothing, and `--client_logs_file` was not written. [#115102](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115102) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `clickhouse-local` hanging forever on `DROP TABLE|VIEW|DATABASE ... SYNC` when `--path` is set and no metadata is loaded from it, for example a new directory on released versions or `--only-system-tables`. [#123862](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123862) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The wasm64 `clickhouse local` now starts with 2 GiB of linear memory, so it no longer fails at startup with `memory access out of bounds` when Node.js races a `memory.grow` across worker threads. Closes [#124067](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124067). [#123944](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123944) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SET profile` having no effect on subsequent queries in `clickhouse-local`. Profile settings such as `readonly` and `join_use_nulls` now apply to subsequent queries again. Closes [#123916](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/123916). [#125171](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125171) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `promql_evaluation_time` silently rounding the requested instant, which could make an instant query return a different sample. The setting also now accepts an integer Unix timestamp over the native protocol, which it previously refused. [#120942](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120942) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* PromQL queries with regex label matchers (`=~`, `!~`) again work under `--dialect promql`. [#115073](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115073) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* Allow PromQL `# ` and `#!` line comments to end at EOF without a trailing newline. [#113201](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113201) ([Minh Vu](https://github.com/fallintoplace)).
* Fix PromQL duplicate-series detection to check one step at a time, so series that share tags but have values at different steps are now merged instead of failing with `Multiple series have the same tags`. Binary operators check for duplicates after matching series, so unmatched series no longer cause this exception. [#124086](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124086) ([Vitaly Baranov](https://github.com/vitlibar)). [#124879](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124879) ([Vitaly Baranov](https://github.com/vitlibar)).
* PromQL: fixed expressions over an instant vector that is known to be empty, such as `(0 < clamp(up, 1, -1)) or vector(7)`, which failed in range queries with `Number of values doesn't match number of steps` or returned samples at `1970-01-01`. [#123879](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123879) ([Valery Petrov](https://github.com/valerypetrov)).
* Fixed `SELECT count()` returning the whole-table row count for a streaming read (`FROM ... STREAM`), ignoring the `CURSOR` bound. The implicit min-max/count projection answered the query from part metadata instead of the streaming reader. [#117188](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/117188) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a server crash (`std::terminate` with `write failed`) in the embedded SSH client when the client's terminal was closed before the line reader was destroyed. [#120764](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120764) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `Code: 1001 STD_EXCEPTION` (`filesystem error: ... File name too long`) when a query names a table or dictionary longer than the filesystem's `NAME_MAX` in `clickhouse local`, or in a server holding a `Filesystem` or `URL` database. Such a name is now reported as missing rather than as a filesystem error. Closes [#121304](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/121304). [#121471](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121471) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* A malformed argument list of an optimization of the `mergeTreeAnalyzeIndexes` table function is now reported as `BAD_ARGUMENTS` instead of a logical error or a raw `std::exception`. [#118644](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118644) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Fixed `max_execution_time` not being enforced in `clickhouse-local` for a query blocked inside a non-interruptible read. `clickhouse-local` did not start the watchdog that turns a time limit expiry into a query cancellation, so a query reading from an unresponsive `s3` endpoint ran to the end of the S3 retry budget (`s3_retry_attempts` defaults to 500, which takes minutes) instead of failing with `TIMEOUT_EXCEEDED` at its limit. [#120933](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120933) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed distributed queries hanging at 100% CPU on macOS when `max_threads = 1`. [#122905](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122905) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed a server abort on macOS when an HTTP client resets the connection during header reading. [#123074](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123074) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a `LOGICAL_ERROR` (`Unexpected return type from materialize`, a server abort in debug and sanitizer builds) when reading a `Buffer` table whose destination declares a column with a different but convertible type and a forwarded filter references that column: with a row policy and the implicit `WHERE` to `PREWHERE` move, and under `query_plan_optimize_lazy_final`. The same cause made such a read fail with `NOT_FOUND_COLUMN_IN_BLOCK` when a text index on that column was read directly and the predicate appeared in both `PREWHERE` and `WHERE`, fail with `TYPE_MISMATCH` over a `URL(Parquet)` destination, abort with `Bad cast from ColumnMap to ColumnArray` when a row policy and an explicit `PREWHERE` both referenced it, or fail with `AMBIGUOUS_COLUMN_NAME` when several `PREWHERE` conditions referenced the converted column. [#113900](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/113900) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)). [#124632](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124632) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `SYSTEM DROP DNS CACHE` leaving entries in the DNS cache when it ran at the same time as the periodic DNS cache update. [#123699](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123699) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix `UNSUPPORTED_METHOD` error for queries using `IN`/`NOT IN`/`GLOBAL IN` with a subquery inside `AND`/`OR` when `enable_function_early_short_circuit` is enabled. [#116037](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/116037) ([fhw12345](https://github.com/fhw12345)).
* Fixed a crash caused by a stack overflow when a deeply nested JSON document was parsed into a `JSON` column or passed to the `JSONExtract` family of functions. Such documents are now rejected with the `TOO_DEEP_RECURSION` error. [#120887](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120887) ([Pavel Kruglov](https://github.com/Avogar)).
* Fixed the `LOGICAL_ERROR` `Not-ready Set is passed as the second argument for function 'in'` for a query with `ORDER BY ... WITH FILL ... INTERPOLATE` whose `INTERPOLATE` expression contains `IN` with a subquery or a table that is not used anywhere else in the query. [#124518](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124518) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of a `WHERE` equality between `Tuple` values with `Nullable` elements, or between `Variant` values, over a comma, `CROSS` or `INNER` join: the pair whose equality is `NULL`, such as `tuple(NULL) = tuple(NULL)`, was returned. With `allow_dynamic_type_in_join_keys = 0`, a comma or `CROSS` join with a `WHERE` equality on a `Dynamic` value no longer returns no rows or fails with `ILLEGAL_COLUMN`. [#124854](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124854) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed secret arguments being exposed in `system.query_log` and the server log when a table function or function was called with the wrong letter case, such as `S3` instead of `s3`, `MySQL` instead of `mysql`, or `ENCRYPT` instead of `encrypt`. Such calls are still rejected. [#124862](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124862) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed tables of a `MySQL` database ignoring the database's `connection_auto_close` setting: with `connection_auto_close = 0`, every read still closed its connection and the next read reconnected. A table attached again with `ATTACH TABLE` also lost the database's settings. `INSERT` into any `MySQL` table ignored the setting and always kept its connection; with the default `connection_auto_close = 1` it now closes it after the query, as reads do. Closes [#124749](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/124749). [#124884](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124884) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Hide the password of a URL in `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, `system.tables` and `system.query_log` only within the URL's authority: a `@` in the query string no longer rewrites the shown host, and a password containing `@` is hidden whole. [#124885](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124885) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Fixed `merge` and `Merge` tables over a `Distributed` or `remote` table rejecting valid queries on a column that some of the tables lack: aggregates over `Decimal` and `Map` columns failed with errors such as `THERE_IS_NO_COLUMN`, and an integer column of this kind in `GROUP BY`, `ORDER BY` or `LIMIT BY` failed with `Positional argument number 0 is out of bounds`. [#124976](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124976) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed trace samples in `system.trace_log` losing their `query_id` while a thread attaches to or detaches from a query, so memory profiling samples remain attributable to the query. [#125176](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125176) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong window function results in the totals row of a `WITH TOTALS` query when an `arrayJoin` expands the totals into several rows: those rows were not sorted by the window's `PARTITION BY` / `ORDER BY` keys. [#125324](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125324) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed primary key and skip index analysis that could skip or over-count rows for conditions on date and time functions such as `toHour`, `toDayOfMonth`, `timezoneOffset` or `toTimeWithFixedDate` over a `Nullable(DateTime)` or `LowCardinality(Nullable(DateTime))` key whose time zone differs from the session time zone. [#125327](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125327) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed wrong results of `ORDER BY`, `DISTINCT`, `LIMIT BY` and aggregation in order over a `Merge` table or the `merge` table function when it reads an `Iceberg` table with a sort order: the rows came back unsorted or duplicated. [#125383](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125383) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed `count()` with a `WHERE` condition on the sorting key returning too many rows on a `MergeTree` table with a normal projection: rows counted from the primary key by the exact count optimization were counted again when the projection was read. Closes [#125183](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125183). [#125412](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125412) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed a possible segmentation fault when reading an `Avro` `map` into a `Map` column (for example `Map(DateTime, String)`) with the `AvroConfluent` format, including `Kafka` tables. Closes [#125457](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125457). [#125505](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125505) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a server crash on `CREATE DICTIONARY` without a `SOURCE` clause when `restore_replace_external_dictionary_source_to_null` is enabled. Such a query now fails with `INCORRECT_DICTIONARY_DEFINITION`, as it does with the setting disabled. With the same setting, a local `CLICKHOUSE` source that uses a constant function such as `DB currentDatabase()` is no longer rejected. [#125659](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125659) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fixed the `Arrow` and `ArrowStream` input formats rejecting valid dictionary-encoded columns whose dictionary contains repeated values. Closes [#125139](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/125139). [#125677](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125677) ([Jay Pokale](https://github.com/JayPokale)).
* Fixed the Arrow Flight interface writing the text of each query, including secrets such as `IDENTIFIED BY` passwords and storage keys, to the server log and `system.text_log`. The gRPC interface did the same for the first 64 characters of each query. Now queries from these interfaces are logged only with their secrets hidden. [#125716](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125716) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Fix a server crash on macOS where an idle thread pool worker aborted with `condition_variable wait failed: Invalid argument`. The `EINVAL` that Darwin's `pthread_cond_wait` returns when the kernel fails the wait is now treated as a spurious wakeup. Closes [#84761](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/84761). [#125721](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125721) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).

#### Build/Testing/Packaging Improvement

* Harden official builds by compiling with `-fstack-protector-strong` and `-fstack-clash-protection`, and by linking with `-Wl,-z,separate-code`. [#119039](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119039) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)). [#123073](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123073) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* The musl build of ClickHouse is now a static position-independent executable, so it gets address space layout randomization like the glibc build. [#125669](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125669) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)).
* Added an optional packaging mode `SYMBOL_SECTIONS=gnu_debugdata` that replaces `.symtab` and `.strtab` in release binaries with compressed `.gnu_debugdata` (MiniDebugInfo), reducing symbol data from about 148 MiB to about 10 MiB in the official build. Stack traces and symbol access from gdb, lldb, elfutils, and perf remain available. [#115322](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/115322) ([Andrew Grigorev](https://github.com/ei-grad)).
* Update `cctz` to 2026d. Time zone names with the `file:` prefix are now rejected. [#121325](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121325) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)). Update `cctz` to 2026e: `America/Winnipeg`, `America/Rainy_River`, and `Canada/Central` now stay at UTC−5 from 2026-11-01, reflecting Manitoba's removal of the DST fall-back. [#124249](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124249) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `OpenSSL` to 3.5.9. [#124278](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124278) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `aws-sdk-cpp` to 1.11.907, the AWS CRT libraries to 0.43.8, and `aws-c-common` to 1.0.2. [#125080](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125080) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* The `delta-kernel-rs` library no longer carries its own copy of OpenSSL, so the binary always links the OpenSSL built from `contrib/openssl` with the current build flags instead of an older copy kept by the Rust build cache. [#123889](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123889) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Rust crates are now built in parallel with each other and with the C++ code, which reduces the build time. [#124645](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124645) ([Antonio Andelic](https://github.com/antonio2368)).
* Builds of the experimental GPU engine are faster: `nvcc` compilations are now cached with `ccache`, and unused PTX plus CUDA line info are no longer generated. [#120421](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120421) ([Konstantin Vedernikov](https://github.com/scanhex12)).
* Fixed building with `-DNO_ARMV81_OR_HIGHER=1` on AArch64: the compatibility profile no longer disables `NEON` for Rust, so `ring`-based components (`chdig`, `delta-kernel-rs`) compile and stay enabled. This unblocks building for ARMv8.2-A CPUs that lack `rcpc`, such as HiSilicon Kunpeng 920. [#107440](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/107440) ([Tiaonmmn](https://github.com/Tiaonmmn)).
* Compile code linked into the position-independent `clickhouse` executable with `-fPIE` instead of `-fPIC`, avoiding unnecessary symbol indirection. Apply the flag to the C++ runtime libraries from `contrib` too, so builds with compilers that do not default to PIE link successfully, including Fedora's `clang` on `aarch64` and `x86_64`. [#125094](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125094) ([oleg-milanov](https://github.com/oleg-milanov)). [#125488](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125488) ([Groene AI](https://github.com/groeneai)).
* Allow building with XRay instrumentation under musl. [#118914](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118914) ([Konstantin Bogdanov](https://github.com/thevar1able)).
* Fixed a missing CMake link dependency that could make `src/Functions/array/distanceTransposed.cpp` fail with `simsimd/simsimd.h file not found` on a from-scratch build. [#120504](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/120504) ([Craig Ringer](https://github.com/ringerc)).
* Reduce linker warnings in Rust/wasm builds: remove `--icf=safe` warnings about `SHT_LLVM_ADDRSIG` in the stripped Rust libraries, and apply the `__jit_debug_register_code` workaround only to the `clickhouse` binary and only when `wasmtime` is linked. [#122904](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122904) ([Raúl Marín](https://github.com/Algunenano)). [#123287](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123287) ([Azat Khuzhin](https://github.com/azat)).
* Update `google-protobuf` to v36.1. `google-cloud-cpp` protobuf sources are now regenerated when `protoc` changes, so incremental builds do not fail after such an update. [#118926](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118926) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)). [#123603](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/123603) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
* Update `rocksdb` to v11.8.1. [#118929](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118929) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `abseil-cpp` to 20260817.0. [#118897](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118897) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `PostgreSQL` to 18.6. [#124281](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124281) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `thrift` to v0.25.0. [#124280](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124280) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `cppkafka` to v0.4.1. [#119468](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119468) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `h3` to v4.5.0. [#119328](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119328) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `flatbuffers` to v25.12.19. [#119100](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119100) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `croaring` to v5.2.2. [#119464](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119464) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `simdutf` to v9.1.1. [#119324](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119324) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `double-conversion` to v3.4.0. [#119095](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119095) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `libcpuid` to v0.8.2. [#121382](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/121382) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `liburing` to 2.15. [#118895](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118895) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `libuv` to v1.52.1. [#119099](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119099) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `libcotp` to v4.2.2. [#122000](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/122000) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `vectorscan` to 5.4.13. [#125774](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125774) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `snappy` to 1.2.2. [#119325](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119325) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `brotli` to v1.2.0. [#119096](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119096) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `libdeflate` to v1.26. [#119349](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119349) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `xxHash` to v0.8.4. [#124231](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/124231) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* Update `google-benchmark` to v1.9.5. [#118893](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/118893) ([Actuele AI](https://github.com/actueleai)).
* `MemoryWorker` now corrects `MemoryTracker` using allocated memory from ASan, TSan, and MSan instead of resident memory in sanitizer builds when cgroups are available. [#119956](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/119956) ([Konstantin Morozov](https://github.com/k-morozov)).
* Add support for cross-compiling ClickHouse for illumos. Closes [#23777](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/issues/23777). [#125073](https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickHouse/pull/125073) ([Alexey Milovidov](https://github.com/alexey-milovidov)).
